00014: async function fetchNewDrawsAfter(db, lottery, lastProcessedDrawId, limit = 25) {
00015:   if (!lastProcessedDrawId) {
00016:     return {
00017:       requiresBootstrap: true,
00018:       draws: [],
00019:     };
00020:   }
00021: 
00022:   const admin = require("firebase-admin");
00023:   const DOC_ID = admin.firestore.FieldPath.documentId();
00024: 
00025:   const snap = await db
00026:     .collection("draws")
00027:     .where("lottery_key", "==", String(lottery).toUpperCase())
00028:     .orderBy(DOC_ID, "asc")
00029:     .startAfter(String(lastProcessedDrawId))
00030:     .limit(limit)
00031:     .get();
00032: 
00033:   return {
00034:     requiresBootstrap: false,
00035:     draws: snap.docs.map((doc) => ({
00036:       id: doc.id,
00037:       ...doc.data(),
00038:     })),
00039:   };
00040: }

00042: async function updateSnapshot({
00043:   lottery = "PT_RIO",
00044:   force = false,
00045: } = {}) {
00046: 
00047:   const db = getAdminDb();
00048: 
00049:   // Bounds atuais do Firestore
00050:   const { result: bounds } = await computeBounds(db, lottery);
00051: 
00052:   // Snapshot existente
00053:   const current = await readStatisticsSnapshot({
00054:     uf: lottery,
00055:     scope: "dashboard_full",
00056:   });
00057: 
00058:   // Metadata de controle incremental
00059:   const metadata = await readSnapshotMetadata(lottery);
00060: 
00061:   const lastProcessedDrawId = metadata?.lastProcessedDrawId || null;
00062:   const latestDrawId = bounds?.maxDocId || null;
00063: 
00064:   const upToDate =
00065:     !force &&
00066:     lastProcessedDrawId &&
00067:     latestDrawId &&
00068:     String(lastProcessedDrawId) === String(latestDrawId);
00069: 
00070:   if (upToDate) {
00071:     return {
00072:       ok: true,
00073:       lottery,
00074:       mode: "noop",
00075:       reason: "snapshot_already_up_to_date",
00076:       bounds,
00077:       metadata,
00078:       processed: 0,
00079:     };
00080:   }
00081: 
00082:   const pending = await fetchNewDrawsAfter(
00083:     db,
00084:     lottery,
00085:     lastProcessedDrawId,
00086:     25
00087:   );
00088: 
00089:   await writeSnapshotMetadata(lottery, {
00090:     lastKnownMinYmd: bounds?.minYmd || null,
00091:     lastKnownMaxYmd: bounds?.maxYmd || null,
00092:     latestDrawId,
00093:     lastCheckAt: new Date().toISOString(),
00094:     pendingIncrementalEngine: true,
00095:     pendingNewDrawsCount: pending.draws.length,
00096:     requiresBootstrap: pending.requiresBootstrap,
00097:   });
00098: 
00099:   return {
00100:     ok: true,
00101:     lottery,
00102:     mode: pending.requiresBootstrap
00103:       ? "bootstrap_required"
00104:       : force
00105:       ? "force_pending"
00106:       : "incremental_pending",
00107:     bounds,
00108:     snapshot: current.data || null,
00109:     metadata,
00110:     pendingNewDraws: pending.draws,
00111:     pendingNewDrawsCount: pending.draws.length,
00112:     requiresBootstrap: pending.requiresBootstrap,
00113:     processed: 0,
00114:     nextStep: pending.requiresBootstrap
00115:       ? "IMPLEMENT_BOOTSTRAP_SNAPSHOT"
00116:       : "IMPLEMENT_APPLY_NEW_DRAWS",
00117:   };
00118: }
00119: 
00120: module.exports = {
00121:   updateSnapshot,
00122: };

00088: function isISODate(s) {
00089:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
00090:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(str)) return false;
00091: 
00092:   const [y, m, d] = str.split("-").map(Number);
00093:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
00094: 
00095:   return (
00096:     dt.getUTCFullYear() === y &&
00097:     dt.getUTCMonth() === m - 1 &&
00098:     dt.getUTCDate() === d
00099:   );
00100: }
00101: 
00102: function isHHMM(s) {
00103:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
00104:   const m = str.match(/^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
00105:   if (!m) return false;
00106: 
00107:   const hh = Number(m[1]);
00108:   const mm = Number(m[2]);
00109: 
00110:   return hh >= 0 && hh <= 23 && mm >= 0 && mm <= 59;
00111: }
00112: 
00113: function normalizeHHMM(value) {
00114:   const s = String(value ?? "").trim();
00115:   if (!s) return "";
00116: 
00117:   if (isHHMM(s)) return s;
00118: 
00119:   const m1 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})h$/i);
00120:   if (m1) {
00121:     const hh = Number(m1[1]);
00122:     if (hh >= 0 && hh <= 23) return `${String(hh).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
00123:     return "";
00124:   }
00125: 
00126:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
00127:   if (m2) {
00128:     const hh = Number(m2[1]);
00129:     if (hh >= 0 && hh <= 23) return `${String(hh).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
00130:     return "";
00131:   }
00132: 
00133:   const m3 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})$/);
00134:   if (m3) {
00135:     const hh = Number(m3[1]);
00136:     const mm = Number(m3[2]);
00137:     if (hh >= 0 && hh <= 23 && mm >= 0 && mm <= 59) {
00138:       return `${String(hh).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(mm).padStart(2, "0")}`;
00139:     }
00140:     return "";
00141:   }
00142: 
00143:   return "";
00144: }
00145: 
00146: function cmpHHMM(a, b) {
00147:   return String(a || "").localeCompare(String(b || ""));
00148: }
00149: 
00150: function inRangeHHMM(hhmm, from, to) {
00151:   const h = String(hhmm || "").trim();
00152:   if (!h || !isHHMM(h)) return false;
00153:   if (from && cmpHHMM(h, from) < 0) return false;
00154:   if (to && cmpHHMM(h, to) > 0) return false;
00155:   return true;
00156: }

00171: function getWindowFromQuery(req) {
00172:   const fromRaw = req.query.from != null ? String(req.query.from).trim() : "";
00173:   const toRaw = req.query.to != null ? String(req.query.to).trim() : "";
00174:

00176:   const to = toRaw ? normalizeHHMM(toRaw) : "";
00177: 
00178:   if (fromRaw && !from) {
00179:     return { error: "Parâmetro inválido: from (use HH:MM, 10h ou 10)" };
00180:   }
00181:   if (toRaw && !to) {

00183:   }

00188: function parsePositionsParam(v) {
00189:   const raw = String(v ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
00190:   if (!raw) return { positions: null, maxPos: null };
00191: 
00192:   const m = raw.match(/^(\d+)\s*(?:\-|\.\.)\s*(\d+)$/);
00193:   if (m) {
00194:     const a = Math.max(1, Number(m[1]));
00195:     const b = Math.max(1, Number(m[2]));
00196:     const lo = Math.min(a, b);
00197:     const hi = Math.max(a, b);
00198:     const positions = [];
00199:     for (let i = lo; i <= hi; i += 1) positions.push(i);
00200:     return { positions, maxPos: hi };
00201:   }
00202: 
00203:   const parts = raw
00204:     .split(",")
00205:     .map((s) => Number(String(s).trim()))
00206:     .filter((n) => Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 1);
00207: 
00208:   if (!parts.length) return { positions: null, maxPos: null };
00209: 
00210:   const uniq = Array.from(new Set(parts)).sort((a, b) => a - b);
00211:   return { positions: uniq, maxPos: uniq[uniq.length - 1] };
00212: }

00217: async function fetchDayDraws(db, ymd) {
00218:   const [s1, s2] = await Promise.all([
00219:     db.collection("draws").where("ymd", "==", ymd).get(),

00221:   ]);

00224: 
00225:   for (const doc of s1.docs) map.set(doc.id, doc);
00226:   for (const doc of s2.docs) map.set(doc.id, doc);
00227:

00229: }

00231: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
00232:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
00233:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 6);
00234:   const results = new Array(arr.length);
00235:   let idx = 0;
00236: 
00237:   async function worker() {
00238:     while (true) {
00239:       const current = idx;
00240:       idx += 1;
00241:       if (current >= arr.length) break;
00242:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
00243:     }
00244:   }
00245: 
00246:   await Promise.all(
00247:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, () => worker())
00248:   );
00249: 
00250:   return results;
00251: }

00253: async function loadPrizesForDraws(db, drawsWindow, includePrizes, positionsInfo) {
00254:   if (!includePrizes) return drawsWindow;
00255: 
00256:   const posArr =
00257:     positionsInfo && Array.isArray(positionsInfo.positions)
00258:       ? positionsInfo.positions
00259:       : null;
00260: 
00261:   const posSet =
00262:     posArr && posArr.length
00263:       ? new Set(posArr.map((n) => Number(n)))
00264:       : null;
00265: 
00266:   const draws = await mapWithConcurrency(drawsWindow, 6, async (d) => {
00267:     let q = db
00268:       .collection("draws")
00269:       .doc(d.id)
00270:       .collection("prizes")
00271:       .orderBy("position", "asc");
00272: 
00273:     if (positionsInfo && positionsInfo.maxPos && posArr) {
00274:       const maxPos = Number(positionsInfo.maxPos) || null;
00275:       const contiguousFrom1 =
00276:         maxPos &&
00277:         posArr.length === maxPos &&
00278:         Number(posArr[0]) === 1 &&
00279:         Number(posArr[posArr.length - 1]) === maxPos;
00280: 
00281:       if (contiguousFrom1) q = q.limit(maxPos);
00282:     }
00283: 
00284:     const prizesSnap = await q.get();
00285:     const rows = prizesSnap.docs.map((p) => p.data());
00286:     const prizes = posSet
00287:       ? rows.filter((r) => posSet.has(Number(r.position)))
00288:       : rows;
00289: 
00290:     return { ...d, prizes };
00291:   });
00292: 
00293:   return draws;
00294: }

00047:   return expected.sort();
00048: }
00049: 
00050: function checkDrawIntegrity(draws, lottery, date, hasFederal) {
00051:   const expectedHours = getExpectedHours(lottery, date, hasFederal);
00052: 
00053:   if (!expectedHours.length) {
00054:     return {
00055:       ok: true,
00056:       missing: [],
00057:       extra: [],
00058:       expectedHours: [],
00059:       hasFederal: Boolean(hasFederal),
00060:     };
00061:   }
00062: 
00063:   const gotHours = (Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [])
00064:     .map((d) => String(d?.close_hour || "").trim())
00065:     .filter(Boolean);
00066: 
00067:   const missing = expectedHours.filter((h) => !gotHours.includes(h));
00068:   const extra = gotHours.filter((h) => !expectedHours.includes(h));
00069: 
00070:   if (missing.length || extra.length) {
00071:     console.warn(
00072:       `[DRAW ALERT] ${lottery} ${date} missing=${missing.join(", ")} extra=${extra.join(", ")} hasFederal=${hasFederal ? "1" : "0"}`
00073:     );
00074:   }
00075: 
00076:   return {
00077:     ok: missing.length === 0 && extra.length === 0,
00078:     missing,
00079:     extra,
00080:     expectedHours,
00081:     hasFederal: Boolean(hasFederal),
00082:   };
00083: }

00088: function isISODate(s) {
00089:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
00090:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(str)) return false;
00091: 
00092:   const [y, m, d] = str.split("-").map(Number);
00093:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
00094: 
00095:   return (
00096:     dt.getUTCFullYear() === y &&
00097:     dt.getUTCMonth() === m - 1 &&
00098:     dt.getUTCDate() === d
00099:   );
00100: }

00102: function isHHMM(s) {
00103:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
00104:   const m = str.match(/^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
00105:   if (!m) return false;
00106: 
00107:   const hh = Number(m[1]);
00108:   const mm = Number(m[2]);
00109: 
00110:   return hh >= 0 && hh <= 23 && mm >= 0 && mm <= 59;
00111: }
00112: 
00113: function normalizeHHMM(value) {
00114:   const s = String(value ?? "").trim();
00115:   if (!s) return "";
00116: 
00117:   if (isHHMM(s)) return s;
00118: 
00119:   const m1 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})h$/i);
00120:   if (m1) {
00121:     const hh = Number(m1[1]);
00122:     if (hh >= 0 && hh <= 23) return `${String(hh).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
00123:     return "";
00124:   }
00125: 
00126:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
00127:   if (m2) {
00128:     const hh = Number(m2[1]);
00129:     if (hh >= 0 && hh <= 23) return `${String(hh).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
00130:     return "";
00131:   }
00132: 
00133:   const m3 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})$/);
00134:   if (m3) {
00135:     const hh = Number(m3[1]);
00136:     const mm = Number(m3[2]);
00137:     if (hh >= 0 && hh <= 23 && mm >= 0 && mm <= 59) {
00138:       return `${String(hh).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(mm).padStart(2, "0")}`;
00139:     }
00140:     return "";
00141:   }
00142: 
00143:   return "";
00144: }
00145: 
00146: function cmpHHMM(a, b) {
00147:   return String(a || "").localeCompare(String(b || ""));
00148: }
00149: 
00150: function inRangeHHMM(hhmm, from, to) {
00151:   const h = String(hhmm || "").trim();
00152:   if (!h || !isHHMM(h)) return false;
00153:   if (from && cmpHHMM(h, from) < 0) return false;
00154:   if (to && cmpHHMM(h, to) > 0) return false;
00155:   return true;
00156: }

00160: }

00163:   const raw = String(v ?? (defBool ? "1" : "0")).trim().toLowerCase();
00164:   return raw === "1" || raw === "true" || raw === "yes";
00165: }

00171: function getWindowFromQuery(req) {
00172:   const fromRaw = req.query.from != null ? String(req.query.from).trim() : "";
00173:   const toRaw = req.query.to != null ? String(req.query.to).trim() : "";
00174: 
00175:   const from = fromRaw ? normalizeHHMM(fromRaw) : "";
00176:   const to = toRaw ? normalizeHHMM(toRaw) : "";
00177: 
00178:   if (fromRaw && !from) {
00179:     return { error: "Parâmetro inválido: from (use HH:MM, 10h ou 10)" };
00180:   }
00181:   if (toRaw && !to) {
00182:     return { error: "Parâmetro inválido: to (use HH:MM, 10h ou 10)" };
00183:   }
00184: 
00185:   return { from, to };

00188: function parsePositionsParam(v) {
00189:   const raw = String(v ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
00190:   if (!raw) return { positions: null, maxPos: null };
00191: 
00192:   const m = raw.match(/^(\d+)\s*(?:\-|\.\.)\s*(\d+)$/);
00193:   if (m) {
00194:     const a = Math.max(1, Number(m[1]));
00195:     const b = Math.max(1, Number(m[2]));
00196:     const lo = Math.min(a, b);
00197:     const hi = Math.max(a, b);
00198:     const positions = [];
00199:     for (let i = lo; i <= hi; i += 1) positions.push(i);
00200:     return { positions, maxPos: hi };
00201:   }
00202: 
00203:   const parts = raw
00204:     .split(",")
00205:     .map((s) => Number(String(s).trim()))
00206:     .filter((n) => Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 1);
00207: 
00208:   if (!parts.length) return { positions: null, maxPos: null };
00209: 
00210:   const uniq = Array.from(new Set(parts)).sort((a, b) => a - b);
00211:   return { positions: uniq, maxPos: uniq[uniq.length - 1] };
00212: }

00217: async function fetchDayDraws(db, ymd) {
00218:   const [s1, s2] = await Promise.all([
00219:     db.collection("draws").where("ymd", "==", ymd).get(),
00220:     db.collection("draws").where("date", "==", ymd).get(),
00221:   ]);
00222: 
00223:   const map = new Map();
00224: 
00225:   for (const doc of s1.docs) map.set(doc.id, doc);
00226:   for (const doc of s2.docs) map.set(doc.id, doc);
00227: 
00228:   return Array.from(map.values());
00229: }

00231: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
00232:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
00233:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 6);
00234:   const results = new Array(arr.length);
00235:   let idx = 0;
00236: 
00237:   async function worker() {
00238:     while (true) {
00239:       const current = idx;
00240:       idx += 1;
00241:       if (current >= arr.length) break;
00242:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
00243:     }
00244:   }
00245: 
00246:   await Promise.all(
00247:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, () => worker())
00248:   );
00249: 
00250:   return results;
00251: }
00252: 
00253: async function loadPrizesForDraws(db, drawsWindow, includePrizes, positionsInfo) {
00254:   if (!includePrizes) return drawsWindow;
00255: 
00256:   const posArr =
00257:     positionsInfo && Array.isArray(positionsInfo.positions)
00258:       ? positionsInfo.positions
00259:       : null;
00260: 
00261:   const posSet =
00262:     posArr && posArr.length
00263:       ? new Set(posArr.map((n) => Number(n)))
00264:       : null;
00265: 
00266:   const draws = await mapWithConcurrency(drawsWindow, 6, async (d) => {
00267:     let q = db
00268:       .collection("draws")
00269:       .doc(d.id)
00270:       .collection("prizes")
00271:       .orderBy("position", "asc");
00272: 
00273:     if (positionsInfo && positionsInfo.maxPos && posArr) {
00274:       const maxPos = Number(positionsInfo.maxPos) || null;
00275:       const contiguousFrom1 =
00276:         maxPos &&
00277:         posArr.length === maxPos &&
00278:         Number(posArr[0]) === 1 &&
00279:         Number(posArr[posArr.length - 1]) === maxPos;
00280: 
00281:       if (contiguousFrom1) q = q.limit(maxPos);
00282:     }
00283: 
00284:     const prizesSnap = await q.get();
00285:     const rows = prizesSnap.docs.map((p) => p.data());
00286:     const prizes = posSet
00287:       ? rows.filter((r) => posSet.has(Number(r.position)))
00288:       : rows;
00289: 
00290:     return { ...d, prizes };
00291:   });
00292: 
00293:   return draws;
00294: }

00325: function applyHourWindow(draws, from, to) {

00188: function parsePositionsParam(v) {
00189:   const raw = String(v ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
00190:   if (!raw) return { positions: null, maxPos: null };
00191: 
00192:   const m = raw.match(/^(\d+)\s*(?:\-|\.\.)\s*(\d+)$/);
00193:   if (m) {
00194:     const a = Math.max(1, Number(m[1]));
00195:     const b = Math.max(1, Number(m[2]));
00196:     const lo = Math.min(a, b);
00197:     const hi = Math.max(a, b);
00198:     const positions = [];
00199:     for (let i = lo; i <= hi; i += 1) positions.push(i);
00200:     return { positions, maxPos: hi };
00201:   }
00202: 
00203:   const parts = raw
00204:     .split(",")
00205:     .map((s) => Number(String(s).trim()))
00206:     .filter((n) => Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 1);
00207: 
00208:   if (!parts.length) return { positions: null, maxPos: null };
00209: 
00210:   const uniq = Array.from(new Set(parts)).sort((a, b) => a - b);
00211:   return { positions: uniq, maxPos: uniq[uniq.length - 1] };
00212: }
00213:

00215:    FIRESTORE
00216: ========================= */
00217: async function fetchDayDraws(db, ymd) {
00218:   const [s1, s2] = await Promise.all([
00219:     db.collection("draws").where("ymd", "==", ymd).get(),
00220:     db.collection("draws").where("date", "==", ymd).get(),
00221:   ]);
00222: 
00223:   const map = new Map();
00224: 
00225:   for (const doc of s1.docs) map.set(doc.id, doc);
00226:   for (const doc of s2.docs) map.set(doc.id, doc);
00227: 
00228:   return Array.from(map.values());
00229: }

00231: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
00232:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
00233:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 6);
00234:   const results = new Array(arr.length);
00235:   let idx = 0;
00236: 
00237:   async function worker() {
00238:     while (true) {
00239:       const current = idx;
00240:       idx += 1;
00241:       if (current >= arr.length) break;
00242:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
00243:     }
00244:   }
00245: 
00246:   await Promise.all(
00247:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, () => worker())
00248:   );
00249: 
00250:   return results;
00251: }
00252: 
00253: async function loadPrizesForDraws(db, drawsWindow, includePrizes, positionsInfo) {
00254:   if (!includePrizes) return drawsWindow;
00255: 
00256:   const posArr =
00257:     positionsInfo && Array.isArray(positionsInfo.positions)
00258:       ? positionsInfo.positions
00259:       : null;
00260: 
00261:   const posSet =
00262:     posArr && posArr.length
00263:       ? new Set(posArr.map((n) => Number(n)))
00264:       : null;
00265: 
00266:   const draws = await mapWithConcurrency(drawsWindow, 6, async (d) => {
00267:     let q = db
00268:       .collection("draws")
00269:       .doc(d.id)
00270:       .collection("prizes")
00271:       .orderBy("position", "asc");
00272: 
00273:     if (positionsInfo && positionsInfo.maxPos && posArr) {
00274:       const maxPos = Number(positionsInfo.maxPos) || null;
00275:       const contiguousFrom1 =
00276:         maxPos &&
00277:         posArr.length === maxPos &&
00278:         Number(posArr[0]) === 1 &&
00279:         Number(posArr[posArr.length - 1]) === maxPos;
00280: 
00281:       if (contiguousFrom1) q = q.limit(maxPos);
00282:     }
00283: 
00284:     const prizesSnap = await q.get();
00285:     const rows = prizesSnap.docs.map((p) => p.data());
00286:     const prizes = posSet
00287:       ? rows.filter((r) => posSet.has(Number(r.position)))
00288:       : rows;
00289: 
00290:     return { ...d, prizes };
00291:   });
00292: 
00293:   return draws;
00294: }

00482: async function handleRange(req, res) {
00483:   try {
00484:     const db = getDb();
00485: 
00486:     const dateFrom = String(req.query.dateFrom || "").trim();
00487:     const dateTo = String(req.query.dateTo || "").trim();
00488:     const lottery = getLotteryFromQuery(req);
00489: 
00490:     if (!requireLotteryOr400(lottery, res)) return;
00491: 
00492:     if (!isISODate(dateFrom) || !isISODate(dateTo)) {
00493:       return res.status(400).json({
00494:         ok: false,
00495:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: dateFrom/dateTo (use YYYY-MM-DD)",
00496:       });
00497:     }
00498: 
00499:     if (dateFrom > dateTo) {
00500:       return res.status(400).json({
00501:         ok: false,
00502:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: dateFrom não pode ser maior que dateTo",
00503:       });
00504:     }
00505: 
00506:     const win = getWindowFromQuery(req);
00507:     if (win.error) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: win.error });
00508: 
00509:     const includePrizes = parseIncludePrizes(req.query.includePrizes, false);
00510:     const positionsInfo = parsePositionsParam(req.query.positions);
00511: 
00512:     const snap = await db
00513:       .collection("draws")
00514:       .where("ymd", ">=", dateFrom)
00515:       .where("ymd", "<=", dateTo)
00516:       .orderBy("ymd", "asc")
00517:       .get();
00518: 
00519:     const rawDraws = snap.docs.map(normalizeDrawDoc);
00520:     const byLottery = applyLotteryFilter(rawDraws, lottery).sort(sortDrawsByDateThenHour);
00521:     const drawsWindow = applyHourWindow(byLottery, win.from || "", win.to || "");
00522:     const draws = await loadPrizesForDraws(db, drawsWindow, includePrizes, positionsInfo);
00523: 
00524:     const groupedIntegrity = {};
00525:     if (lottery === "PT_RIO") {
00526:       const rawByDay = rawDraws.reduce((acc, d) => {
00527:         const ymd = String(d.ymd || d.date || "").trim();
00528:         if (!ymd) return acc;
00529:         if (!acc[ymd]) acc[ymd] = [];
00530:         acc[ymd].push(d);
00531:         return acc;
00532:       }, {});
00533: 
00534:       const filteredByDay = draws.reduce((acc, d) => {
00535:         const ymd = String(d.ymd || d.date || "").trim();
00536:         if (!ymd) return acc;
00537:         if (!acc[ymd]) acc[ymd] = [];
00538:         acc[ymd].push(d);
00539:         return acc;
00540:       }, {});
00541: 
00542:       const allDays = Array.from(
00543:         new Set([
00544:           ...Object.keys(rawByDay),
00545:           ...Object.keys(filteredByDay),
00546:         ])
00547:       ).sort();
00548: 
00549:       for (const ymd of allDays) {
00550:         const dayRaw = rawByDay[ymd] || [];
00551:         const dayFiltered = filteredByDay[ymd] || [];
00552:         const hasFederal = hasFederalInDraws(dayRaw);
00553:         groupedIntegrity[ymd] = checkDrawIntegrity(dayFiltered, lottery, ymd, hasFederal);
00554:       }
00555:     }
00556: 
00557:     return res.json({
00558:       ok: true,
00559:       mode: "range",
00560:       integrityByDay: groupedIntegrity,
00561:       dateFrom,
00562:       dateTo,
00563:       lottery,
00564:       from: win.from || null,
00565:       to: win.to || null,
00566:       includePrizes,
00567:       count: draws.length,
00568:       draws,
00569:     });
00570:   } catch (e) {
00571:     return res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "erro" });
00572:   }
00573: }
00574: 
00575: router.get("/draws/day", handleDay);
00576: router.get("/draws/range", handleRange);
00577:

00188: function parsePositionsParam(v) {
00189:   const raw = String(v ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
00190:   if (!raw) return { positions: null, maxPos: null };
00191: 
00192:   const m = raw.match(/^(\d+)\s*(?:\-|\.\.)\s*(\d+)$/);
00193:   if (m) {
00194:     const a = Math.max(1, Number(m[1]));
00195:     const b = Math.max(1, Number(m[2]));
00196:     const lo = Math.min(a, b);
00197:     const hi = Math.max(a, b);
00198:     const positions = [];
00199:     for (let i = lo; i <= hi; i += 1) positions.push(i);
00200:     return { positions, maxPos: hi };
00201:   }
00202: 
00203:   const parts = raw
00204:     .split(",")
00205:     .map((s) => Number(String(s).trim()))
00206:     .filter((n) => Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 1);
00207: 
00208:   if (!parts.length) return { positions: null, maxPos: null };
00209: 
00210:   const uniq = Array.from(new Set(parts)).sort((a, b) => a - b);
00211:   return { positions: uniq, maxPos: uniq[uniq.length - 1] };
00212: }

00217: async function fetchDayDraws(db, ymd) {
00218:   const [s1, s2] = await Promise.all([
00219:     db.collection("draws").where("ymd", "==", ymd).get(),
00220:     db.collection("draws").where("date", "==", ymd).get(),
00221:   ]);
00222: 
00223:   const map = new Map();
00224: 
00225:   for (const doc of s1.docs) map.set(doc.id, doc);
00226:   for (const doc of s2.docs) map.set(doc.id, doc);
00227: 
00228:   return Array.from(map.values());
00229: }

00231: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
00232:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
00233:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 6);
00234:   const results = new Array(arr.length);
00235:   let idx = 0;
00236: 
00237:   async function worker() {
00238:     while (true) {
00239:       const current = idx;
00240:       idx += 1;
00241:       if (current >= arr.length) break;
00242:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
00243:     }
00244:   }
00245: 
00246:   await Promise.all(
00247:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, () => worker())
00248:   );
00249: 
00250:   return results;
00251: }

00253: async function loadPrizesForDraws(db, drawsWindow, includePrizes, positionsInfo) {
00254:   if (!includePrizes) return drawsWindow;
00255: 
00256:   const posArr =
00257:     positionsInfo && Array.isArray(positionsInfo.positions)
00258:       ? positionsInfo.positions
00259:       : null;
00260: 
00261:   const posSet =
00262:     posArr && posArr.length
00263:       ? new Set(posArr.map((n) => Number(n)))
00264:       : null;
00265: 
00266:   const draws = await mapWithConcurrency(drawsWindow, 6, async (d) => {
00267:     let q = db
00268:       .collection("draws")
00269:       .doc(d.id)
00270:       .collection("prizes")
00271:       .orderBy("position", "asc");
00272: 
00273:     if (positionsInfo && positionsInfo.maxPos && posArr) {
00274:       const maxPos = Number(positionsInfo.maxPos) || null;
00275:       const contiguousFrom1 =
00276:         maxPos &&
00277:         posArr.length === maxPos &&
00278:         Number(posArr[0]) === 1 &&
00279:         Number(posArr[posArr.length - 1]) === maxPos;
00280: 
00281:       if (contiguousFrom1) q = q.limit(maxPos);
00282:     }
00283: 
00284:     const prizesSnap = await q.get();
00285:     const rows = prizesSnap.docs.map((p) => p.data());
00286:     const prizes = posSet
00287:       ? rows.filter((r) => posSet.has(Number(r.position)))
00288:       : rows;
00289: 
00290:     return { ...d, prizes };
00291:   });
00292: 
00293:   return draws;
00294: }

00299: function normalizeDrawDoc(doc) {
00300:   const data = doc.data() || {};
00301:   return {
00302:     id: doc.id,
00303:     ...data,
00304:     close_hour: normalizeHHMM(data.close_hour ?? data.close ?? data.hour),
00305:   };

00308: function sortDrawsByHourOnly(a, b) {
00309:   return cmpHHMM(a.close_hour, b.close_hour);
00310: }
00311: 
00312: function sortDrawsByDateThenHour(a, b) {
00313:   const da = String(a.ymd || a.date || "");
00314:   const db = String(b.ymd || b.date || "");
00315:   if (da !== db) return da.localeCompare(db);
00316:   return cmpHHMM(a.close_hour, b.close_hour);
00317: }

00319: function applyLotteryFilter(draws, lottery) {
00320:   return (Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []).filter(
00321:     (d) => upTrim(d.lottery_key) === lottery
00322:   );
00323: }
00324: 
00325: function applyHourWindow(draws, from, to) {
00326:   return (Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []).filter((d) => {
00327:     if (!from && !to) return true;
00328:     return inRangeHHMM(d.close_hour, from || "", to || "");
00329:   });
00330: }

00333:   return (Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []).some(
00334:     (d) => upTrim(d.lottery_key) === "FEDERAL"
00335:   );

00187: 
00188: function parsePositionsParam(v) {
00189:   const raw = String(v ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
00190:   if (!raw) return { positions: null, maxPos: null };
00191: 
00192:   const m = raw.match(/^(\d+)\s*(?:\-|\.\.)\s*(\d+)$/);
00193:   if (m) {
00194:     const a = Math.max(1, Number(m[1]));
00195:     const b = Math.max(1, Number(m[2]));
00196:     const lo = Math.min(a, b);
00197:     const hi = Math.max(a, b);
00198:     const positions = [];
00199:     for (let i = lo; i <= hi; i += 1) positions.push(i);
00200:     return { positions, maxPos: hi };
00201:   }
00202: 
00203:   const parts = raw
00204:     .split(",")
00205:     .map((s) => Number(String(s).trim()))
00206:     .filter((n) => Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 1);
00207: 
00208:   if (!parts.length) return { positions: null, maxPos: null };
00209: 
00210:   const uniq = Array.from(new Set(parts)).sort((a, b) => a - b);
00211:   return { positions: uniq, maxPos: uniq[uniq.length - 1] };
00212: }
00213: 
00214: /* =========================
00215:    FIRESTORE
00216: ========================= */
00217: async function fetchDayDraws(db, ymd) {
00218:   const [s1, s2] = await Promise.all([
00219:     db.collection("draws").where("ymd", "==", ymd).get(),
00220:     db.collection("draws").where("date", "==", ymd).get(),
00221:   ]);
00222: 
00223:   const map = new Map();
00224: 
00225:   for (const doc of s1.docs) map.set(doc.id, doc);
00226:   for (const doc of s2.docs) map.set(doc.id, doc);
00227: 
00228:   return Array.from(map.values());
00229: }
00230: 
00231: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
00232:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
00233:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 6);
00234:   const results = new Array(arr.length);
00235:   let idx = 0;
00236: 
00237:   async function worker() {
00238:     while (true) {
00239:       const current = idx;
00240:       idx += 1;
00241:       if (current >= arr.length) break;
00242:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
00243:     }
00244:   }
00245: 
00246:   await Promise.all(
00247:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, () => worker())
00248:   );
00249: 
00250:   return results;
00251: }
00252: 
00253: async function loadPrizesForDraws(db, drawsWindow, includePrizes, positionsInfo) {
00254:   if (!includePrizes) return drawsWindow;
00255: 
00256:   const posArr =
00257:     positionsInfo && Array.isArray(positionsInfo.positions)
00258:       ? positionsInfo.positions
00259:       : null;
00260: 
00261:   const posSet =
00262:     posArr && posArr.length
00263:       ? new Set(posArr.map((n) => Number(n)))
00264:       : null;
00265: 
00266:   const draws = await mapWithConcurrency(drawsWindow, 6, async (d) => {
00267:     let q = db
00268:       .collection("draws")
00269:       .doc(d.id)
00270:       .collection("prizes")
00271:       .orderBy("position", "asc");
00272: 
00273:     if (positionsInfo && positionsInfo.maxPos && posArr) {
00274:       const maxPos = Number(positionsInfo.maxPos) || null;
00275:       const contiguousFrom1 =
00276:         maxPos &&
00277:         posArr.length === maxPos &&
00278:         Number(posArr[0]) === 1 &&
00279:         Number(posArr[posArr.length - 1]) === maxPos;
00280: 
00281:       if (contiguousFrom1) q = q.limit(maxPos);
00282:     }
00283: 
00284:     const prizesSnap = await q.get();
00285:     const rows = prizesSnap.docs.map((p) => p.data());
00286:     const prizes = posSet
00287:       ? rows.filter((r) => posSet.has(Number(r.position)))
00288:       : rows;
00289: 
00290:     return { ...d, prizes };
00291:   });
00292: 
00293:   return draws;
00294: }
00295: 
00296: /* =========================
00297:    NORMALIZAÇÃO DE DRAW
00298: ========================= */
00299: function normalizeDrawDoc(doc) {
00300:   const data = doc.data() || {};
00301:   return {
00302:     id: doc.id,
00303:     ...data,
00304:     close_hour: normalizeHHMM(data.close_hour ?? data.close ?? data.hour),
00305:   };
00306: }
00307: 
00308: function sortDrawsByHourOnly(a, b) {
00309:   return cmpHHMM(a.close_hour, b.close_hour);
00310: }
00311: 
00312: function sortDrawsByDateThenHour(a, b) {
00313:   const da = String(a.ymd || a.date || "");
00314:   const db = String(b.ymd || b.date || "");
00315:   if (da !== db) return da.localeCompare(db);
00316:   return cmpHHMM(a.close_hour, b.close_hour);
00317: }
00318: 
00319: function applyLotteryFilter(draws, lottery) {
00320:   return (Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []).filter(
00321:     (d) => upTrim(d.lottery_key) === lottery
00322:   );
00323: }
00324: 
00325: function applyHourWindow(draws, from, to) {
00326:   return (Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []).filter((d) => {
00327:     if (!from && !to) return true;
00328:     return inRangeHHMM(d.close_hour, from || "", to || "");
00329:   });
00330: }
00331: 
00332: function hasFederalInDraws(draws) {
00333:   return (Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []).some(
00334:     (d) => upTrim(d.lottery_key) === "FEDERAL"
00335:   );
00336: }

00339:    REDIRECT / VALIDATION
00340: ========================= */
00341: function redirect308(destPath) {
00342:   return (req, res) => {
00343:     const q = req.url.includes("?") ? req.url.slice(req.url.indexOf("?")) : "";
00344:     return res.redirect(308, `${req.baseUrl}${destPath}${q}`);
00345:   };
00346: }
00347: 
00348: function requireLotteryOr400(lottery, res) {
00349:   if (!lottery) {
00350:     res.status(400).json({
00351:       ok: false,
00352:       error: "Parâmetro inválido: lottery (use RJ/PT_RIO ou FED/FEDERAL)",
00353:     });
00354:     return false;
00355:   }
00356:   return true;
00357: }

00362: router.get("/draws", async (req, res) => {
00363:   try {
00364:     const db = getDb();
00365: 
00366:     const date = String(req.query.date || "").trim();
00367:     const lottery = getLotteryFromQuery(req);
00368: 
00369:     const fromRaw = req.query.from != null ? String(req.query.from).trim() : "";
00370:     const toRaw = req.query.to != null ? String(req.query.to).trim() : "";
00371: 
00372:     const from = fromRaw ? normalizeHHMM(fromRaw) : "";
00373:     const to = toRaw ? normalizeHHMM(toRaw) : "";
00374: 
00375:     const includePrizes = parseIncludePrizes(req.query.includePrizes, true);
00376:     const positionsInfo = parsePositionsParam(req.query.positions);
00377: 
00378:     if (!isISODate(date)) {
00379:       return res.status(400).json({
00380:         ok: false,
00381:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: date (use YYYY-MM-DD)",
00382:       });
00383:     }
00384: 
00385:     if (!requireLotteryOr400(lottery, res)) return;
00386: 
00387:     if (fromRaw && !from) {
00388:       return res.status(400).json({
00389:         ok: false,
00390:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: from (use HH:MM, 10h ou 10)",
00391:       });
00392:     }
00393: 
00394:     if (toRaw && !to) {
00395:       return res.status(400).json({
00396:         ok: false,
00397:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: to (use HH:MM, 10h ou 10)",
00398:       });
00399:     }
00400: 
00401:     const docs = await fetchDayDraws(db, date);
00402:     const rawDraws = docs.map(normalizeDrawDoc);
00403:     const hasFederal = hasFederalInDraws(rawDraws);
00404: 
00405:     const byLottery = applyLotteryFilter(rawDraws, lottery).sort(sortDrawsByHourOnly);
00406:     const drawsWindow = applyHourWindow(byLottery, from || "", to || "");
00407:     const draws = await loadPrizesForDraws(db, drawsWindow, includePrizes, positionsInfo);
00408: 
00409:     const integrity = checkDrawIntegrity(draws, lottery, date, hasFederal);
00410: 
00411:     return res.json({
00412:       ok: true,
00413:       integrity,
00414:       date,
00415:       lottery,
00416:       from: from || null,
00417:       to: to || null,
00418:       includePrizes,
00419:       count: draws.length,
00420:       draws,
00421:     });
00422:   } catch (e) {
00423:     return res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "erro" });
00424:   }
00425: });

00433: async function handleDay(req, res) {
00434:   try {
00435:     const db = getDb();
00436: 
00437:     const date = String(req.query.date || "").trim();
00438:     const lottery = getLotteryFromQuery(req);
00439: 
00440:     if (!requireLotteryOr400(lottery, res)) return;
00441: 
00442:     if (!isISODate(date)) {
00443:       return res.status(400).json({
00444:         ok: false,
00445:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: date (use YYYY-MM-DD)",
00446:       });
00447:     }
00448: 
00449:     const win = getWindowFromQuery(req);
00450:     if (win.error) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: win.error });
00451: 
00452:     const includePrizes = parseIncludePrizes(req.query.includePrizes, true);
00453:     const positionsInfo = parsePositionsParam(req.query.positions);
00454: 
00455:     const docs = await fetchDayDraws(db, date);
00456:     const rawDraws = docs.map(normalizeDrawDoc);
00457:     const hasFederal = hasFederalInDraws(rawDraws);
00458: 
00459:     const byLottery = applyLotteryFilter(rawDraws, lottery).sort(sortDrawsByHourOnly);
00460:     const drawsWindow = applyHourWindow(byLottery, win.from || "", win.to || "");
00461:     const draws = await loadPrizesForDraws(db, drawsWindow, includePrizes, positionsInfo);
00462: 
00463:     const integrity = checkDrawIntegrity(draws, lottery, date, hasFederal);
00464: 
00465:     return res.json({
00466:       ok: true,
00467:       mode: "day",
00468:       integrity,
00469:       date,
00470:       lottery,
00471:       from: win.from || null,
00472:       to: win.to || null,
00473:       includePrizes,
00474:       count: draws.length,
00475:       draws,
00476:     });
00477:   } catch (e) {
00478:     return res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "erro" });
00479:   }
00480: }
00481: 
00482: async function handleRange(req, res) {
00483:   try {
00484:     const db = getDb();
00485: 
00486:     const dateFrom = String(req.query.dateFrom || "").trim();
00487:     const dateTo = String(req.query.dateTo || "").trim();
00488:     const lottery = getLotteryFromQuery(req);
00489: 
00490:     if (!requireLotteryOr400(lottery, res)) return;
00491: 
00492:     if (!isISODate(dateFrom) || !isISODate(dateTo)) {
00493:       return res.status(400).json({
00494:         ok: false,
00495:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: dateFrom/dateTo (use YYYY-MM-DD)",
00496:       });
00497:     }
00498: 
00499:     if (dateFrom > dateTo) {
00500:       return res.status(400).json({
00501:         ok: false,
00502:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: dateFrom não pode ser maior que dateTo",
00503:       });
00504:     }
00505: 
00506:     const win = getWindowFromQuery(req);
00507:     if (win.error) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: win.error });
00508: 
00509:     const includePrizes = parseIncludePrizes(req.query.includePrizes, false);
00510:     const positionsInfo = parsePositionsParam(req.query.positions);
00511: 
00512:     const snap = await db
00513:       .collection("draws")
00514:       .where("ymd", ">=", dateFrom)
00515:       .where("ymd", "<=", dateTo)
00516:       .orderBy("ymd", "asc")
00517:       .get();
00518: 
00519:     const rawDraws = snap.docs.map(normalizeDrawDoc);
00520:     const byLottery = applyLotteryFilter(rawDraws, lottery).sort(sortDrawsByDateThenHour);
00521:     const drawsWindow = applyHourWindow(byLottery, win.from || "", win.to || "");
00522:     const draws = await loadPrizesForDraws(db, drawsWindow, includePrizes, positionsInfo);
00523: 
00524:     const groupedIntegrity = {};
00525:     if (lottery === "PT_RIO") {
00526:       const rawByDay = rawDraws.reduce((acc, d) => {
00527:         const ymd = String(d.ymd || d.date || "").trim();
00528:         if (!ymd) return acc;
00529:         if (!acc[ymd]) acc[ymd] = [];
00530:         acc[ymd].push(d);
00531:         return acc;
00532:       }, {});
00533: 
00534:       const filteredByDay = draws.reduce((acc, d) => {
00535:         const ymd = String(d.ymd || d.date || "").trim();
00536:         if (!ymd) return acc;
00537:         if (!acc[ymd]) acc[ymd] = [];
00538:         acc[ymd].push(d);
00539:         return acc;
00540:       }, {});
00541: 
00542:       const allDays = Array.from(
00543:         new Set([
00544:           ...Object.keys(rawByDay),
00545:           ...Object.keys(filteredByDay),
00546:         ])
00547:       ).sort();
00548: 
00549:       for (const ymd of allDays) {
00550:         const dayRaw = rawByDay[ymd] || [];
00551:         const dayFiltered = filteredByDay[ymd] || [];
00552:         const hasFederal = hasFederalInDraws(dayRaw);
00553:         groupedIntegrity[ymd] = checkDrawIntegrity(dayFiltered, lottery, ymd, hasFederal);
00554:       }
00555:     }
00556: 
00557:     return res.json({
00558:       ok: true,
00559:       mode: "range",
00560:       integrityByDay: groupedIntegrity,
00561:       dateFrom,
00562:       dateTo,
00563:       lottery,
00564:       from: win.from || null,
00565:       to: win.to || null,
00566:       includePrizes,
00567:       count: draws.length,
00568:       draws,
00569:     });
00570:   } catch (e) {
00571:     return res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "erro" });
00572:   }
00573: }
00574: 
00575: router.get("/draws/day", handleDay);
00576: router.get("/draws/range", handleRange);
00577: 
00578: module.exports = router;

00482: async function handleRange(req, res) {
00483:   try {
00484:     const db = getDb();
00485: 
00486:     const dateFrom = String(req.query.dateFrom || "").trim();
00487:     const dateTo = String(req.query.dateTo || "").trim();
00488:     const lottery = getLotteryFromQuery(req);
00489: 
00490:     if (!requireLotteryOr400(lottery, res)) return;
00491: 
00492:     if (!isISODate(dateFrom) || !isISODate(dateTo)) {
00493:       return res.status(400).json({
00494:         ok: false,
00495:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: dateFrom/dateTo (use YYYY-MM-DD)",
00496:       });
00497:     }
00498: 
00499:     if (dateFrom > dateTo) {
00500:       return res.status(400).json({
00501:         ok: false,
00502:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: dateFrom não pode ser maior que dateTo",
00503:       });
00504:     }
00505: 
00506:     const win = getWindowFromQuery(req);
00507:     if (win.error) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: win.error });
00508: 
00509:     const includePrizes = parseIncludePrizes(req.query.includePrizes, false);
00510:     const positionsInfo = parsePositionsParam(req.query.positions);
00511: 
00512:     const snap = await db
00513:       .collection("draws")
00514:       .where("ymd", ">=", dateFrom)
00515:       .where("ymd", "<=", dateTo)
00516:       .orderBy("ymd", "asc")
00517:       .get();
00518: 
00519:     const rawDraws = snap.docs.map(normalizeDrawDoc);
00520:     const byLottery = applyLotteryFilter(rawDraws, lottery).sort(sortDrawsByDateThenHour);
00521:     const drawsWindow = applyHourWindow(byLottery, win.from || "", win.to || "");
00522:     const draws = await loadPrizesForDraws(db, drawsWindow, includePrizes, positionsInfo);
00523: 
00524:     const groupedIntegrity = {};
00525:     if (lottery === "PT_RIO") {
00526:       const rawByDay = rawDraws.reduce((acc, d) => {
00527:         const ymd = String(d.ymd || d.date || "").trim();
00528:         if (!ymd) return acc;
00529:         if (!acc[ymd]) acc[ymd] = [];
00530:         acc[ymd].push(d);
00531:         return acc;
00532:       }, {});
00533: 
00534:       const filteredByDay = draws.reduce((acc, d) => {
00535:         const ymd = String(d.ymd || d.date || "").trim();
00536:         if (!ymd) return acc;
00537:         if (!acc[ymd]) acc[ymd] = [];
00538:         acc[ymd].push(d);
00539:         return acc;
00540:       }, {});
00541: 
00542:       const allDays = Array.from(
00543:         new Set([
00544:           ...Object.keys(rawByDay),
00545:           ...Object.keys(filteredByDay),
00546:         ])
00547:       ).sort();
00548: 
00549:       for (const ymd of allDays) {
00550:         const dayRaw = rawByDay[ymd] || [];
00551:         const dayFiltered = filteredByDay[ymd] || [];
00552:         const hasFederal = hasFederalInDraws(dayRaw);
00553:         groupedIntegrity[ymd] = checkDrawIntegrity(dayFiltered, lottery, ymd, hasFederal);
00554:       }
00555:     }
00556: 
00557:     return res.json({
00558:       ok: true,
00559:       mode: "range",
00560:       integrityByDay: groupedIntegrity,
00561:       dateFrom,
00562:       dateTo,
00563:       lottery,
00564:       from: win.from || null,
00565:       to: win.to || null,
00566:       includePrizes,
00567:       count: draws.length,
00568:       draws,
00569:     });
00570:   } catch (e) {
00571:     return res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "erro" });
00572:   }
00573: }

00576: router.get("/draws/range", handleRange);

00217: async function fetchDayDraws(db, ymd) {

00231: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
00232:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
00233:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 6);
00234:   const results = new Array(arr.length);
00235:   let idx = 0;
00236: 
00237:   async function worker() {
00238:     while (true) {
00239:       const current = idx;
00240:       idx += 1;
00241:       if (current >= arr.length) break;
00242:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
00243:     }
00244:   }
00245: 
00246:   await Promise.all(
00247:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, () => worker())
00248:   );
00249: 
00250:   return results;
00251: }

00253: async function loadPrizesForDraws(db, drawsWindow, includePrizes, positionsInfo) {
00254:   if (!includePrizes) return drawsWindow;
00255: 
00256:   const posArr =
00257:     positionsInfo && Array.isArray(positionsInfo.positions)
00258:       ? positionsInfo.positions
00259:       : null;
00260: 
00261:   const posSet =
00262:     posArr && posArr.length
00263:       ? new Set(posArr.map((n) => Number(n)))
00264:       : null;
00265: 
00266:   const draws = await mapWithConcurrency(drawsWindow, 6, async (d) => {
00267:     let q = db
00268:       .collection("draws")
00269:       .doc(d.id)
00270:       .collection("prizes")
00271:       .orderBy("position", "asc");
00272: 
00273:     if (positionsInfo && positionsInfo.maxPos && posArr) {
00274:       const maxPos = Number(positionsInfo.maxPos) || null;
00275:       const contiguousFrom1 =
00276:         maxPos &&
00277:         posArr.length === maxPos &&
00278:         Number(posArr[0]) === 1 &&
00279:         Number(posArr[posArr.length - 1]) === maxPos;
00280: 
00281:       if (contiguousFrom1) q = q.limit(maxPos);
00282:     }
00283: 
00284:     const prizesSnap = await q.get();
00285:     const rows = prizesSnap.docs.map((p) => p.data());
00286:     const prizes = posSet
00287:       ? rows.filter((r) => posSet.has(Number(r.position)))
00288:       : rows;
00289: 
00290:     return { ...d, prizes };
00291:   });
00292: 
00293:   return draws;
00294: }

00299: function normalizeDrawDoc(doc) {
00300:   const data = doc.data() || {};
00301:   return {
00302:     id: doc.id,
00303:     ...data,
00304:     close_hour: normalizeHHMM(data.close_hour ?? data.close ?? data.hour),
00305:   };
00306: }

00308: function sortDrawsByHourOnly(a, b) {
00309:   return cmpHHMM(a.close_hour, b.close_hour);
00310: }
00311: 
00312: function sortDrawsByDateThenHour(a, b) {
00313:   const da = String(a.ymd || a.date || "");
00314:   const db = String(b.ymd || b.date || "");
00315:   if (da !== db) return da.localeCompare(db);
00316:   return cmpHHMM(a.close_hour, b.close_hour);
00317: }
00318: 
00319: function applyLotteryFilter(draws, lottery) {
00320:   return (Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []).filter(
00321:     (d) => upTrim(d.lottery_key) === lottery
00322:   );
00323: }
00324: 
00325: function applyHourWindow(draws, from, to) {
00326:   return (Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []).filter((d) => {
00327:     if (!from && !to) return true;
00328:     return inRangeHHMM(d.close_hour, from || "", to || "");
00329:   });
00330: }
00331: 
00332: function hasFederalInDraws(draws) {
00333:   return (Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []).some(
00334:     (d) => upTrim(d.lottery_key) === "FEDERAL"
00335:   );
00336: }

00350:     res.status(400).json({

00264:       : null;
00265: 
00266:   const draws = await mapWithConcurrency(drawsWindow, 6, async (d) => {
00267:     let q = db
00268:       .collection("draws")
00269:       .doc(d.id)
00270:       .collection("prizes")
00271:       .orderBy("position", "asc");
00272: 
00273:     if (positionsInfo && positionsInfo.maxPos && posArr) {
00274:       const maxPos = Number(positionsInfo.maxPos) || null;
00275:       const contiguousFrom1 =
00276:         maxPos &&
00277:         posArr.length === maxPos &&
00278:         Number(posArr[0]) === 1 &&
00279:         Number(posArr[posArr.length - 1]) === maxPos;
00280: 
00281:       if (contiguousFrom1) q = q.limit(maxPos);
00282:     }
00283: 
00284:     const prizesSnap = await q.get();
00285:     const rows = prizesSnap.docs.map((p) => p.data());
00286:     const prizes = posSet
00287:       ? rows.filter((r) => posSet.has(Number(r.position)))
00288:       : rows;
00289: 
00290:     return { ...d, prizes };
00291:   });
00292: 
00293:   return draws;
00294: }
00295: 
00296: /* =========================
00297:    NORMALIZAÇÃO DE DRAW
00298: ========================= */
00299: function normalizeDrawDoc(doc) {
00300:   const data = doc.data() || {};
00301:   return {
00302:     id: doc.id,
00303:     ...data,
00304:     close_hour: normalizeHHMM(data.close_hour ?? data.close ?? data.hour),
00305:   };
00306: }
00307: 
00308: function sortDrawsByHourOnly(a, b) {
00309:   return cmpHHMM(a.close_hour, b.close_hour);
00310: }
00311: 
00312: function sortDrawsByDateThenHour(a, b) {
00313:   const da = String(a.ymd || a.date || "");
00314:   const db = String(b.ymd || b.date || "");
00315:   if (da !== db) return da.localeCompare(db);
00316:   return cmpHHMM(a.close_hour, b.close_hour);
00317: }
00318: 
00319: function applyLotteryFilter(draws, lottery) {
00320:   return (Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []).filter(
00321:     (d) => upTrim(d.lottery_key) === lottery
00322:   );
00323: }
00324: 
00325: function applyHourWindow(draws, from, to) {
00326:   return (Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []).filter((d) => {
00327:     if (!from && !to) return true;
00328:     return inRangeHHMM(d.close_hour, from || "", to || "");
00329:   });
00330: }
00331: 
00332: function hasFederalInDraws(draws) {
00333:   return (Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []).some(
00334:     (d) => upTrim(d.lottery_key) === "FEDERAL"
00335:   );
00336: }
00337: 
00338: /* =========================
00339:    REDIRECT / VALIDATION
00340: ========================= */
00341: function redirect308(destPath) {
00342:   return (req, res) => {
00343:     const q = req.url.includes("?") ? req.url.slice(req.url.indexOf("?")) : "";
00344:     return res.redirect(308, `${req.baseUrl}${destPath}${q}`);
00345:   };
00346: }
00347: 
00348: function requireLotteryOr400(lottery, res) {
00349:   if (!lottery) {
00350:     res.status(400).json({
00351:       ok: false,
00352:       error: "Parâmetro inválido: lottery (use RJ/PT_RIO ou FED/FEDERAL)",
00353:     });
00354:     return false;
00355:   }
00356:   return true;
00357: }

00362: router.get("/draws", async (req, res) => {
00363:   try {
00364:     const db = getDb();
00365: 
00366:     const date = String(req.query.date || "").trim();
00367:     const lottery = getLotteryFromQuery(req);
00368: 
00369:     const fromRaw = req.query.from != null ? String(req.query.from).trim() : "";
00370:     const toRaw = req.query.to != null ? String(req.query.to).trim() : "";
00371: 
00372:     const from = fromRaw ? normalizeHHMM(fromRaw) : "";
00373:     const to = toRaw ? normalizeHHMM(toRaw) : "";
00374: 
00375:     const includePrizes = parseIncludePrizes(req.query.includePrizes, true);
00376:     const positionsInfo = parsePositionsParam(req.query.positions);
00377: 
00378:     if (!isISODate(date)) {
00379:       return res.status(400).json({
00380:         ok: false,
00381:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: date (use YYYY-MM-DD)",
00382:       });
00383:     }
00384: 
00385:     if (!requireLotteryOr400(lottery, res)) return;
00386: 
00387:     if (fromRaw && !from) {
00388:       return res.status(400).json({
00389:         ok: false,
00390:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: from (use HH:MM, 10h ou 10)",
00391:       });
00392:     }
00393: 
00394:     if (toRaw && !to) {
00395:       return res.status(400).json({
00396:         ok: false,
00397:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: to (use HH:MM, 10h ou 10)",
00398:       });
00399:     }
00400: 
00401:     const docs = await fetchDayDraws(db, date);
00402:     const rawDraws = docs.map(normalizeDrawDoc);
00403:     const hasFederal = hasFederalInDraws(rawDraws);
00404: 
00405:     const byLottery = applyLotteryFilter(rawDraws, lottery).sort(sortDrawsByHourOnly);
00406:     const drawsWindow = applyHourWindow(byLottery, from || "", to || "");
00407:     const draws = await loadPrizesForDraws(db, drawsWindow, includePrizes, positionsInfo);
00408: 
00409:     const integrity = checkDrawIntegrity(draws, lottery, date, hasFederal);
00410: 
00411:     return res.json({
00412:       ok: true,
00413:       integrity,
00414:       date,
00415:       lottery,
00416:       from: from || null,
00417:       to: to || null,
00418:       includePrizes,
00419:       count: draws.length,
00420:       draws,
00421:     });
00422:   } catch (e) {
00423:     return res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "erro" });
00424:   }
00425: });

00433: async function handleDay(req, res) {
00434:   try {
00435:     const db = getDb();
00436: 
00437:     const date = String(req.query.date || "").trim();
00438:     const lottery = getLotteryFromQuery(req);
00439: 
00440:     if (!requireLotteryOr400(lottery, res)) return;
00441: 
00442:     if (!isISODate(date)) {
00443:       return res.status(400).json({
00444:         ok: false,
00445:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: date (use YYYY-MM-DD)",
00446:       });
00447:     }
00448: 
00449:     const win = getWindowFromQuery(req);
00450:     if (win.error) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: win.error });
00451: 
00452:     const includePrizes = parseIncludePrizes(req.query.includePrizes, true);
00453:     const positionsInfo = parsePositionsParam(req.query.positions);
00454: 
00455:     const docs = await fetchDayDraws(db, date);
00456:     const rawDraws = docs.map(normalizeDrawDoc);
00457:     const hasFederal = hasFederalInDraws(rawDraws);
00458: 
00459:     const byLottery = applyLotteryFilter(rawDraws, lottery).sort(sortDrawsByHourOnly);
00460:     const drawsWindow = applyHourWindow(byLottery, win.from || "", win.to || "");
00461:     const draws = await loadPrizesForDraws(db, drawsWindow, includePrizes, positionsInfo);
00462: 
00463:     const integrity = checkDrawIntegrity(draws, lottery, date, hasFederal);
00464: 
00465:     return res.json({
00466:       ok: true,
00467:       mode: "day",
00468:       integrity,
00469:       date,
00470:       lottery,
00471:       from: win.from || null,
00472:       to: win.to || null,
00473:       includePrizes,
00474:       count: draws.length,
00475:       draws,
00476:     });
00477:   } catch (e) {
00478:     return res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "erro" });
00479:   }
00480: }
00481: 
00482: async function handleRange(req, res) {
00483:   try {
00484:     const db = getDb();
00485: 
00486:     const dateFrom = String(req.query.dateFrom || "").trim();
00487:     const dateTo = String(req.query.dateTo || "").trim();
00488:     const lottery = getLotteryFromQuery(req);
00489: 
00490:     if (!requireLotteryOr400(lottery, res)) return;
00491: 
00492:     if (!isISODate(dateFrom) || !isISODate(dateTo)) {
00493:       return res.status(400).json({
00494:         ok: false,
00495:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: dateFrom/dateTo (use YYYY-MM-DD)",
00496:       });
00497:     }
00498: 
00499:     if (dateFrom > dateTo) {
00500:       return res.status(400).json({
00501:         ok: false,
00502:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: dateFrom não pode ser maior que dateTo",
00503:       });
00504:     }
00505: 
00506:     const win = getWindowFromQuery(req);
00507:     if (win.error) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: win.error });
00508: 
00509:     const includePrizes = parseIncludePrizes(req.query.includePrizes, false);
00510:     const positionsInfo = parsePositionsParam(req.query.positions);
00511: 
00512:     const snap = await db
00513:       .collection("draws")
00514:       .where("ymd", ">=", dateFrom)
00515:       .where("ymd", "<=", dateTo)
00516:       .orderBy("ymd", "asc")
00517:       .get();
00518: 
00519:     const rawDraws = snap.docs.map(normalizeDrawDoc);
00520:     const byLottery = applyLotteryFilter(rawDraws, lottery).sort(sortDrawsByDateThenHour);
00521:     const drawsWindow = applyHourWindow(byLottery, win.from || "", win.to || "");
00522:     const draws = await loadPrizesForDraws(db, drawsWindow, includePrizes, positionsInfo);
00523: 
00524:     const groupedIntegrity = {};
00525:     if (lottery === "PT_RIO") {
00526:       const rawByDay = rawDraws.reduce((acc, d) => {
00527:         const ymd = String(d.ymd || d.date || "").trim();
00528:         if (!ymd) return acc;
00529:         if (!acc[ymd]) acc[ymd] = [];
00530:         acc[ymd].push(d);
00531:         return acc;
00532:       }, {});
00533: 
00534:       const filteredByDay = draws.reduce((acc, d) => {
00535:         const ymd = String(d.ymd || d.date || "").trim();
00536:         if (!ymd) return acc;
00537:         if (!acc[ymd]) acc[ymd] = [];
00538:         acc[ymd].push(d);
00539:         return acc;
00540:       }, {});
00541: 
00542:       const allDays = Array.from(
00543:         new Set([
00544:           ...Object.keys(rawByDay),
00545:           ...Object.keys(filteredByDay),
00546:         ])
00547:       ).sort();
00548: 
00549:       for (const ymd of allDays) {
00550:         const dayRaw = rawByDay[ymd] || [];
00551:         const dayFiltered = filteredByDay[ymd] || [];
00552:         const hasFederal = hasFederalInDraws(dayRaw);
00553:         groupedIntegrity[ymd] = checkDrawIntegrity(dayFiltered, lottery, ymd, hasFederal);
00554:       }
00555:     }
00556: 
00557:     return res.json({
00558:       ok: true,
00559:       mode: "range",
00560:       integrityByDay: groupedIntegrity,
00561:       dateFrom,
00562:       dateTo,
00563:       lottery,
00564:       from: win.from || null,
00565:       to: win.to || null,
00566:       includePrizes,
00567:       count: draws.length,
00568:       draws,
00569:     });
00570:   } catch (e) {
00571:     return res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "erro" });
00572:   }
00573: }
00574: 
00575: router.get("/draws/day", handleDay);
00576: router.get("/draws/range", handleRange);
00577:

00264:       : null;

00268:       .collection("draws")

00520:     const byLottery = applyLotteryFilter(rawDraws, lottery).sort(sortDrawsByDateThenHour);

00530:         acc[ymd].push(d);

00325: function applyHourWindow(draws, from, to) {

00328:     return inRangeHHMM(d.close_hour, from || "", to || "");

00482: async function handleRange(req, res) {
00483:   try {
00484:     const db = getDb();
00485: 
00486:     const dateFrom = String(req.query.dateFrom || "").trim();
00487:     const dateTo = String(req.query.dateTo || "").trim();
00488:     const lottery = getLotteryFromQuery(req);
00489: 
00490:     if (!requireLotteryOr400(lottery, res)) return;
00491: 
00492:     if (!isISODate(dateFrom) || !isISODate(dateTo)) {
00493:       return res.status(400).json({
00494:         ok: false,
00495:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: dateFrom/dateTo (use YYYY-MM-DD)",
00496:       });
00497:     }
00498: 
00499:     if (dateFrom > dateTo) {
00500:       return res.status(400).json({
00501:         ok: false,
00502:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: dateFrom não pode ser maior que dateTo",
00503:       });
00504:     }
00505: 
00506:     const win = getWindowFromQuery(req);
00507:     if (win.error) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: win.error });
00508: 
00509:     const includePrizes = parseIncludePrizes(req.query.includePrizes, false);
00510:     const positionsInfo = parsePositionsParam(req.query.positions);
00511: 
00512:     const snap = await db
00513:       .collection("draws")
00514:       .where("ymd", ">=", dateFrom)
00515:       .where("ymd", "<=", dateTo)
00516:       .orderBy("ymd", "asc")
00517:       .get();
00518: 
00519:     const rawDraws = snap.docs.map(normalizeDrawDoc);
00520:     const byLottery = applyLotteryFilter(rawDraws, lottery).sort(sortDrawsByDateThenHour);
00521:     const drawsWindow = applyHourWindow(byLottery, win.from || "", win.to || "");
00522:     const draws = await loadPrizesForDraws(db, drawsWindow, includePrizes, positionsInfo);
00523: 
00524:     const groupedIntegrity = {};
00525:     if (lottery === "PT_RIO") {
00526:       const rawByDay = rawDraws.reduce((acc, d) => {
00527:         const ymd = String(d.ymd || d.date || "").trim();
00528:         if (!ymd) return acc;
00529:         if (!acc[ymd]) acc[ymd] = [];
00530:         acc[ymd].push(d);
00531:         return acc;
00532:       }, {});
00533: 
00534:       const filteredByDay = draws.reduce((acc, d) => {
00535:         const ymd = String(d.ymd || d.date || "").trim();
00536:         if (!ymd) return acc;
00537:         if (!acc[ymd]) acc[ymd] = [];
00538:         acc[ymd].push(d);
00539:         return acc;
00540:       }, {});
00541: 
00542:       const allDays = Array.from(
00543:         new Set([
00544:           ...Object.keys(rawByDay),
00545:           ...Object.keys(filteredByDay),
00546:         ])
00547:       ).sort();
00548: 
00549:       for (const ymd of allDays) {
00550:         const dayRaw = rawByDay[ymd] || [];
00551:         const dayFiltered = filteredByDay[ymd] || [];
00552:         const hasFederal = hasFederalInDraws(dayRaw);
00553:         groupedIntegrity[ymd] = checkDrawIntegrity(dayFiltered, lottery, ymd, hasFederal);
00554:       }
00555:     }
00556: 
00557:     return res.json({
00558:       ok: true,
00559:       mode: "range",
00560:       integrityByDay: groupedIntegrity,
00561:       dateFrom,
00562:       dateTo,
00563:       lottery,

00570:   } catch (e) {

00572:   }
00573: }

00482: async function handleRange(req, res) {
00483:   try {
00484:     const db = getDb();
00485: 
00486:     const dateFrom = String(req.query.dateFrom || "").trim();
00487:     const dateTo = String(req.query.dateTo || "").trim();
00488:     const lottery = getLotteryFromQuery(req);
00489: 
00490:     if (!requireLotteryOr400(lottery, res)) return;
00491: 
00492:     if (!isISODate(dateFrom) || !isISODate(dateTo)) {
00493:       return res.status(400).json({
00494:         ok: false,
00495:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: dateFrom/dateTo (use YYYY-MM-DD)",
00496:       });
00497:     }
00498: 
00499:     if (dateFrom > dateTo) {
00500:       return res.status(400).json({
00501:         ok: false,
00502:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: dateFrom não pode ser maior que dateTo",
00503:       });
00504:     }
00505: 
00506:     const win = getWindowFromQuery(req);
00507:     if (win.error) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: win.error });
00508: 
00509:     const includePrizes = parseIncludePrizes(req.query.includePrizes, false);
00510:     const positionsInfo = parsePositionsParam(req.query.positions);
00511: 
00512:     const snap = await db
00513:       .collection("draws")
00514:       .where("ymd", ">=", dateFrom)
00515:       .where("ymd", "<=", dateTo)
00516:       .orderBy("ymd", "asc")
00517:       .get();
00518: 
00519:     const rawDraws = snap.docs.map(normalizeDrawDoc);
00520:     const byLottery = applyLotteryFilter(rawDraws, lottery).sort(sortDrawsByDateThenHour);
00521:     const drawsWindow = applyHourWindow(byLottery, win.from || "", win.to || "");
00522:     const draws = await loadPrizesForDraws(db, drawsWindow, includePrizes, positionsInfo);
00523: 
00524:     const groupedIntegrity = {};
00525:     if (lottery === "PT_RIO") {
00526:       const rawByDay = rawDraws.reduce((acc, d) => {
00527:         const ymd = String(d.ymd || d.date || "").trim();
00528:         if (!ymd) return acc;
00529:         if (!acc[ymd]) acc[ymd] = [];
00530:         acc[ymd].push(d);
00531:         return acc;
00532:       }, {});
00533: 
00534:       const filteredByDay = draws.reduce((acc, d) => {
00535:         const ymd = String(d.ymd || d.date || "").trim();
00536:         if (!ymd) return acc;
00537:         if (!acc[ymd]) acc[ymd] = [];
00538:         acc[ymd].push(d);
00539:         return acc;
00540:       }, {});
00541: 
00542:       const allDays = Array.from(
00543:         new Set([
00544:           ...Object.keys(rawByDay),
00545:           ...Object.keys(filteredByDay),
00546:         ])
00547:       ).sort();
00548: 
00549:       for (const ymd of allDays) {
00550:         const dayRaw = rawByDay[ymd] || [];
00551:         const dayFiltered = filteredByDay[ymd] || [];
00552:         const hasFederal = hasFederalInDraws(dayRaw);
00553:         groupedIntegrity[ymd] = checkDrawIntegrity(dayFiltered, lottery, ymd, hasFederal);
00554:       }
00555:     }
00556: 
00557:     return res.json({
00558:       ok: true,
00559:       mode: "range",
00560:       integrityByDay: groupedIntegrity,
00561:       dateFrom,
00562:       dateTo,
00563:       lottery,
00564:       from: win.from || null,
00565:       to: win.to || null,
00566:       includePrizes,
00567:       count: draws.length,
00568:       draws,
00569:     });
00570:   } catch (e) {
00571:     return res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "erro" });
00572:   }
00573: }
00574: 
00575: router.get("/draws/day", handleDay);
00576: router.get("/draws/range", handleRange);

00253: async function loadPrizesForDraws(db, drawsWindow, includePrizes, positionsInfo) {
00254:   if (!includePrizes) return drawsWindow;
00255: 
00256:   const posArr =
00257:     positionsInfo && Array.isArray(positionsInfo.positions)
00258:       ? positionsInfo.positions
00259:       : null;
00260: 
00261:   const posSet =
00262:     posArr && posArr.length
00263:       ? new Set(posArr.map((n) => Number(n)))
00264:       : null;
00265: 
00266:   const draws = await mapWithConcurrency(drawsWindow, 6, async (d) => {
00267:     let q = db
00268:       .collection("draws")
00269:       .doc(d.id)
00270:       .collection("prizes")
00271:       .orderBy("position", "asc");
00272: 
00273:     if (positionsInfo && positionsInfo.maxPos && posArr) {
00274:       const maxPos = Number(positionsInfo.maxPos) || null;
00275:       const contiguousFrom1 =
00276:         maxPos &&
00277:         posArr.length === maxPos &&
00278:         Number(posArr[0]) === 1 &&
00279:         Number(posArr[posArr.length - 1]) === maxPos;
00280: 
00281:       if (contiguousFrom1) q = q.limit(maxPos);
00282:     }
00283: 
00284:     const prizesSnap = await q.get();
00285:     const rows = prizesSnap.docs.map((p) => p.data());
00286:     const prizes = posSet
00287:       ? rows.filter((r) => posSet.has(Number(r.position)))
00288:       : rows;
00289: 
00290:     return { ...d, prizes };
00291:   });
00292: 
00293:   return draws;
00294: }

00312: function sortDrawsByDateThenHour(a, b) {
00313:   const da = String(a.ymd || a.date || "");
00314:   const db = String(b.ymd || b.date || "");
00315:   if (da !== db) return da.localeCompare(db);
00316:   return cmpHHMM(a.close_hour, b.close_hour);
00317: }

00319: function applyLotteryFilter(draws, lottery) {
00320:   return (Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []).filter(
00321:     (d) => upTrim(d.lottery_key) === lottery
00322:   );

00325: function applyHourWindow(draws, from, to) {
00326:   return (Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []).filter((d) => {
00327:     if (!from && !to) return true;
00328:     return inRangeHHMM(d.close_hour, from || "", to || "");
00329:   });
00330: }

00423:     return res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "erro" });
00424:   }
00425: });

00433: async function handleDay(req, res) {
00434:   try {
00435:     const db = getDb();
00436: 
00437:     const date = String(req.query.date || "").trim();
00438:     const lottery = getLotteryFromQuery(req);
00439: 
00440:     if (!requireLotteryOr400(lottery, res)) return;
00441: 
00442:     if (!isISODate(date)) {
00443:       return res.status(400).json({
00444:         ok: false,
00445:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: date (use YYYY-MM-DD)",
00446:       });
00447:     }
00448: 
00449:     const win = getWindowFromQuery(req);
00450:     if (win.error) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: win.error });
00451: 
00452:     const includePrizes = parseIncludePrizes(req.query.includePrizes, true);
00453:     const positionsInfo = parsePositionsParam(req.query.positions);
00454: 
00455:     const docs = await fetchDayDraws(db, date);
00456:     const rawDraws = docs.map(normalizeDrawDoc);
00457:     const hasFederal = hasFederalInDraws(rawDraws);
00458: 
00459:     const byLottery = applyLotteryFilter(rawDraws, lottery).sort(sortDrawsByHourOnly);
00460:     const drawsWindow = applyHourWindow(byLottery, win.from || "", win.to || "");
00461:     const draws = await loadPrizesForDraws(db, drawsWindow, includePrizes, positionsInfo);
00462: 
00463:     const integrity = checkDrawIntegrity(draws, lottery, date, hasFederal);
00464: 
00465:     return res.json({
00466:       ok: true,
00467:       mode: "day",
00468:       integrity,
00469:       date,
00470:       lottery,
00471:       from: win.from || null,
00472:       to: win.to || null,
00473:       includePrizes,
00474:       count: draws.length,
00475:       draws,
00476:     });
00477:   } catch (e) {
00478:     return res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "erro" });
00479:   }
00480: }
00481: 
00482: async function handleRange(req, res) {
00483:   try {
00484:     const db = getDb();
00485: 
00486:     const dateFrom = String(req.query.dateFrom || "").trim();
00487:     const dateTo = String(req.query.dateTo || "").trim();
00488:     const lottery = getLotteryFromQuery(req);
00489: 
00490:     if (!requireLotteryOr400(lottery, res)) return;
00491: 
00492:     if (!isISODate(dateFrom) || !isISODate(dateTo)) {
00493:       return res.status(400).json({
00494:         ok: false,
00495:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: dateFrom/dateTo (use YYYY-MM-DD)",
00496:       });
00497:     }
00498: 
00499:     if (dateFrom > dateTo) {
00500:       return res.status(400).json({
00501:         ok: false,
00502:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: dateFrom não pode ser maior que dateTo",
00503:       });
00504:     }
00505: 
00506:     const win = getWindowFromQuery(req);
00507:     if (win.error) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: win.error });
00508: 
00509:     const includePrizes = parseIncludePrizes(req.query.includePrizes, false);
00510:     const positionsInfo = parsePositionsParam(req.query.positions);
00511: 
00512:     const snap = await db
00513:       .collection("draws")
00514:       .where("ymd", ">=", dateFrom)
00515:       .where("ymd", "<=", dateTo)
00516:       .orderBy("ymd", "asc")
00517:       .get();
00518: 
00519:     const rawDraws = snap.docs.map(normalizeDrawDoc);
00520:     const byLottery = applyLotteryFilter(rawDraws, lottery).sort(sortDrawsByDateThenHour);
00521:     const drawsWindow = applyHourWindow(byLottery, win.from || "", win.to || "");
00522:     const draws = await loadPrizesForDraws(db, drawsWindow, includePrizes, positionsInfo);
00523: 
00524:     const groupedIntegrity = {};
00525:     if (lottery === "PT_RIO") {
00526:       const rawByDay = rawDraws.reduce((acc, d) => {
00527:         const ymd = String(d.ymd || d.date || "").trim();
00528:         if (!ymd) return acc;
00529:         if (!acc[ymd]) acc[ymd] = [];
00530:         acc[ymd].push(d);
00531:         return acc;
00532:       }, {});
00533: 
00534:       const filteredByDay = draws.reduce((acc, d) => {
00535:         const ymd = String(d.ymd || d.date || "").trim();
00536:         if (!ymd) return acc;
00537:         if (!acc[ymd]) acc[ymd] = [];
00538:         acc[ymd].push(d);
00539:         return acc;
00540:       }, {});
00541: 
00542:       const allDays = Array.from(
00543:         new Set([
00544:           ...Object.keys(rawByDay),
00545:           ...Object.keys(filteredByDay),
00546:         ])
00547:       ).sort();
00548: 
00549:       for (const ymd of allDays) {
00550:         const dayRaw = rawByDay[ymd] || [];
00551:         const dayFiltered = filteredByDay[ymd] || [];
00552:         const hasFederal = hasFederalInDraws(dayRaw);
00553:         groupedIntegrity[ymd] = checkDrawIntegrity(dayFiltered, lottery, ymd, hasFederal);
00554:       }
00555:     }
00556: 
00557:     return res.json({
00558:       ok: true,
00559:       mode: "range",
00560:       integrityByDay: groupedIntegrity,
00561:       dateFrom,
00562:       dateTo,
00563:       lottery,
00564:       from: win.from || null,
00565:       to: win.to || null,
00566:       includePrizes,
00567:       count: draws.length,
00568:       draws,
00569:     });
00570:   } catch (e) {
00571:     return res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "erro" });
00572:   }
00573: }
00574: 
00575: router.get("/draws/day", handleDay);
00576: router.get("/draws/range", handleRange);
00577: 
00578: module.exports = router;

00299: function normalizeDrawDoc(doc) {
00300:   const data = doc.data() || {};
00301:   return {
00302:     id: doc.id,
00303:     ...data,
00304:     close_hour: normalizeHHMM(data.close_hour ?? data.close ?? data.hour),
00305:   };
00306: }
00307: 
00308: function sortDrawsByHourOnly(a, b) {
00309:   return cmpHHMM(a.close_hour, b.close_hour);
00310: }
00311: 
00312: function sortDrawsByDateThenHour(a, b) {
00313:   const da = String(a.ymd || a.date || "");
00314:   const db = String(b.ymd || b.date || "");
00315:   if (da !== db) return da.localeCompare(db);
00316:   return cmpHHMM(a.close_hour, b.close_hour);
00317: }
00318: 
00319: function applyLotteryFilter(draws, lottery) {
00320:   return (Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []).filter(
00321:     (d) => upTrim(d.lottery_key) === lottery
00322:   );
00323: }
00324: 
00325: function applyHourWindow(draws, from, to) {
00326:   return (Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []).filter((d) => {
00327:     if (!from && !to) return true;
00328:     return inRangeHHMM(d.close_hour, from || "", to || "");
00329:   });
00330: }
00331: 
00332: function hasFederalInDraws(draws) {
00333:   return (Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []).some(
00334:     (d) => upTrim(d.lottery_key) === "FEDERAL"
00335:   );
00336: }
00337:

00339:    REDIRECT / VALIDATION
00340: ========================= */
00341: function redirect308(destPath) {
00342:   return (req, res) => {
00343:     const q = req.url.includes("?") ? req.url.slice(req.url.indexOf("?")) : "";
00344:     return res.redirect(308, `${req.baseUrl}${destPath}${q}`);
00345:   };
00346: }
00347: 
00348: function requireLotteryOr400(lottery, res) {
00349:   if (!lottery) {
00350:     res.status(400).json({
00351:       ok: false,
00352:       error: "Parâmetro inválido: lottery (use RJ/PT_RIO ou FED/FEDERAL)",
00353:     });
00354:     return false;
00355:   }
00356:   return true;
00357: }

00362: router.get("/draws", async (req, res) => {
00363:   try {
00364:     const db = getDb();
00365: 
00366:     const date = String(req.query.date || "").trim();
00367:     const lottery = getLotteryFromQuery(req);
00368: 
00369:     const fromRaw = req.query.from != null ? String(req.query.from).trim() : "";
00370:     const toRaw = req.query.to != null ? String(req.query.to).trim() : "";
00371: 
00372:     const from = fromRaw ? normalizeHHMM(fromRaw) : "";
00373:     const to = toRaw ? normalizeHHMM(toRaw) : "";
00374: 
00375:     const includePrizes = parseIncludePrizes(req.query.includePrizes, true);
00376:     const positionsInfo = parsePositionsParam(req.query.positions);
00377: 
00378:     if (!isISODate(date)) {
00379:       return res.status(400).json({
00380:         ok: false,
00381:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: date (use YYYY-MM-DD)",
00382:       });
00383:     }
00384: 
00385:     if (!requireLotteryOr400(lottery, res)) return;
00386: 
00387:     if (fromRaw && !from) {
00388:       return res.status(400).json({
00389:         ok: false,
00390:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: from (use HH:MM, 10h ou 10)",
00391:       });
00392:     }
00393: 
00394:     if (toRaw && !to) {
00395:       return res.status(400).json({
00396:         ok: false,
00397:         error: "Parâmetro inválido: to (use HH:MM, 10h ou 10)",
00398:       });
00399:     }
00400: 
00401:     const docs = await fetchDayDraws(db, date);
00402:     const rawDraws = docs.map(normalizeDrawDoc);
00403:     const hasFederal = hasFederalInDraws(rawDraws);
00404: 
00405:     const byLottery = applyLotteryFilter(rawDraws, lottery).sort(sortDrawsByHourOnly);
00406:     const drawsWindow = applyHourWindow(byLottery, from || "", to || "");
00407:     const draws = await loadPrizesForDraws(db, drawsWindow, includePrizes, positionsInfo);
00408: 
00409:     const integrity = checkDrawIntegrity(draws, lottery, date, hasFederal);
00410: 
00411:     return res.json({
00412:       ok: true,
00413:       integrity,
00414:       date,
00415:       lottery,
00416:       from: from || null,
00417:       to: to || null,
00418:       includePrizes,
00419:       count: draws.length,
00420:       draws,
00421:     });

00446: function readGapsMap(lottery, opts) {
00447:   const key = upTrim(lottery || "PT_RIO") || "PT_RIO";
00448:   const reload = !!(opts && opts.reload);
00449: 
00450:   const cached = _gapsCache.get(key);
00451:   if (!reload && cached && Date.now() - cached.loadedAt < GAPS_TTL_MS) {
00452:     return cached.map;
00453:   }
00454: 
00455:   const p = path.join(DEFAULT_GAPS_DIR, `${key}.json`);
00456:   let map = {};
00457:   try {
00458:     if (fs.existsSync(p)) {
00459:       const raw = fs.readFileSync(p, "utf8");
00460:       const j = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : {};
00461:       if (j && typeof j === "object") map = j;
00462:     }
00463:   } catch {
00464:     map = {};
00465:   }
00466: 
00467:   _gapsCache.set(key, { loadedAt: Date.now(), map });
00468:   return map;
00469: }

00471: function normalizeGapEntry(entry) {
00472:   if (Array.isArray(entry)) {
00473:     return {
00474:       removedHard: normalizeHourList(entry),
00475:       removedSoft: [],
00476:     };
00477:   }
