00480:   const hard = obj.removedHard || obj.removeHard || obj.hard || obj.expectedRemovedHard || [];
00481:   const soft = obj.removedSoft || obj.removeSoft || obj.soft || obj.expectedRemovedSoft || [];
00482: 
00483:   return {
00484:     removedHard: normalizeHourList(hard),
00485:     removedSoft: normalizeHourList(soft),
00486:   };
00487: }

00489: function getGapsForDate(lotteryKey, ymd, opts) {

00491:   const entry = map?.gapsByDay?.[ymd] ?? map?.[ymd];

00497: /* =========================

00499: ========================= */

00501: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
00502:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
00503:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 6);
00504:   const results = new Array(arr.length);
00505:   let idx = 0;
00506: 
00507:   async function worker() {
00508:     while (true) {
00509:       const current = idx;
00510:       idx += 1;
00511:       if (current >= arr.length) break;
00512:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
00513:     }
00514:   }
00515: 
00516:   await Promise.all(
00517:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, () => worker())
00518:   );
00519:   return results;
00520: }

00526: function buildCalendarStatus({ slotsSummary, blocked = false, blockedReason = "", isToday = false }) {
00527:   const expected = Number(slotsSummary?.expectedTotal || 0);
00528:   const found = Number(slotsSummary?.foundTotal || 0);
00529:   const missing = Number(slotsSummary?.missingTotal || 0);
00530: 
00531:   let status = "empty";
00532:   if (blocked) status = blockedReason === "future_date" ? "future" : "blocked";
00533:   else if (isToday && missing > 0) status = "pending";
00534:   else if (expected > 0 && missing === 0) status = "complete";
00535:   else if (found > 0 && missing > 0) status = "partial";
00536:   else if (expected > 0 && found === 0) status = "empty";
00537: 
00538:   return {
00539:     status,
00540:     expected,
00541:     found,
00542:     missing,
00543:     completionPct: Number(slotsSummary?.completionPct ?? 0),
00544:   };
00545: }

00547: function buildSlots(opts) {
00548:   const {
00549:     scheduleAll,
00550:     byHour,
00551:     baseSoft,
00552:     removedHardApplied,
00553:     removedSoftApplied,
00554:     expectedHard,
00555:     expectedSoft,
00556:     expectedHardPublished,
00557:     expectedSoftPublished,
00558:     capToday,
00559:     isToday,
00560:     nowMinBR,
00561:     slotGraceMin,
00562:   } = opts || {};
00563: 
00564:   return (Array.isArray(scheduleAll) ? scheduleAll : []).map((hh) => {
00565:     const draw = byHour?.get ? byHour.get(hh) || null : null;
00566: 
00567:     const isSoft = Array.isArray(baseSoft) && baseSoft.includes(hh);
00568:     const kind = isSoft ? "soft" : "hard";
00569: 
00570:     if (removedHardApplied?.has?.(hh) || removedSoftApplied?.has?.(hh)) {
00571:       return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "source_gap", draw: null };
00572:     }
00573: 
00574:     if (draw) return { hour: hh, kind, status: "valid", draw };
00575: 
00576:     if (
00577:       capToday &&
00578:       isToday &&
00579:       ((expectedHard && expectedHard.includes(hh)) || (expectedSoft && expectedSoft.includes(hh)))
00580:     ) {
00581:       const published = isSlotPublishedToday(hh, nowMinBR, slotGraceMin);
00582:       if (!published) {
00583:         return { hour: hh, kind, status: "future", reason: "not_yet_published", draw: null };
00584:       }
00585:     }
00586: 
00587:     if (expectedHardPublished && expectedHardPublished.includes(hh)) {
00588:       return { hour: hh, kind: "hard", status: "missing", draw: null };
00589:     }
00590:     if (expectedSoftPublished && expectedSoftPublished.includes(hh)) {
00591:       return { hour: hh, kind: "soft", status: "soft_missing", draw: null };
00592:     }
00593: 
00594:     return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "not_expected", draw: null };
00595:   });
00596: }

00443: const _gapsCache = new Map();
00444: const GAPS_TTL_MS = 60_000;
00445: 
00446: function readGapsMap(lottery, opts) {
00447:   const key = upTrim(lottery || "PT_RIO") || "PT_RIO";
00448:   const reload = !!(opts && opts.reload);
00449: 
00450:   const cached = _gapsCache.get(key);
00451:   if (!reload && cached && Date.now() - cached.loadedAt < GAPS_TTL_MS) {
00452:     return cached.map;
00453:   }
00454: 
00455:   const p = path.join(DEFAULT_GAPS_DIR, `${key}.json`);
00456:   let map = {};
00457:   try {
00458:     if (fs.existsSync(p)) {
00459:       const raw = fs.readFileSync(p, "utf8");
00460:       const j = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : {};
00461:       if (j && typeof j === "object") map = j;
00462:     }
00463:   } catch {
00464:     map = {};
00465:   }
00466: 
00467:   _gapsCache.set(key, { loadedAt: Date.now(), map });
00468:   return map;
00469: }
00470: 
00471: function normalizeGapEntry(entry) {
00472:   if (Array.isArray(entry)) {
00473:     return {
00474:       removedHard: normalizeHourList(entry),
00475:       removedSoft: [],
00476:     };
00477:   }
00478: 
00479:   const obj = entry && typeof entry === "object" ? entry : {};
00480:   const hard = obj.removedHard || obj.removeHard || obj.hard || obj.expectedRemovedHard || [];
00481:   const soft = obj.removedSoft || obj.removeSoft || obj.soft || obj.expectedRemovedSoft || [];
00482: 
00483:   return {
00484:     removedHard: normalizeHourList(hard),
00485:     removedSoft: normalizeHourList(soft),
00486:   };
00487: }
00488: 
00489: function getGapsForDate(lotteryKey, ymd, opts) {
00490:   const map = readGapsMap(lotteryKey, opts);
00491:   const entry = map?.gapsByDay?.[ymd] ?? map?.[ymd];
00492: 
00493:   if (!entry) return { removedHard: [], removedSoft: [] };
00494:   return normalizeGapEntry(entry);
00495: }
00496: 
00497: /* =========================
00498:    CONCURRENCY
00499: ========================= */
00500: 
00501: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
00502:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
00503:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 6);
00504:   const results = new Array(arr.length);
00505:   let idx = 0;
00506: 
00507:   async function worker() {
00508:     while (true) {
00509:       const current = idx;
00510:       idx += 1;
00511:       if (current >= arr.length) break;
00512:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
00513:     }
00514:   }
00515: 
00516:   await Promise.all(
00517:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, () => worker())
00518:   );
00519:   return results;
00520: }
00521: 
00522: /* =========================
00523:    ROUTE
00524: ========================= */
00525: 
00526: function buildCalendarStatus({ slotsSummary, blocked = false, blockedReason = "", isToday = false }) {
00527:   const expected = Number(slotsSummary?.expectedTotal || 0);
00528:   const found = Number(slotsSummary?.foundTotal || 0);
00529:   const missing = Number(slotsSummary?.missingTotal || 0);
00530: 
00531:   let status = "empty";
00532:   if (blocked) status = blockedReason === "future_date" ? "future" : "blocked";
00533:   else if (isToday && missing > 0) status = "pending";
00534:   else if (expected > 0 && missing === 0) status = "complete";
00535:   else if (found > 0 && missing > 0) status = "partial";
00536:   else if (expected > 0 && found === 0) status = "empty";
00537: 
00538:   return {
00539:     status,
00540:     expected,
00541:     found,
00542:     missing,
00543:     completionPct: Number(slotsSummary?.completionPct ?? 0),
00544:   };
00545: }
00546: 
00547: function buildSlots(opts) {
00548:   const {
00549:     scheduleAll,
00550:     byHour,
00551:     baseSoft,
00552:     removedHardApplied,
00553:     removedSoftApplied,
00554:     expectedHard,
00555:     expectedSoft,
00556:     expectedHardPublished,
00557:     expectedSoftPublished,
00558:     capToday,
00559:     isToday,
00560:     nowMinBR,
00561:     slotGraceMin,
00562:   } = opts || {};
00563: 
00564:   return (Array.isArray(scheduleAll) ? scheduleAll : []).map((hh) => {
00565:     const draw = byHour?.get ? byHour.get(hh) || null : null;
00566: 
00567:     const isSoft = Array.isArray(baseSoft) && baseSoft.includes(hh);
00568:     const kind = isSoft ? "soft" : "hard";
00569: 
00570:     if (removedHardApplied?.has?.(hh) || removedSoftApplied?.has?.(hh)) {
00571:       return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "source_gap", draw: null };
00572:     }
00573: 
00574:     if (draw) return { hour: hh, kind, status: "valid", draw };
00575: 
00576:     if (
00577:       capToday &&
00578:       isToday &&
00579:       ((expectedHard && expectedHard.includes(hh)) || (expectedSoft && expectedSoft.includes(hh)))
00580:     ) {
00581:       const published = isSlotPublishedToday(hh, nowMinBR, slotGraceMin);
00582:       if (!published) {
00583:         return { hour: hh, kind, status: "future", reason: "not_yet_published", draw: null };
00584:       }
00585:     }
00586: 
00587:     if (expectedHardPublished && expectedHardPublished.includes(hh)) {
00588:       return { hour: hh, kind: "hard", status: "missing", draw: null };
00589:     }
00590:     if (expectedSoftPublished && expectedSoftPublished.includes(hh)) {
00591:       return { hour: hh, kind: "soft", status: "soft_missing", draw: null };
00592:     }
00593: 
00594:     return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "not_expected", draw: null };
00595:   });
00596: }

00443: const _gapsCache = new Map();
00444: const GAPS_TTL_MS = 60_000;
00445: 
00446: function readGapsMap(lottery, opts) {
00447:   const key = upTrim(lottery || "PT_RIO") || "PT_RIO";
00448:   const reload = !!(opts && opts.reload);
00449: 
00450:   const cached = _gapsCache.get(key);
00451:   if (!reload && cached && Date.now() - cached.loadedAt < GAPS_TTL_MS) {
00452:     return cached.map;
00453:   }
00454: 
00455:   const p = path.join(DEFAULT_GAPS_DIR, `${key}.json`);
00456:   let map = {};
00457:   try {
00458:     if (fs.existsSync(p)) {
00459:       const raw = fs.readFileSync(p, "utf8");
00460:       const j = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : {};
00461:       if (j && typeof j === "object") map = j;
00462:     }
00463:   } catch {
00464:     map = {};
00465:   }
00466: 
00467:   _gapsCache.set(key, { loadedAt: Date.now(), map });
00468:   return map;
00469: }

00471: function normalizeGapEntry(entry) {
00472:   if (Array.isArray(entry)) {
00473:     return {
00474:       removedHard: normalizeHourList(entry),
00475:       removedSoft: [],
00476:     };
00477:   }
00478: 
00479:   const obj = entry && typeof entry === "object" ? entry : {};
00480:   const hard = obj.removedHard || obj.removeHard || obj.hard || obj.expectedRemovedHard || [];
00481:   const soft = obj.removedSoft || obj.removeSoft || obj.soft || obj.expectedRemovedSoft || [];
00482: 
00483:   return {
00484:     removedHard: normalizeHourList(hard),
00485:     removedSoft: normalizeHourList(soft),
00486:   };
00487: }
00488: 
00489: function getGapsForDate(lotteryKey, ymd, opts) {
00490:   const map = readGapsMap(lotteryKey, opts);
00491:   const entry = map?.gapsByDay?.[ymd] ?? map?.[ymd];
00492: 
00493:   if (!entry) return { removedHard: [], removedSoft: [] };
00494:   return normalizeGapEntry(entry);
00495: }
00496: 
00497: /* =========================
00498:    CONCURRENCY
00499: ========================= */
00500: 
00501: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
00502:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
00503:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 6);
00504:   const results = new Array(arr.length);
00505:   let idx = 0;
00506: 
00507:   async function worker() {
00508:     while (true) {
00509:       const current = idx;
00510:       idx += 1;
00511:       if (current >= arr.length) break;
00512:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
00513:     }
00514:   }
00515: 
00516:   await Promise.all(
00517:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, () => worker())
00518:   );
00519:   return results;
00520: }

00526: function buildCalendarStatus({ slotsSummary, blocked = false, blockedReason = "", isToday = false }) {
00527:   const expected = Number(slotsSummary?.expectedTotal || 0);
00528:   const found = Number(slotsSummary?.foundTotal || 0);
00529:   const missing = Number(slotsSummary?.missingTotal || 0);
00530: 
00531:   let status = "empty";
00532:   if (blocked) status = blockedReason === "future_date" ? "future" : "blocked";
00533:   else if (isToday && missing > 0) status = "pending";
00534:   else if (expected > 0 && missing === 0) status = "complete";
00535:   else if (found > 0 && missing > 0) status = "partial";
00536:   else if (expected > 0 && found === 0) status = "empty";
00537: 
00538:   return {
00539:     status,
00540:     expected,
00541:     found,
00542:     missing,
00543:     completionPct: Number(slotsSummary?.completionPct ?? 0),
00544:   };
00545: }
00546: 
00547: function buildSlots(opts) {
00548:   const {
00549:     scheduleAll,
00550:     byHour,
00551:     baseSoft,
00552:     removedHardApplied,
00553:     removedSoftApplied,
00554:     expectedHard,
00555:     expectedSoft,
00556:     expectedHardPublished,
00557:     expectedSoftPublished,
00558:     capToday,
00559:     isToday,
00560:     nowMinBR,
00561:     slotGraceMin,
00562:   } = opts || {};
00563: 
00564:   return (Array.isArray(scheduleAll) ? scheduleAll : []).map((hh) => {
00565:     const draw = byHour?.get ? byHour.get(hh) || null : null;
00566: 
00567:     const isSoft = Array.isArray(baseSoft) && baseSoft.includes(hh);
00568:     const kind = isSoft ? "soft" : "hard";
00569: 
00570:     if (removedHardApplied?.has?.(hh) || removedSoftApplied?.has?.(hh)) {
00571:       return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "source_gap", draw: null };
00572:     }
00573: 
00574:     if (draw) return { hour: hh, kind, status: "valid", draw };
00575: 
00576:     if (
00577:       capToday &&
00578:       isToday &&
00579:       ((expectedHard && expectedHard.includes(hh)) || (expectedSoft && expectedSoft.includes(hh)))
00580:     ) {
00581:       const published = isSlotPublishedToday(hh, nowMinBR, slotGraceMin);
00582:       if (!published) {
00583:         return { hour: hh, kind, status: "future", reason: "not_yet_published", draw: null };
00584:       }
00585:     }
00586: 
00587:     if (expectedHardPublished && expectedHardPublished.includes(hh)) {
00588:       return { hour: hh, kind: "hard", status: "missing", draw: null };
00589:     }
00590:     if (expectedSoftPublished && expectedSoftPublished.includes(hh)) {
00591:       return { hour: hh, kind: "soft", status: "soft_missing", draw: null };
00592:     }
00593: 
00594:     return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "not_expected", draw: null };
00595:   });
00596: }

00446: function readGapsMap(lottery, opts) {
00447:   const key = upTrim(lottery || "PT_RIO") || "PT_RIO";
00448:   const reload = !!(opts && opts.reload);
00449:

00451:   if (!reload && cached && Date.now() - cached.loadedAt < GAPS_TTL_MS) {
00452:     return cached.map;
00453:   }

00456:   let map = {};

00458:     if (fs.existsSync(p)) {
00459:       const raw = fs.readFileSync(p, "utf8");
00460:       const j = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : {};
00461:       if (j && typeof j === "object") map = j;
00462:     }
00463:   } catch {
00464:     map = {};
00465:   }
00466: 
00467:   _gapsCache.set(key, { loadedAt: Date.now(), map });

00471: function normalizeGapEntry(entry) {
00472:   if (Array.isArray(entry)) {
00473:     return {
00474:       removedHard: normalizeHourList(entry),
00475:       removedSoft: [],
00476:     };
00477:   }
00478: 
00479:   const obj = entry && typeof entry === "object" ? entry : {};
00480:   const hard = obj.removedHard || obj.removeHard || obj.hard || obj.expectedRemovedHard || [];
00481:   const soft = obj.removedSoft || obj.removeSoft || obj.soft || obj.expectedRemovedSoft || [];
00482: 
00483:   return {
00484:     removedHard: normalizeHourList(hard),
00485:     removedSoft: normalizeHourList(soft),
00486:   };
00487: }

00497: /* =========================
00498:    CONCURRENCY
00499: ========================= */
00500: 
00501: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
00502:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
00503:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 6);
00504:   const results = new Array(arr.length);
00505:   let idx = 0;
00506: 
00507:   async function worker() {
00508:     while (true) {
00509:       const current = idx;
00510:       idx += 1;
00511:       if (current >= arr.length) break;
00512:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
00513:     }
00514:   }
00515: 
00516:   await Promise.all(
00517:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, () => worker())
00518:   );
00519:   return results;
00520: }

00526: function buildCalendarStatus({ slotsSummary, blocked = false, blockedReason = "", isToday = false }) {
00527:   const expected = Number(slotsSummary?.expectedTotal || 0);
00528:   const found = Number(slotsSummary?.foundTotal || 0);
00529:   const missing = Number(slotsSummary?.missingTotal || 0);
00530: 
00531:   let status = "empty";
00532:   if (blocked) status = blockedReason === "future_date" ? "future" : "blocked";
00533:   else if (isToday && missing > 0) status = "pending";
00534:   else if (expected > 0 && missing === 0) status = "complete";
00535:   else if (found > 0 && missing > 0) status = "partial";
00536:   else if (expected > 0 && found === 0) status = "empty";
00537: 
00538:   return {
00539:     status,
00540:     expected,
00541:     found,
00542:     missing,
00543:     completionPct: Number(slotsSummary?.completionPct ?? 0),
00544:   };
00545: }
00546: 
00547: function buildSlots(opts) {
00548:   const {
00549:     scheduleAll,
00550:     byHour,
00551:     baseSoft,
00552:     removedHardApplied,
00553:     removedSoftApplied,
00554:     expectedHard,
00555:     expectedSoft,
00556:     expectedHardPublished,
00557:     expectedSoftPublished,
00558:     capToday,
00559:     isToday,
00560:     nowMinBR,
00561:     slotGraceMin,
00562:   } = opts || {};
00563: 
00564:   return (Array.isArray(scheduleAll) ? scheduleAll : []).map((hh) => {
00565:     const draw = byHour?.get ? byHour.get(hh) || null : null;
00566: 
00567:     const isSoft = Array.isArray(baseSoft) && baseSoft.includes(hh);
00568:     const kind = isSoft ? "soft" : "hard";
00569: 
00570:     if (removedHardApplied?.has?.(hh) || removedSoftApplied?.has?.(hh)) {
00571:       return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "source_gap", draw: null };
00572:     }
00573: 
00574:     if (draw) return { hour: hh, kind, status: "valid", draw };
00575: 
00576:     if (
00577:       capToday &&
00578:       isToday &&
00579:       ((expectedHard && expectedHard.includes(hh)) || (expectedSoft && expectedSoft.includes(hh)))
00580:     ) {
00581:       const published = isSlotPublishedToday(hh, nowMinBR, slotGraceMin);
00582:       if (!published) {
00583:         return { hour: hh, kind, status: "future", reason: "not_yet_published", draw: null };
00584:       }
00585:     }
00586: 
00587:     if (expectedHardPublished && expectedHardPublished.includes(hh)) {
00588:       return { hour: hh, kind: "hard", status: "missing", draw: null };
00589:     }
00590:     if (expectedSoftPublished && expectedSoftPublished.includes(hh)) {
00591:       return { hour: hh, kind: "soft", status: "soft_missing", draw: null };
00592:     }
00593: 
00594:     return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "not_expected", draw: null };
00595:   });
00596: }

00534:   else if (expected > 0 && missing === 0) status = "complete";
00535:   else if (found > 0 && missing > 0) status = "partial";
00536:   else if (expected > 0 && found === 0) status = "empty";
00537:

00539:     status,

00547: function buildSlots(opts) {
00548:   const {
00549:     scheduleAll,
00550:     byHour,
00551:     baseSoft,
00552:     removedHardApplied,
00553:     removedSoftApplied,
00554:     expectedHard,
00555:     expectedSoft,
00556:     expectedHardPublished,
00557:     expectedSoftPublished,
00558:     capToday,
00559:     isToday,
00560:     nowMinBR,
00561:     slotGraceMin,
00562:   } = opts || {};
00563: 
00564:   return (Array.isArray(scheduleAll) ? scheduleAll : []).map((hh) => {
00565:     const draw = byHour?.get ? byHour.get(hh) || null : null;
00566: 
00567:     const isSoft = Array.isArray(baseSoft) && baseSoft.includes(hh);
00568:     const kind = isSoft ? "soft" : "hard";
00569: 
00570:     if (removedHardApplied?.has?.(hh) || removedSoftApplied?.has?.(hh)) {
00571:       return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "source_gap", draw: null };
00572:     }
00573: 
00574:     if (draw) return { hour: hh, kind, status: "valid", draw };
00575: 
00576:     if (
00577:       capToday &&
00578:       isToday &&
00579:       ((expectedHard && expectedHard.includes(hh)) || (expectedSoft && expectedSoft.includes(hh)))
00580:     ) {
00581:       const published = isSlotPublishedToday(hh, nowMinBR, slotGraceMin);
00582:       if (!published) {
00583:         return { hour: hh, kind, status: "future", reason: "not_yet_published", draw: null };
00584:       }
00585:     }
00586: 
00587:     if (expectedHardPublished && expectedHardPublished.includes(hh)) {
00588:       return { hour: hh, kind: "hard", status: "missing", draw: null };
00589:     }
00590:     if (expectedSoftPublished && expectedSoftPublished.includes(hh)) {
00591:       return { hour: hh, kind: "soft", status: "soft_missing", draw: null };
00592:     }
00593: 
00594:     return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "not_expected", draw: null };
00595:   });

00471: function normalizeGapEntry(entry) {
00472:   if (Array.isArray(entry)) {
00473:     return {
00474:       removedHard: normalizeHourList(entry),
00475:       removedSoft: [],
00476:     };
00477:   }
00478: 
00479:   const obj = entry && typeof entry === "object" ? entry : {};
00480:   const hard = obj.removedHard || obj.removeHard || obj.hard || obj.expectedRemovedHard || [];
00481:   const soft = obj.removedSoft || obj.removeSoft || obj.soft || obj.expectedRemovedSoft || [];
00482: 
00483:   return {
00484:     removedHard: normalizeHourList(hard),
00485:     removedSoft: normalizeHourList(soft),
00486:   };
00487: }
00488: 
00489: function getGapsForDate(lotteryKey, ymd, opts) {
00490:   const map = readGapsMap(lotteryKey, opts);
00491:   const entry = map?.gapsByDay?.[ymd] ?? map?.[ymd];
00492: 
00493:   if (!entry) return { removedHard: [], removedSoft: [] };
00494:   return normalizeGapEntry(entry);
00495: }

00497: /* =========================
00498:    CONCURRENCY
00499: ========================= */
00500: 
00501: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
00502:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
00503:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 6);
00504:   const results = new Array(arr.length);
00505:   let idx = 0;
00506: 
00507:   async function worker() {
00508:     while (true) {
00509:       const current = idx;
00510:       idx += 1;
00511:       if (current >= arr.length) break;
00512:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
00513:     }
00514:   }
00515: 
00516:   await Promise.all(
00517:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, () => worker())
00518:   );
00519:   return results;
00520: }
00521:

00523:    ROUTE
00524: ========================= */
00525: 
00526: function buildCalendarStatus({ slotsSummary, blocked = false, blockedReason = "", isToday = false }) {
00527:   const expected = Number(slotsSummary?.expectedTotal || 0);
00528:   const found = Number(slotsSummary?.foundTotal || 0);
00529:   const missing = Number(slotsSummary?.missingTotal || 0);
00530: 
00531:   let status = "empty";
00532:   if (blocked) status = blockedReason === "future_date" ? "future" : "blocked";
00533:   else if (isToday && missing > 0) status = "pending";
00534:   else if (expected > 0 && missing === 0) status = "complete";
00535:   else if (found > 0 && missing > 0) status = "partial";
00536:   else if (expected > 0 && found === 0) status = "empty";
00537: 
00538:   return {
00539:     status,
00540:     expected,
00541:     found,
00542:     missing,
00543:     completionPct: Number(slotsSummary?.completionPct ?? 0),
00544:   };
00545: }

00547: function buildSlots(opts) {
00548:   const {
00549:     scheduleAll,
00550:     byHour,
00551:     baseSoft,
00552:     removedHardApplied,
00553:     removedSoftApplied,
00554:     expectedHard,
00555:     expectedSoft,
00556:     expectedHardPublished,
00557:     expectedSoftPublished,
00558:     capToday,
00559:     isToday,
00560:     nowMinBR,
00561:     slotGraceMin,
00562:   } = opts || {};
00563: 
00564:   return (Array.isArray(scheduleAll) ? scheduleAll : []).map((hh) => {
00565:     const draw = byHour?.get ? byHour.get(hh) || null : null;
00566: 
00567:     const isSoft = Array.isArray(baseSoft) && baseSoft.includes(hh);
00568:     const kind = isSoft ? "soft" : "hard";
00569: 
00570:     if (removedHardApplied?.has?.(hh) || removedSoftApplied?.has?.(hh)) {
00571:       return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "source_gap", draw: null };
00572:     }
00573: 
00574:     if (draw) return { hour: hh, kind, status: "valid", draw };
00575: 
00576:     if (
00577:       capToday &&
00578:       isToday &&
00579:       ((expectedHard && expectedHard.includes(hh)) || (expectedSoft && expectedSoft.includes(hh)))
00580:     ) {
00581:       const published = isSlotPublishedToday(hh, nowMinBR, slotGraceMin);
00582:       if (!published) {
00583:         return { hour: hh, kind, status: "future", reason: "not_yet_published", draw: null };
00584:       }
00585:     }
00586: 
00587:     if (expectedHardPublished && expectedHardPublished.includes(hh)) {
00588:       return { hour: hh, kind: "hard", status: "missing", draw: null };
00589:     }
00590:     if (expectedSoftPublished && expectedSoftPublished.includes(hh)) {
00591:       return { hour: hh, kind: "soft", status: "soft_missing", draw: null };
00592:     }
00593: 
00594:     return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "not_expected", draw: null };
00595:   });
00596: }

00489: function getGapsForDate(lotteryKey, ymd, opts) {
00490:   const map = readGapsMap(lotteryKey, opts);
00491:   const entry = map?.gapsByDay?.[ymd] ?? map?.[ymd];
00492: 
00493:   if (!entry) return { removedHard: [], removedSoft: [] };
00494:   return normalizeGapEntry(entry);
00495: }

00497: /* =========================
00498:    CONCURRENCY
00499: ========================= */
00500: 
00501: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
00502:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
00503:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 6);
00504:   const results = new Array(arr.length);
00505:   let idx = 0;
00506: 
00507:   async function worker() {
00508:     while (true) {
00509:       const current = idx;
00510:       idx += 1;
00511:       if (current >= arr.length) break;
00512:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
00513:     }
00514:   }
00515: 
00516:   await Promise.all(
00517:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, () => worker())
00518:   );
00519:   return results;
00520: }
00521: 
00522: /* =========================
00523:    ROUTE
00524: ========================= */
00525: 
00526: function buildCalendarStatus({ slotsSummary, blocked = false, blockedReason = "", isToday = false }) {
00527:   const expected = Number(slotsSummary?.expectedTotal || 0);
00528:   const found = Number(slotsSummary?.foundTotal || 0);
00529:   const missing = Number(slotsSummary?.missingTotal || 0);
00530: 
00531:   let status = "empty";
00532:   if (blocked) status = blockedReason === "future_date" ? "future" : "blocked";
00533:   else if (isToday && missing > 0) status = "pending";
00534:   else if (expected > 0 && missing === 0) status = "complete";
00535:   else if (found > 0 && missing > 0) status = "partial";
00536:   else if (expected > 0 && found === 0) status = "empty";
00537: 
00538:   return {
00539:     status,
00540:     expected,
00541:     found,
00542:     missing,
00543:     completionPct: Number(slotsSummary?.completionPct ?? 0),
00544:   };
00545: }
00546: 
00547: function buildSlots(opts) {
00548:   const {
00549:     scheduleAll,
00550:     byHour,
00551:     baseSoft,
00552:     removedHardApplied,
00553:     removedSoftApplied,
00554:     expectedHard,
00555:     expectedSoft,
00556:     expectedHardPublished,
00557:     expectedSoftPublished,
00558:     capToday,
00559:     isToday,
00560:     nowMinBR,
00561:     slotGraceMin,
00562:   } = opts || {};
00563: 
00564:   return (Array.isArray(scheduleAll) ? scheduleAll : []).map((hh) => {
00565:     const draw = byHour?.get ? byHour.get(hh) || null : null;
00566: 
00567:     const isSoft = Array.isArray(baseSoft) && baseSoft.includes(hh);
00568:     const kind = isSoft ? "soft" : "hard";
00569: 
00570:     if (removedHardApplied?.has?.(hh) || removedSoftApplied?.has?.(hh)) {
00571:       return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "source_gap", draw: null };
00572:     }
00573: 
00574:     if (draw) return { hour: hh, kind, status: "valid", draw };
00575: 
00576:     if (
00577:       capToday &&
00578:       isToday &&
00579:       ((expectedHard && expectedHard.includes(hh)) || (expectedSoft && expectedSoft.includes(hh)))
00580:     ) {
00581:       const published = isSlotPublishedToday(hh, nowMinBR, slotGraceMin);
00582:       if (!published) {
00583:         return { hour: hh, kind, status: "future", reason: "not_yet_published", draw: null };
00584:       }
00585:     }
00586: 
00587:     if (expectedHardPublished && expectedHardPublished.includes(hh)) {
00588:       return { hour: hh, kind: "hard", status: "missing", draw: null };
00589:     }
00590:     if (expectedSoftPublished && expectedSoftPublished.includes(hh)) {
00591:       return { hour: hh, kind: "soft", status: "soft_missing", draw: null };
00592:     }
00593: 
00594:     return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "not_expected", draw: null };
00595:   });
00596: }

00527:   const expected = Number(slotsSummary?.expectedTotal || 0);

00531:   let status = "empty";
00532:   if (blocked) status = blockedReason === "future_date" ? "future" : "blocked";
00533:   else if (isToday && missing > 0) status = "pending";
00534:   else if (expected > 0 && missing === 0) status = "complete";
00535:   else if (found > 0 && missing > 0) status = "partial";
00536:   else if (expected > 0 && found === 0) status = "empty";
00537: 
00538:   return {
00539:     status,
00540:     expected,
00541:     found,
00542:     missing,
00543:     completionPct: Number(slotsSummary?.completionPct ?? 0),
00544:   };

00547: function buildSlots(opts) {
00548:   const {
00549:     scheduleAll,
00550:     byHour,
00551:     baseSoft,
00552:     removedHardApplied,
00553:     removedSoftApplied,
00554:     expectedHard,
00555:     expectedSoft,
00556:     expectedHardPublished,
00557:     expectedSoftPublished,
00558:     capToday,
00559:     isToday,
00560:     nowMinBR,
00561:     slotGraceMin,
00562:   } = opts || {};
00563: 
00564:   return (Array.isArray(scheduleAll) ? scheduleAll : []).map((hh) => {
00565:     const draw = byHour?.get ? byHour.get(hh) || null : null;
00566: 
00567:     const isSoft = Array.isArray(baseSoft) && baseSoft.includes(hh);
00568:     const kind = isSoft ? "soft" : "hard";
00569: 
00570:     if (removedHardApplied?.has?.(hh) || removedSoftApplied?.has?.(hh)) {
00571:       return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "source_gap", draw: null };
00572:     }
00573: 
00574:     if (draw) return { hour: hh, kind, status: "valid", draw };
00575: 
00576:     if (
00577:       capToday &&
00578:       isToday &&
00579:       ((expectedHard && expectedHard.includes(hh)) || (expectedSoft && expectedSoft.includes(hh)))
00580:     ) {
00581:       const published = isSlotPublishedToday(hh, nowMinBR, slotGraceMin);
00582:       if (!published) {
00583:         return { hour: hh, kind, status: "future", reason: "not_yet_published", draw: null };
00584:       }
00585:     }
00586: 
00587:     if (expectedHardPublished && expectedHardPublished.includes(hh)) {
00588:       return { hour: hh, kind: "hard", status: "missing", draw: null };
00589:     }
00590:     if (expectedSoftPublished && expectedSoftPublished.includes(hh)) {
00591:       return { hour: hh, kind: "soft", status: "soft_missing", draw: null };
00592:     }
00593: 
00594:     return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "not_expected", draw: null };
00595:   });
00596: }

00547: function buildSlots(opts) {
00548:   const {
00549:     scheduleAll,
00550:     byHour,
00551:     baseSoft,
00552:     removedHardApplied,
00553:     removedSoftApplied,
00554:     expectedHard,
00555:     expectedSoft,
00556:     expectedHardPublished,
00557:     expectedSoftPublished,
00558:     capToday,
00559:     isToday,
00560:     nowMinBR,
00561:     slotGraceMin,
00562:   } = opts || {};
00563: 
00564:   return (Array.isArray(scheduleAll) ? scheduleAll : []).map((hh) => {
00565:     const draw = byHour?.get ? byHour.get(hh) || null : null;
00566: 
00567:     const isSoft = Array.isArray(baseSoft) && baseSoft.includes(hh);
00568:     const kind = isSoft ? "soft" : "hard";
00569: 
00570:     if (removedHardApplied?.has?.(hh) || removedSoftApplied?.has?.(hh)) {
00571:       return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "source_gap", draw: null };
00572:     }
00573: 
00574:     if (draw) return { hour: hh, kind, status: "valid", draw };
00575: 
00576:     if (
00577:       capToday &&
00578:       isToday &&
00579:       ((expectedHard && expectedHard.includes(hh)) || (expectedSoft && expectedSoft.includes(hh)))
00580:     ) {
00581:       const published = isSlotPublishedToday(hh, nowMinBR, slotGraceMin);
00582:       if (!published) {
00583:         return { hour: hh, kind, status: "future", reason: "not_yet_published", draw: null };
00584:       }
00585:     }
00586: 
00587:     if (expectedHardPublished && expectedHardPublished.includes(hh)) {
00588:       return { hour: hh, kind: "hard", status: "missing", draw: null };
00589:     }
00590:     if (expectedSoftPublished && expectedSoftPublished.includes(hh)) {
00591:       return { hour: hh, kind: "soft", status: "soft_missing", draw: null };
00592:     }
00593: 
00594:     return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "not_expected", draw: null };
00595:   });
00596: }

00546: 
00547: function buildSlots(opts) {
00548:   const {
00549:     scheduleAll,
00550:     byHour,
00551:     baseSoft,
00552:     removedHardApplied,
00553:     removedSoftApplied,
00554:     expectedHard,
00555:     expectedSoft,
00556:     expectedHardPublished,
00557:     expectedSoftPublished,
00558:     capToday,
00559:     isToday,
00560:     nowMinBR,
00561:     slotGraceMin,
00562:   } = opts || {};
00563: 
00564:   return (Array.isArray(scheduleAll) ? scheduleAll : []).map((hh) => {
00565:     const draw = byHour?.get ? byHour.get(hh) || null : null;
00566: 
00567:     const isSoft = Array.isArray(baseSoft) && baseSoft.includes(hh);
00568:     const kind = isSoft ? "soft" : "hard";
00569: 
00570:     if (removedHardApplied?.has?.(hh) || removedSoftApplied?.has?.(hh)) {
00571:       return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "source_gap", draw: null };
00572:     }
00573: 
00574:     if (draw) return { hour: hh, kind, status: "valid", draw };
00575: 
00576:     if (
00577:       capToday &&
00578:       isToday &&
00579:       ((expectedHard && expectedHard.includes(hh)) || (expectedSoft && expectedSoft.includes(hh)))
00580:     ) {
00581:       const published = isSlotPublishedToday(hh, nowMinBR, slotGraceMin);
00582:       if (!published) {
00583:         return { hour: hh, kind, status: "future", reason: "not_yet_published", draw: null };
00584:       }
00585:     }
00586: 
00587:     if (expectedHardPublished && expectedHardPublished.includes(hh)) {
00588:       return { hour: hh, kind: "hard", status: "missing", draw: null };
00589:     }
00590:     if (expectedSoftPublished && expectedSoftPublished.includes(hh)) {
00591:       return { hour: hh, kind: "soft", status: "soft_missing", draw: null };
00592:     }
00593: 
00594:     return { hour: hh, kind, status: "gap", reason: "not_expected", draw: null };
00595:   });
00596: }

00001: "use strict";
00002: 
00003: const { admin, getDb } = require("../service/firebaseAdmin");
00004: 
00005: function pad2(n) { return String(n).padStart(2, "0"); }
00006: 
00007: /**
00008:  * Formata Date -> YYYY-MM-DD no timezone informado (padrão: America/Sao_Paulo).
00009:  * Evita “virada de dia” quando o servidor roda em UTC mas o dado é local (BRT).
00010:  */
00011: function formatDateToYMDInTZ(date, timeZone = "America/Sao_Paulo") {
00012:   if (!(date instanceof Date) || Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return null;
00013: 
00014:   // formatToParts => { year, month, day } no TZ pedido
00015:   const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", {
00016:     timeZone,
00017:     year: "numeric",
00018:     month: "2-digit",
00019:     day: "2-digit",
00020:   }).formatToParts(date);
00021: 
00022:   const map = Object.fromEntries(parts.map((p) => [p.type, p.value]));
00023:   const y = map.year, m = map.month, d = map.day;
00024:   if (!y || !m || !d) return null;
00025:   return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
00026: }
00027: 
00028: function isISODateStrict(s) {
00029:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
00030:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(str)) return false;
00031:   const [y, m, d] = str.split("-").map(Number);
00032:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
00033:   return dt.getUTCFullYear() === y && dt.getUTCMonth() === m - 1 && dt.getUTCDate() === d;
00034: }
00035: 
00036: function normalizeToYMD(input, timeZone = "America/Sao_Paulo") {
00037:   if (!input) return null;
00038: 
00039:   // Firestore Timestamp (admin.firestore.Timestamp)
00040:   if (typeof input === "object" && typeof input.toDate === "function") {
00041:     const d = input.toDate();
00042:     const ymd = formatDateToYMDInTZ(d, timeZone);
00043:     if (ymd) return ymd;
00044:   }
00045: 
00046:   // objetos {seconds} / {_seconds}
00047:   if (
00048:     typeof input === "object" &&
00049:     (Number.isFinite(Number(input.seconds)) || Number.isFinite(Number(input._seconds)))
00050:   ) {
00051:     const sec = Number.isFinite(Number(input.seconds)) ? Number(input.seconds) : Number(input._seconds);
00052:     const d = new Date(sec * 1000);
00053:     const ymd = formatDateToYMDInTZ(d, timeZone);
00054:     if (ymd) return ymd;
00055:   }
00056: 
00057:   // Date
00058:   if (input instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(input.getTime())) {
00059:     const ymd = formatDateToYMDInTZ(input, timeZone);
00060:     if (ymd) return ymd;
00061:   }
00062: 
00063:   // strings
00064:   const s = String(input || "").trim();
00065:   if (!s) return null;
00066: 
00067:   // ISO com ou sem hora: pega só YYYY-MM-DD do começo
00068:   const iso = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/);
00069:   if (iso) return `${iso[1]}-${iso[2]}-${iso[3]}`;
00070: 
00071:   // BR: DD/MM/YYYY
00072:   const br = s.match(/^(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{4})$/);
00073:   if (br) return `${br[3]}-${br[2]}-${br[1]}`;
00074: 
00075:   return null;
00076: }
00077: 
00078: function normalizeHourLike(v) {
00079:   if (v == null) return null;
00080:   const s = String(v).trim();
00081:   if (!s) return null;
00082:   const m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:[:hH]?(\d{2}))?/);
00083:   if (!m) return s;
00084:   const hh = String(m[1]).padStart(2, "0");
00085:   const mm = String(m[2] || "00").padStart(2, "0");
00086:   return `${hh}:${mm}`;
00087: }
00088: 
00089: function isIndexError(err) {
00090:   const msg = String(err?.message || "").toLowerCase();
00091:   const code = String(err?.code || "").toLowerCase();
00092:   return (
00093:     code.includes("failed-precondition") ||
00094:     msg.includes("failed_precondition") ||
00095:     (msg.includes("index") && msg.includes("create")) ||
00096:     (msg.includes("requires") && msg.includes("index"))
00097:   );
00098: }
00099: 
00100: function extractYmdAndHour(docData) {
00101:   const y =
00102:     docData?.ymd ||
00103:     normalizeToYMD(
00104:       docData?.date ??
00105:       docData?.draw_date ??
00106:       docData?.close_date ??
00107:       docData?.dt ??
00108:       docData?.data
00109:     );
00110: 
00111:   const h = normalizeHourLike(
00112:     docData?.close_hour ??
00113:     docData?.closeHour ??
00114:     docData?.close_hour_raw ??
00115:     docData?.hour ??
00116:     docData?.hora
00117:   );
00118: 
00119:   const ymd = y && isISODateStrict(y) ? y : null;
00120:   const hour = h || null;
00121:   return { ymd, hour };
00122: }
00123: 
00124: function auditCountsFromDocs(rows) {
00125:   const setYmd = new Set();
00126:   const setYmdHour = new Set();
00127:   const mapKeyCount = new Map();
00128:   let invalidYmd = 0;
00129: 
00130:   for (const r of rows) {
00131:     const { ymd, hour } = extractYmdAndHour(r);
00132:     if (!ymd) { invalidYmd++; continue; }
00133:     setYmd.add(ymd);
00134: 
00135:     const key = `${ymd}__${hour || ""}`;
00136:     mapKeyCount.set(key, (mapKeyCount.get(key) || 0) + 1);
00137:     if (hour) setYmdHour.add(key);
00138:   }
00139: 
00140:   const dupKeys = [...mapKeyCount.entries()]
00141:     .filter(([, n]) => n > 1)
00142:     .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);
00143: 
00144:   return {
00145:     docs_brutos: rows.length,
00146:     unique_ymd: setYmd.size,
00147:     unique_ymd_hour: setYmdHour.size,
00148:     invalid_ymd: invalidYmd,
00149:     keys_total: mapKeyCount.size,
00150:     keys_duplicadas: dupKeys.length,
00151:     top_duplicadas: dupKeys.slice(0, 15),
00152:   };
00153: }
00154: 
00155: async function fetchRowsForRange(db, { lotteryKey, fromYmd, toYmd, pageSize = 2000, maxPages = 80 } = {}) {
00156:   const lot = lotteryKey ? String(lotteryKey).trim().toUpperCase() : null;
00157: 
00158:   // 1) tentativa indexada (aceita range parcial)
00159:   try {
00160:     let q = db.collection("draws");
00161:     if (lot) q = q.where("lottery_key", "==", lot);
00162:     if (fromYmd) q = q.where("ymd", ">=", fromYmd);
00163:     if (toYmd) q = q.where("ymd", "<=", toYmd);
00164: 
00165:     const snap = await q.get();
00166:     const rows = snap.docs.map((d) => ({ id: d.id, ...(d.data() || {}) }));
00167:     return { rows, source: "query: where(lottery_key)+where(ymd>=/<=)", usedFallback: false };
00168:   } catch (e) {
00169:     if (!isIndexError(e)) throw e;
00170:   }
00171: 
00172:   // 2) fallback sem índice: pagina por docId e filtra em memória
00173:   const DOC_ID = admin.firestore.FieldPath.documentId();
00174:   let q = db.collection("draws");
00175:   if (lot) q = q.where("lottery_key", "==", lot);
00176:   q = q.orderBy(DOC_ID, "asc");
00177: 
00178:   let last = null;
00179:   const out = [];
00180: 
00181:   for (let page = 1; page <= maxPages; page++) {
00182:     let qq = q.limit(pageSize);
00183:     if (last) qq = qq.startAfter(last);
00184: 
00185:     const snap = await qq.get();
00186:     if (snap.empty) break;
00187: 
00188:     for (const doc of snap.docs) {
00189:       const data = doc.data() || {};
00190:       const ymd = normalizeToYMD(data.ymd ?? data.date ?? data.draw_date ?? data.close_date ?? data.dt ?? data.data);
00191:       if (!ymd || !isISODateStrict(ymd)) continue;
00192:       if (fromYmd && ymd < fromYmd) continue;
00193:       if (toYmd && ymd > toYmd) continue;
00194:       out.push({ id: doc.id, ...data });
00195:     }
00196: 
00197:     last = snap.docs[snap.docs.length - 1];
00198:   }
00199: 
00200:   return { rows: out, source: "fallback: paginate by docId + filter(ymd)", usedFallback: true };
00201: }
00202: 
00203: function parseArg(name, def = null) {
00204:   const i = process.argv.findIndex((x) => x === name);
00205:   if (i >= 0 && process.argv[i + 1] != null) return process.argv[i + 1];
00206:   return def;
00207: }
00208: 
00209: async function main() {
00210:   const db = getDb();
00211: 
00212:   const lot = parseArg("--lot", "PT_RIO");
00213:   const fromArg = parseArg("--from", null);
00214:   const toArg = parseArg("--to", null);
00215: 
00216:   const pageSize = Math.max(200, Number(parseArg("--page", "2000")) || 2000);
00217:   const maxPages = Math.max(1, Number(parseArg("--maxPages", "120")) || 120);
00218: 
00219:   const lotteryKey = lot ? String(lot).trim().toUpperCase() : null;
00220: 
00221:   const fromYmd = fromArg && isISODateStrict(fromArg) ? fromArg : null;
00222:   const toYmd = toArg && isISODateStrict(toArg) ? toArg : null;
00223: 
00224:   const fr = await fetchRowsForRange(db, { lotteryKey, fromYmd, toYmd, pageSize, maxPages });
00225:   const audit = auditCountsFromDocs(fr.rows);
00226: 
00227:   console.log("==================================");
00228:   console.log(`[AUDIT] draws (lottery_key=${lotteryKey || "ALL"})`);
00229:   console.log(`range: ${fromYmd || "N/A"} -> ${toYmd || "N/A"}`);
00230:   console.log(`fetch_source: ${fr.source}${fr.usedFallback ? " (NO-INDEX fallback)" : ""}`);
00231:   console.log("----------------------------------");
00232:   console.log(`docs_brutos: ${audit.docs_brutos}`);
00233:   console.log(`unique_ymd: ${audit.unique_ymd}`);
00234:   console.log(`unique(ymd+hora): ${audit.unique_ymd_hour}`);
00235:   console.log(`invalid_ymd: ${audit.invalid_ymd}`);
00236:   console.log(`keys_total: ${audit.keys_total}`);
00237:   console.log(`keys_duplicadas: ${audit.keys_duplicadas}`);
00238:   console.log("top_duplicadas(ymd__hora => n):");
00239:   audit.top_duplicadas.forEach(([k, n]) => console.log(`  ${k} => ${n}`));
00240:   console.log("==================================");
00241: }
00242: 
00243: main().catch((e) => {
00244:   console.error("ERRO:", e?.stack || e?.message || e);
00245:   process.exit(1);
00246: });

00001: "use strict";
00002: 
00003: const fs = require("fs");
00004: const path = require("path");
00005: const { db } = require("../service/firebaseAdmin");
00006: const { fetchKingResults } = require("./importKingApostas");
00007: 
00008: const START = process.argv[2] || "2022-06-07";
00009: const END = process.argv[3] || "2026-07-08";
00010: const LOTTERY_KEY = "FEDERAL";
00011: 
00012: function pad2(n) {
00013:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
00014: }
00015: 
00016: function addDays(ymd, days) {
00017:   const d = new Date(`${ymd}T00:00:00Z`);
00018:   d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() + days);
00019:   return `${d.getUTCFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getUTCMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(d.getUTCDate())}`;
00020: }
00021: 
00022: function normalizeHHMM(value) {
00023:   const s = String(value ?? "").trim();
00024:   if (/^\d{2}:\d{2}$/.test(s)) return s;
00025: 
00026:   const m1 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})h$/i);
00027:   if (m1) return `${String(m1[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
00028: 
00029:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
00030:   if (m2) return `${String(m2[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
00031: 
00032:   const m3 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})$/);
00033:   if (m3) return `${String(m3[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(m3[2]).padStart(2, "0")}`;
00034: 
00035:   return "";
00036: }
00037: 
00038: function normalizeCloseHourForFederal(value) {
00039:   const raw = normalizeHHMM(value);
00040:   if (!raw) return "";
00041:   return `${raw.slice(0, 2)}:00`;
00042: }
00043: 
00044: function normMilhar(v) {
00045:   const s = String(v ?? "").replace(/\D/g, "").trim();
00046:   if (!s) return "";
00047:   return s.slice(-4).padStart(4, "0");
00048: }
00049: 
00050: function extractKingPrizes(draw) {
00051:   const out = {};
00052:   for (let i = 1; i <= 7; i++) {
00053:     const v = normMilhar(draw?.[`prize_${i}`]);
00054:     if (v) out[i] = v;
00055:   }
00056:   return out;
00057: }
00058: 
00059: async function readFirestoreByDate(date) {
00060:   const snap = await db.collection("draws").where("date", "==", date).get();
00061: 
00062:   const bySlot = new Map();
00063: 
00064:   for (const doc of snap.docs) {
00065:     const d = doc.data() || {};
00066:     const lk = String(d.lottery_key || d.lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00067:     if (lk !== LOTTERY_KEY) continue;
00068: 
00069:     const slot = normalizeCloseHourForFederal(d.close_hour || d.close || d.hour || "");
00070:     if (!slot) continue;
00071: 
00072:     const pSnap = await doc.ref.collection("prizes").get();
00073:     const prizes = {};
00074: 
00075:     pSnap.forEach((pdoc) => {
00076:       const p = pdoc.data() || {};
00077:       const pos = Number(p.position || String(pdoc.id || "").replace(/\D/g, ""));
00078:       const milhar = normMilhar(p.milhar || p.value || p.raw);
00079:       if (Number.isFinite(pos) && pos >= 1 && pos <= 7 && milhar) {
00080:         prizes[pos] = milhar;
00081:       }
00082:     });
00083: 
00084:     const row = {
00085:       id: doc.id,
00086:       slot,
00087:       prizes,
00088:       prizeCount: Object.keys(prizes).length,
00089:     };
00090: 
00091:     if (!bySlot.has(slot)) bySlot.set(slot, []);
00092:     bySlot.get(slot).push(row);
00093:   }
00094: 
00095:   return bySlot;
00096: }
00097: 
00098: function pickBestDoc(arr) {
00099:   const list = Array.isArray(arr) ? arr.slice() : [];
00100:   list.sort((a, b) => Number(b.prizeCount || 0) - Number(a.prizeCount || 0));
00101:   return list[0] || null;
00102: }
00103: 
00104: async function auditDate(date) {
00105:   const payload = await fetchKingResults({ date, lotteryKey: LOTTERY_KEY });
00106:   const sourceDraws = Array.isArray(payload?.data) ? payload.data : [];
00107: 
00108:   const sourceBySlot = new Map();
00109: 
00110:   for (const draw of sourceDraws) {
00111:     const slot = normalizeCloseHourForFederal(draw?.close_hour || "");
00112:     if (!slot) continue;
00113:     sourceBySlot.set(slot, {
00114:       slot,
00115:       prizes: extractKingPrizes(draw),
00116:     });
00117:   }
00118: 
00119:   const fsBySlot = await readFirestoreByDate(date);
00120: 
00121:   const allSlots = Array.from(
00122:     new Set([...sourceBySlot.keys(), ...fsBySlot.keys()])
00123:   ).sort();
00124: 
00125:   const problems = [];
00126: 
00127:   for (const slot of allSlots) {
00128:     const src = sourceBySlot.get(slot);
00129:     const docs = fsBySlot.get(slot) || [];
00130:     const fsDoc = pickBestDoc(docs);
00131: 
00132:     if (!src) {
00133:       problems.push({ date, slot, type: "extra_firestore", firestoreDocs: docs.map(x => x.id) });
00134:       continue;
00135:     }
00136: 
00137:     if (!fsDoc) {
00138:       problems.push({ date, slot, type: "missing_firestore" });
00139:       continue;
00140:     }
00141: 
00142:     if (docs.length > 1) {
00143:       problems.push({ date, slot, type: "duplicate_firestore", count: docs.length, docs: docs.map(x => x.id) });
00144:     }
00145: 
00146:     for (let pos = 1; pos <= 7; pos++) {
00147:       const a = src.prizes[pos] || "";
00148:       const b = fsDoc.prizes[pos] || "";
00149: 
00150:       if (!a || !b || a !== b) {
00151:         problems.push({
00152:           date,
00153:           slot,
00154:           type: "prize_mismatch",
00155:           position: pos,
00156:           source: a,
00157:           firestore: b,
00158:           firestoreDoc: fsDoc.id,
00159:         });
00160:       }
00161:     }
00162:   }
00163: 
00164:   return {
00165:     date,
00166:     sourceSlots: Array.from(sourceBySlot.keys()).sort(),
00167:     firestoreSlots: Array.from(fsBySlot.keys()).sort(),
00168:     problems,
00169:   };
00170: }
00171: 
00172: async function main() {
00173:   const started = Date.now();
00174:   const results = [];
00175:   let totalDays = 0;
00176:   let totalSlots = 0;
00177:   let totalProblems = 0;
00178: 
00179:   for (let d = START; d <= END; d = addDays(d, 1)) {
00180:     totalDays++;
00181: 
00182:     try {
00183:       const r = await auditDate(d);
00184:       results.push(r);
00185:       totalSlots += r.sourceSlots.length;
00186:       totalProblems += r.problems.length;
00187: 
00188:       const status = r.problems.length ? "PROBLEM" : "OK";
00189:       console.log(`[CONTENT] ${d} ${status} sourceSlots=${r.sourceSlots.length} problems=${r.problems.length}`);
00190: 
00191:       if (r.problems.length) {
00192:         console.log(JSON.stringify(r.problems.slice(0, 10), null, 2));
00193:       }
00194:     } catch (e) {
00195:       totalProblems++;
00196:       const r = {
00197:         date: d,
00198:         sourceSlots: [],
00199:         firestoreSlots: [],
00200:         problems: [{ date: d, type: "audit_error", message: String(e?.message || e) }],
00201:       };
00202:       results.push(r);
00203:       console.log(`[CONTENT] ${d} ERROR ${String(e?.message || e)}`);
00204:     }
00205:   }
00206: 
00207:   const out = {
00208:     period: { start: START, end: END },
00209:     lotteryKey: LOTTERY_KEY,
00210:     totalDays,
00211:     totalSourceSlots: totalSlots,
00212:     totalProblems,
00213:     ok: totalProblems === 0,
00214:     tookMs: Date.now() - started,
00215:     generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
00216:     results,
00217:   };
00218: 
00219:   const dir = path.join(__dirname, "..", "logs");
00220:   fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
00221: 
00222:   const file = path.join(dir, `content-audit-${LOTTERY_KEY}-${START}_to_${END}.json`);
00223:   fs.writeFileSync(file, JSON.stringify(out, null, 2), "utf8");
00224: 
00225:   console.log("");
00226:   console.log("========== AUDITORIA DE CONTEÚDO ==========");
00227:   console.log(`Período: ${START} até ${END}`);
00228:   console.log(`Loteria: ${LOTTERY_KEY}`);
00229:   console.log(`Dias auditados: ${totalDays}`);
00230:   console.log(`Sorteios fonte: ${totalSlots}`);
00231:   console.log(`Problemas: ${totalProblems}`);
00232:   console.log(`OK: ${out.ok ? "SIM" : "NÃO"}`);
00233:   console.log(`Arquivo: ${file}`);
00234: 
00235:   process.exit(out.ok ? 0 : 2);
00236: }

00001: "use strict";

00016: function addDays(ymd, days) {
00017:   const d = new Date(`${ymd}T00:00:00Z`);
00018:   d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() + days);
00019:   return `${d.getUTCFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getUTCMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(d.getUTCDate())}`;
00020: }

00022: function normalizeHHMM(value) {
00023:   const s = String(value ?? "").trim();
00024:   if (/^\d{2}:\d{2}$/.test(s)) return s;
00025: 
00026:   const m1 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})h$/i);
00027:   if (m1) return `${String(m1[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
00028: 
00029:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
00030:   if (m2) return `${String(m2[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
00031: 
00032:   const m3 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})$/);
00033:   if (m3) return `${String(m3[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(m3[2]).padStart(2, "0")}`;
00034: 
00035:   return "";
00036: }
00037: 
00038: function normalizeCloseHourForFederal(value) {
00039:   const raw = normalizeHHMM(value);
00040:   if (!raw) return "";
00041:   return `${raw.slice(0, 2)}:00`;
00042: }

00044: function normMilhar(v) {
00045:   const s = String(v ?? "").replace(/\D/g, "").trim();
00046:   if (!s) return "";
00047:   return s.slice(-4).padStart(4, "0");
00048: }

00050: function extractKingPrizes(draw) {

00053:     const v = normMilhar(draw?.[`prize_${i}`]);
00054:     if (v) out[i] = v;

00056:   return out;

00059: async function readFirestoreByDate(date) {
00060:   const snap = await db.collection("draws").where("date", "==", date).get();
00061: 
00062:   const bySlot = new Map();
00063: 
00064:   for (const doc of snap.docs) {
00065:     const d = doc.data() || {};
00066:     const lk = String(d.lottery_key || d.lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00067:     if (lk !== LOTTERY_KEY) continue;
00068: 
00069:     const slot = normalizeCloseHourForFederal(d.close_hour || d.close || d.hour || "");
00070:     if (!slot) continue;
00071: 
00072:     const pSnap = await doc.ref.collection("prizes").get();
00073:     const prizes = {};
00074: 
00075:     pSnap.forEach((pdoc) => {
00076:       const p = pdoc.data() || {};
00077:       const pos = Number(p.position || String(pdoc.id || "").replace(/\D/g, ""));
00078:       const milhar = normMilhar(p.milhar || p.value || p.raw);
00079:       if (Number.isFinite(pos) && pos >= 1 && pos <= 7 && milhar) {
00080:         prizes[pos] = milhar;
00081:       }
00082:     });
00083: 
00084:     const row = {
00085:       id: doc.id,
00086:       slot,
00087:       prizes,
00088:       prizeCount: Object.keys(prizes).length,
00089:     };
00090: 
00091:     if (!bySlot.has(slot)) bySlot.set(slot, []);
00092:     bySlot.get(slot).push(row);
00093:   }
00094: 
00095:   return bySlot;
00096: }
00097: 
00098: function pickBestDoc(arr) {
00099:   const list = Array.isArray(arr) ? arr.slice() : [];
00100:   list.sort((a, b) => Number(b.prizeCount || 0) - Number(a.prizeCount || 0));
00101:   return list[0] || null;
00102: }

00104: async function auditDate(date) {
00105:   const payload = await fetchKingResults({ date, lotteryKey: LOTTERY_KEY });
00106:   const sourceDraws = Array.isArray(payload?.data) ? payload.data : [];
00107: 
00108:   const sourceBySlot = new Map();
00109: 
00110:   for (const draw of sourceDraws) {
00111:     const slot = normalizeCloseHourForFederal(draw?.close_hour || "");
00112:     if (!slot) continue;
00113:     sourceBySlot.set(slot, {
00114:       slot,
00115:       prizes: extractKingPrizes(draw),
00116:     });
00117:   }
00118: 
00119:   const fsBySlot = await readFirestoreByDate(date);
00120: 
00121:   const allSlots = Array.from(
00122:     new Set([...sourceBySlot.keys(), ...fsBySlot.keys()])
00123:   ).sort();
00124: 
00125:   const problems = [];
00126: 
00127:   for (const slot of allSlots) {
00128:     const src = sourceBySlot.get(slot);
00129:     const docs = fsBySlot.get(slot) || [];
00130:     const fsDoc = pickBestDoc(docs);
00131: 
00132:     if (!src) {
00133:       problems.push({ date, slot, type: "extra_firestore", firestoreDocs: docs.map(x => x.id) });
00134:       continue;
00135:     }
00136: 
00137:     if (!fsDoc) {
00138:       problems.push({ date, slot, type: "missing_firestore" });
00139:       continue;
00140:     }
00141: 
00142:     if (docs.length > 1) {
00143:       problems.push({ date, slot, type: "duplicate_firestore", count: docs.length, docs: docs.map(x => x.id) });
00144:     }
00145: 
00146:     for (let pos = 1; pos <= 7; pos++) {
00147:       const a = src.prizes[pos] || "";
00148:       const b = fsDoc.prizes[pos] || "";
00149: 
00150:       if (!a || !b || a !== b) {
00151:         problems.push({
00152:           date,
00153:           slot,
00154:           type: "prize_mismatch",
00155:           position: pos,
00156:           source: a,
00157:           firestore: b,
00158:           firestoreDoc: fsDoc.id,
00159:         });
00160:       }
00161:     }
00162:   }
00163: 
00164:   return {
00165:     date,
00166:     sourceSlots: Array.from(sourceBySlot.keys()).sort(),
00167:     firestoreSlots: Array.from(fsBySlot.keys()).sort(),
00168:     problems,
00169:   };
00170: }
00171: 
00172: async function main() {
00173:   const started = Date.now();
00174:   const results = [];
00175:   let totalDays = 0;
00176:   let totalSlots = 0;
00177:   let totalProblems = 0;
00178: 
00179:   for (let d = START; d <= END; d = addDays(d, 1)) {
00180:     totalDays++;
00181: 
00182:     try {
00183:       const r = await auditDate(d);
00184:       results.push(r);
00185:       totalSlots += r.sourceSlots.length;
00186:       totalProblems += r.problems.length;
00187: 
00188:       const status = r.problems.length ? "PROBLEM" : "OK";
00189:       console.log(`[CONTENT] ${d} ${status} sourceSlots=${r.sourceSlots.length} problems=${r.problems.length}`);
00190: 
00191:       if (r.problems.length) {
00192:         console.log(JSON.stringify(r.problems.slice(0, 10), null, 2));
00193:       }
00194:     } catch (e) {
00195:       totalProblems++;
00196:       const r = {
00197:         date: d,
00198:         sourceSlots: [],
00199:         firestoreSlots: [],
00200:         problems: [{ date: d, type: "audit_error", message: String(e?.message || e) }],
00201:       };
00202:       results.push(r);
00203:       console.log(`[CONTENT] ${d} ERROR ${String(e?.message || e)}`);
00204:     }
00205:   }
00206: 
00207:   const out = {
00208:     period: { start: START, end: END },
00209:     lotteryKey: LOTTERY_KEY,
00210:     totalDays,
00211:     totalSourceSlots: totalSlots,
00212:     totalProblems,
00213:     ok: totalProblems === 0,
00214:     tookMs: Date.now() - started,
00215:     generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
00216:     results,
00217:   };
00218: 
00219:   const dir = path.join(__dirname, "..", "logs");
00220:   fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
00221: 
00222:   const file = path.join(dir, `content-audit-${LOTTERY_KEY}-${START}_to_${END}.json`);
00223:   fs.writeFileSync(file, JSON.stringify(out, null, 2), "utf8");
00224: 
00225:   console.log("");
00226:   console.log("========== AUDITORIA DE CONTEÚDO ==========");
00227:   console.log(`Período: ${START} até ${END}`);
00228:   console.log(`Loteria: ${LOTTERY_KEY}`);
00229:   console.log(`Dias auditados: ${totalDays}`);
00230:   console.log(`Sorteios fonte: ${totalSlots}`);
00231:   console.log(`Problemas: ${totalProblems}`);
00232:   console.log(`OK: ${out.ok ? "SIM" : "NÃO"}`);
00233:   console.log(`Arquivo: ${file}`);
00234: 
00235:   process.exit(out.ok ? 0 : 2);
00236: }
00237: 
00238: main().catch((e) => {
00239:   console.error("ERRO:", e?.message || e);
00240:   process.exit(1);

00070: function normalizeHourList(arr) {
00071:   const out = [];
00072:   const seen = new Set();

00074:   for (const v of Array.isArray(arr) ? arr : []) {
00075:     const m = String(v ?? "").match(/\d{1,2}/);
00076:     if (!m) continue;

00095: function pickRangeForDate(ranges, ymd) {
00096:   for (const r of Array.isArray(ranges) ? ranges : []) {
00097:     const from0 = String(r?.from || "").trim();
00098:     const to0 = String(r?.to || "").trim();
00099: 
00100:     const hasFrom = isISODate(from0);
00101:     const hasTo = isISODate(to0);
00102: 
00103:     if (!hasFrom && !hasTo) return r;
00104:     if (!hasFrom && hasTo && ymd <= to0) return r;
00105:     if (hasFrom && !hasTo && ymd >= from0) return r;
00106:     if (hasFrom && hasTo && ymd >= from0 && ymd <= to0) return r;
00107:   }
00108: 
00109:   return null;
00110: }

00146:     source: "slot_schedule",
00147:   };
00148: }
00149: 
00150: function getGapsForDate(ymd, sourceGaps) {
00151:   const entry = sourceGaps?.gapsByDay?.[ymd] ?? sourceGaps?.[ymd] ?? null;
00152:   if (!entry) return { removedHard: [], removedSoft: [] };
00153: 
00154:   return {
00155:     removedHard: normalizeHourList(entry.removedHard || entry.hard || []),
00156:     removedSoft: normalizeHourList(entry.removedSoft || entry.soft || []),
00157:   };
00158: }
00159: 
00160: function isNoDrawDay(ymd, noDrawDays) {
00161:   const set = new Set(Array.isArray(noDrawDays?.days) ? noDrawDays.days : []);
00162:   return set.has(ymd);
00163: }
00164: 
00165: function normalizeSlot(close) {
00166:   if (!close) return null;
00167: 
00168:   const m = String(close).match(/(\d{1,2})/);
00169:   if (!m) return null;
00170: 
00171:   const hh = Number(m[1]);
00172:   if (!Number.isFinite(hh) || hh < 0 || hh > 23) return null;
00173: 
00174:   return pad2(hh);
00175: }
00176: 
00177: function isTargetLottery(draw) {
00178:   const lk1 = String(draw?.lottery_key || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00179:   const lk2 = String(draw?.lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00180:   return lk1 === TARGET_LOTTERY || lk2 === TARGET_LOTTERY;
00181: }
00182: 
00183: async function fetchDay(db, ymd) {
00184:   const [s1, s2] = await Promise.all([
00185:     db.collection("draws").where("date", "==", ymd).get(),
00186:     db.collection("draws").where("ymd", "==", ymd).get(),
00187:   ]);
00188: 
00189:   const map = new Map();
00190:   for (const d of s1.docs) map.set(d.id, d);
00191:   for (const d of s2.docs) map.set(d.id, d);
00192: 
00193:   return Array.from(map.values());
00194: }
00195: 
00196: async function run() {
00197:   const db = getDb();
00198:   const slotSchedule = readSlotSchedule();
00199:   const sourceGaps = readSourceGaps();
00200:   const noDrawDays = readNoDrawDays();
00201: 
00202:   let date = START;
00203: 
00204:   const report = {
00205:     days: 0,
00206:     daysWithError: [],
00207:     blockedNoDraw: [],
00208:     missingHard: [],
00209:     missingSoft: [],
00210:     duplicates: [],
00211:     unexpected: [],
00212:     noDocsButExpected: [],
00213:   };
00214: 
00215:   while (date <= END) {
00216:     report.days++;
00217: 
00218:     if (report.days % 25 === 0 || date === START || date === END) {
00219:       console.log(`Scanning: ${date} | dias=${report.days}`);
00220:     }
00221: 
00222:     try {
00223:       if (isNoDrawDay(date, noDrawDays)) {
00224:         report.blockedNoDraw.push({ date, reason: "no_draw_days" });
00225:         date = nextDay(date);
00226:         continue;
00227:       }
00228: 
00229:       const expectedBase = getExpectedForDate(date, slotSchedule);
00230:       const gaps = getGapsForDate(date, sourceGaps);
00231: 
00232:       const expectedHard = expectedBase.hard.filter((h) => !gaps.removedHard.includes(h));
00233:       const expectedSoft = expectedBase.soft.filter((h) => !gaps.removedSoft.includes(h));
00234:       const expectedAll = uniqSorted([...expectedHard, ...expectedSoft]);
00235: 
00236:       const docs = await fetchDay(db, date);
00237: 
00238:       const draws = docs
00239:         .map((d) => d.data())
00240:         .filter((d) => isTargetLottery(d));
00241: 
00242:       const present = [];
00243: 
00244:       for (const d of draws) {
00245:         const slot = normalizeSlot(
00246:           d.close_hour ||
00247:             d.close ||
00248:             d.close_hour_raw ||
00249:             d.hour ||
00250:             d.horario
00251:         );
00252: 
00253:         if (slot) present.push(slot);
00254:       }
00255: 
00256:       const presentSet = new Set(present);
00257: 
00258:       if (!draws.length && expectedAll.length) {
00259:         report.noDocsButExpected.push({
00260:           date,
00261:           expectedHard,
00262:           expectedSoft,
00263:         });
00264:       }
00265: 
00266:       for (const h of expectedHard) {
00267:         if (!presentSet.has(h)) {
00268:           report.missingHard.push({ date, h });
00269:         }
00270:       }
00271: 
00272:       for (const h of expectedSoft) {
00273:         if (!presentSet.has(h)) {
00274:           report.missingSoft.push({ date, h });
00275:         }
00276:       }
00277: 
00278:       const seen = new Set();
00279: 
00280:       for (const h of present) {
00281:         if (seen.has(h)) {
00282:           report.duplicates.push({ date, h });
00283:         }
00284:         seen.add(h);
00285: 
00286:         if (!expectedAll.includes(h)) {
00287:           report.unexpected.push({ date, h });
00288:         }
00289:       }
00290:     } catch (e) {
00291:       report.daysWithError.push({
00292:         date,
00293:         error: String(e?.message || e),
00294:       });
00295:     }
00296: 
00297:     date = nextDay(date);
00298:   }
00299: 
00300:   console.log("\n===== AUDITORIA HISTÓRICA REAL =====\n");
00301:   console.log("Loteria:", TARGET_LOTTERY);
00302:   console.log("Período:", `${START} até ${END}`);
00303:   console.log("Dias analisados:", report.days);
00304: 
00305:   console.log("\nDias bloqueados (no_draw_days):", report.blockedNoDraw.length);
00306:   console.log("Dias com erro:", report.daysWithError.length);
00307:   console.log("Dias sem docs mas com horários esperados:", report.noDocsButExpected.length);
00308: 
00309:   console.log("\nMissing HARD:", report.missingHard.length);
00310:   console.log("Missing SOFT:", report.missingSoft.length);
00311:   console.log("Duplicados:", report.duplicates.length);
00312:   console.log("Unexpected:", report.unexpected.length);
00313: 
00314:   console.log("\n--- DIAS BLOQUEADOS ---");
00315:   console.table(report.blockedNoDraw.slice(0, 50));
00316: 
00317:   console.log("\n--- HARD ---");
00318:   console.table(report.missingHard.slice(0, 50));
00319: 
00320:   console.log("\n--- SOFT ---");
00321:   console.table(report.missingSoft.slice(0, 50));
00322: 
00323:   console.log("\n--- DIAS SEM DOCS MAS COM EXPECTATIVA ---");
00324:   console.table(report.noDocsButExpected.slice(0, 50));
00325: 
00326:   console.log("\n--- DUPLICADOS ---");
00327:   console.table(report.duplicates.slice(0, 50));
00328: 
00329:   console.log("\n--- UNEXPECTED ---");
00330:   console.table(report.unexpected.slice(0, 50));
00331: 
00332:   console.log("\n--- DIAS COM ERRO ---");
00333:   console.table(report.daysWithError.slice(0, 50));
00334: }
00335: 
00336: run().catch((e) => {
00337:   console.error(e);
00338:   process.exit(1);
00339: });

00001: "use strict";
00002: 
00003: const fs = require("fs");
00004: const path = require("path");
00005: const { db } = require("../service/firebaseAdmin");
00006: const { fetchKingResults } = require("./importKingApostas");
00007: 
00008: const START = process.argv[2] || "2022-06-07";
00009: const END = process.argv[3] || "2026-07-08";
00010: const LOTTERY_KEY = "PT_RIO";
00011: 
00012: function pad2(n) {
00013:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
00014: }
00015: 
00016: function addDays(ymd, days) {
00017:   const d = new Date(`${ymd}T00:00:00Z`);
00018:   d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() + days);
00019:   return `${d.getUTCFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getUTCMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(d.getUTCDate())}`;
00020: }
00021: 
00022: function normalizeHHMM(value) {
00023:   const s = String(value ?? "").trim();
00024:   if (/^\d{2}:\d{2}$/.test(s)) return s;
00025: 
00026:   const m1 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})h$/i);
00027:   if (m1) return `${String(m1[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
00028: 
00029:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
00030:   if (m2) return `${String(m2[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
00031: 
00032:   const m3 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})$/);
00033:   if (m3) return `${String(m3[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(m3[2]).padStart(2, "0")}`;
00034: 
00035:   return "";
00036: }
00037: 
00038: function normalizeCloseHourForPtRio(value) {
00039:   const raw = normalizeHHMM(value);
00040:   if (!raw) return "";
00041:   return `${raw.slice(0, 2)}:00`;
00042: }
00043: 
00044: function normMilhar(v) {
00045:   const s = String(v ?? "").replace(/\D/g, "").trim();
00046:   if (!s) return "";
00047:   return s.slice(-4).padStart(4, "0");
00048: }
00049: 
00050: function extractKingPrizes(draw) {
00051:   const out = {};
00052:   for (let i = 1; i <= 7; i++) {
00053:     const v = normMilhar(draw?.[`prize_${i}`]);
00054:     if (v) out[i] = v;
00055:   }
00056:   return out;
00057: }
00058: 
00059: async function readFirestoreByDate(date) {
00060:   const snap = await db.collection("draws").where("date", "==", date).get();
00061: 
00062:   const bySlot = new Map();
00063: 
00064:   for (const doc of snap.docs) {
00065:     const d = doc.data() || {};
00066:     const lk = String(d.lottery_key || d.lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00067:     if (lk !== LOTTERY_KEY) continue;
00068: 
00069:     const slot = normalizeCloseHourForPtRio(d.close_hour || d.close || d.hour || "");
00070:     if (!slot) continue;
00071: 
00072:     const pSnap = await doc.ref.collection("prizes").get();
00073:     const prizes = {};
00074: 
00075:     pSnap.forEach((pdoc) => {
00076:       const p = pdoc.data() || {};
00077:       const pos = Number(p.position || String(pdoc.id || "").replace(/\D/g, ""));
00078:       const milhar = normMilhar(p.milhar || p.value || p.raw);
00079:       if (Number.isFinite(pos) && pos >= 1 && pos <= 7 && milhar) {
00080:         prizes[pos] = milhar;
00081:       }
00082:     });
00083: 
00084:     const row = {
00085:       id: doc.id,
00086:       slot,
00087:       prizes,
00088:       prizeCount: Object.keys(prizes).length,
00089:     };
00090: 
00091:     if (!bySlot.has(slot)) bySlot.set(slot, []);
00092:     bySlot.get(slot).push(row);
00093:   }
00094: 
00095:   return bySlot;
00096: }
00097: 
00098: function pickBestDoc(arr) {
00099:   const list = Array.isArray(arr) ? arr.slice() : [];
00100:   list.sort((a, b) => Number(b.prizeCount || 0) - Number(a.prizeCount || 0));
00101:   return list[0] || null;
00102: }
00103: 
00104: async function auditDate(date) {
00105:   const payload = await fetchKingResults({ date, lotteryKey: LOTTERY_KEY });
00106:   const sourceDraws = Array.isArray(payload?.data) ? payload.data : [];
00107: 
00108:   const sourceBySlot = new Map();
00109: 
00110:   for (const draw of sourceDraws) {
00111:     const slot = normalizeCloseHourForPtRio(draw?.close_hour || "");
00112:     if (!slot) continue;
00113:     sourceBySlot.set(slot, {
00114:       slot,
00115:       prizes: extractKingPrizes(draw),
00116:     });
00117:   }
00118: 
00119:   const fsBySlot = await readFirestoreByDate(date);
00120: 
00121:   const allSlots = Array.from(
00122:     new Set([...sourceBySlot.keys(), ...fsBySlot.keys()])
00123:   ).sort();
00124: 
00125:   const problems = [];
00126: 
00127:   for (const slot of allSlots) {
00128:     const src = sourceBySlot.get(slot);
00129:     const docs = fsBySlot.get(slot) || [];
00130:     const fsDoc = pickBestDoc(docs);
00131: 
00132:     if (!src) {
00133:       problems.push({ date, slot, type: "extra_firestore", firestoreDocs: docs.map(x => x.id) });
00134:       continue;
00135:     }
00136: 
00137:     if (!fsDoc) {
00138:       problems.push({ date, slot, type: "missing_firestore" });
00139:       continue;
00140:     }
00141: 
00142:     if (docs.length > 1) {
00143:       problems.push({ date, slot, type: "duplicate_firestore", count: docs.length, docs: docs.map(x => x.id) });
00144:     }
00145: 
00146:     for (let pos = 1; pos <= 7; pos++) {
00147:       const a = src.prizes[pos] || "";
00148:       const b = fsDoc.prizes[pos] || "";
00149: 
00150:       if (!a || !b || a !== b) {
00151:         problems.push({
00152:           date,
00153:           slot,
00154:           type: "prize_mismatch",
00155:           position: pos,
00156:           source: a,
00157:           firestore: b,
00158:           firestoreDoc: fsDoc.id,
00159:         });
00160:       }
00161:     }
00162:   }
00163: 
00164:   return {
00165:     date,
00166:     sourceSlots: Array.from(sourceBySlot.keys()).sort(),
00167:     firestoreSlots: Array.from(fsBySlot.keys()).sort(),
00168:     problems,
00169:   };
00170: }
00171: 
00172: async function main() {
00173:   const started = Date.now();
00174:   const results = [];
00175:   let totalDays = 0;
00176:   let totalSlots = 0;
00177:   let totalProblems = 0;
00178: 
00179:   for (let d = START; d <= END; d = addDays(d, 1)) {
00180:     totalDays++;
00181: 
00182:     try {
00183:       const r = await auditDate(d);
00184:       results.push(r);
00185:       totalSlots += r.sourceSlots.length;
00186:       totalProblems += r.problems.length;
00187: 
00188:       const status = r.problems.length ? "PROBLEM" : "OK";
00189:       console.log(`[CONTENT] ${d} ${status} sourceSlots=${r.sourceSlots.length} problems=${r.problems.length}`);
00190: 
00191:       if (r.problems.length) {
00192:         console.log(JSON.stringify(r.problems.slice(0, 10), null, 2));
00193:       }
00194:     } catch (e) {
00195:       totalProblems++;
00196:       const r = {
00197:         date: d,
00198:         sourceSlots: [],
00199:         firestoreSlots: [],
00200:         problems: [{ date: d, type: "audit_error", message: String(e?.message || e) }],
00201:       };
00202:       results.push(r);
00203:       console.log(`[CONTENT] ${d} ERROR ${String(e?.message || e)}`);
00204:     }
00205:   }
00206: 
00207:   const out = {
00208:     period: { start: START, end: END },
00209:     lotteryKey: LOTTERY_KEY,
00210:     totalDays,
00211:     totalSourceSlots: totalSlots,
00212:     totalProblems,
00213:     ok: totalProblems === 0,
00214:     tookMs: Date.now() - started,
00215:     generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
00216:     results,
00217:   };
00218: 
00219:   const dir = path.join(__dirname, "..", "logs");
00220:   fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
00221: 
00222:   const file = path.join(dir, `content-audit-${LOTTERY_KEY}-${START}_to_${END}.json`);
00223:   fs.writeFileSync(file, JSON.stringify(out, null, 2), "utf8");
00224: 
00225:   console.log("");
00226:   console.log("========== AUDITORIA DE CONTEÚDO ==========");
00227:   console.log(`Período: ${START} até ${END}`);
00228:   console.log(`Loteria: ${LOTTERY_KEY}`);
00229:   console.log(`Dias auditados: ${totalDays}`);
00230:   console.log(`Sorteios fonte: ${totalSlots}`);
00231:   console.log(`Problemas: ${totalProblems}`);
00232:   console.log(`OK: ${out.ok ? "SIM" : "NÃO"}`);
00233:   console.log(`Arquivo: ${file}`);
00234: 
00235:   process.exit(out.ok ? 0 : 2);
00236: }
00237: 
00238: main().catch((e) => {
00239:   console.error("ERRO:", e?.message || e);
00240:   process.exit(1);

00022: function normalizeHHMM(value) {
00023:   const s = String(value ?? "").trim();
00024:   if (/^\d{2}:\d{2}$/.test(s)) return s;
00025: 
00026:   const m1 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})h$/i);
00027:   if (m1) return `${String(m1[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
00028: 
00029:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
00030:   if (m2) return `${String(m2[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
00031: 
00032:   const m3 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})$/);
00033:   if (m3) return `${String(m3[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(m3[2]).padStart(2, "0")}`;
00034: 
00035:   return "";
00036: }

00104: async function auditDate(date) {

00084: function addMinutesToHHMM(hhmm, minutes) {
00085:   const [h, m] = String(hhmm || "00:00").split(":").map(Number);
00086:   const total = (h * 60) + m + Number(minutes || 0);
00087:   const hh = Math.floor(total / 60);
00088:   const mm = total % 60;
00089:   return `${pad2(hh)}:${pad2(mm)}`;
00090: }

00098: 
00099:   const now = nowHHMMLocal();
00100:   const toleranceMin = 10;
00101: 
00102:   return expected.filter(h => addMinutesToHHMM(h, toleranceMin) <= now);
00103: }
