00076:   if (pickHour(d)) score += 5;
00077:   if (Array.isArray(d.prizes) && d.prizes.length) score += 50;
00078:   if (Array.isArray(d.results) && d.results.length) score += 30;
00079:   if (d.lotteryKey === "FEDERAL") score += 20;
00080:   if (d.source) score += 5;
00081:   if (d.createdAt) score += 2;
00082:   if (d.updatedAt) score += 3;
00083:   return score;
00084: }
00085: 
00086: function makeKey(d) {
00087:   const date = pickDate(d);
00088:   const hash = safeStr(d.contestHash || d.resultHash || d.contentHash || "");
00089:   if (date && hash) return `${date}:${hash}`;
00090:   return `${date}:${pickResultHash(d)}`;
00091: }
00092: 
00093: async function main() {
00094:   if (!admin.apps.length) {
00095:     admin.initializeApp();
00096:   }
00097: 
00098:   const db = admin.firestore();
00099: 
00100:   const snap = await db.collection("draws").get();
00101: 
00102:   const docs = [];
00103: 
00104:   snap.forEach((doc) => {
00105:     const data = doc.data() || {};
00106:     const lotteryKey = safeStr(data.lottery_key || data.lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
00107:     if (lotteryKey !== "FEDERAL") return;
00108: 
00109:     const date = pickDate(data);
00110: 
00111:     if (date >= START && date <= END) {
00112:       docs.push({
00113:         id: doc.id,
00114:         refPath: doc.ref.path,
00115:         data,
00116:         date,
00117:         hour: pickHour(data),
00118:         key: makeKey(data),
00119:         score: scoreDoc(data),
00120:       });
00121:     }
00122:   });
00123: 
00124:   const groups = new Map();
00125: 
00126:   for (const d of docs) {
00127:     if (!groups.has(d.key)) groups.set(d.key, []);
00128:     groups.get(d.key).push(d);
00129:   }
00130: 
00131:   const duplicated = [];
00132:   const keep = [];
00133:   const remove = [];
00134: 
00135:   for (const [key, arr] of groups.entries()) {
00136:     arr.sort((a, b) => {
00137:       if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
00138:       return String(a.id).localeCompare(String(b.id));
00139:     });
00140: 
00141:     keep.push(arr[0]);
00142: 
00143:     if (arr.length > 1) {
00144:       duplicated.push({ key, count: arr.length, keep: arr[0], remove: arr.slice(1) });
00145:       remove.push(...arr.slice(1));
00146:     }
00147:   }
00148: 
00149:   const report = {
00150:     start: START,
00151:     end: END,
00152:     totalDocs: docs.length,
00153:     uniqueKeys: groups.size,
00154:     duplicatedKeys: duplicated.length,
00155:     docsToKeep: keep.length,
00156:     docsToRemove: remove.length,
00157:     duplicated: duplicated.map((g) => ({
00158:       key: g.key,
00159:       count: g.count,
00160:       keep: {
00161:         id: g.keep.id,
00162:         path: g.keep.refPath,
00163:         date: g.keep.date,
00164:         hour: g.keep.hour,
00165:         score: g.keep.score,
00166:       },
00167:       remove: g.remove.map((r) => ({
00168:         id: r.id,
00169:         path: r.refPath,
00170:         date: r.date,
00171:         hour: r.hour,
00172:         score: r.score,
00173:       })),
00174:     })),
00175:   };
00176: 
00177:   const outDir = path.join(__dirname, "..", "logs");
00178:   fs.mkdirSync(outDir, { recursive: true });
00179: 
00180:   const outFile = path.join(
00181:     outDir,
00182:     `federal-duplicates-dry-run-${START}_to_${END}.json`
00183:   );
00184: 
00185:   fs.writeFileSync(outFile, JSON.stringify(report, null, 2), "utf8");
00186: 
00187:   const APPLY = process.argv.includes("--apply");
00188: 
00189:   console.log("");
00190:   console.log(APPLY ? "========== FEDERAL DUPLICIDADES — APPLY ==========" : "========== FEDERAL DUPLICIDADES — DRY RUN ==========");
00191:   console.log(`Documentos FEDERAL no período: ${report.totalDocs}`);
00192:   console.log(`Chaves únicas: ${report.uniqueKeys}`);
00193:   console.log(`Chaves duplicadas: ${report.duplicatedKeys}`);
00194:   console.log(`Manter: ${report.docsToKeep}`);
00195:   console.log(`Remover se aplicar: ${report.docsToRemove}`);
00196:   console.log(`Arquivo: ${outFile}`);
00197:   if (APPLY) {
00198:     console.log("");
00199:     console.log("Aplicando remoção dos duplicados...");
00200: 
00201:     const batchSize = 400;
00202:     let deleted = 0;
00203: 
00204:     for (let i = 0; i < remove.length; i += batchSize) {
00205:       const batch = db.batch();
00206:       const chunk = remove.slice(i, i + batchSize);
00207: 
00208:       for (const item of chunk) {
00209:         const ref = db.doc(item.refPath);
00210: 
00211:         const prizesSnap = await ref.collection("prizes").get();
00212:         prizesSnap.forEach((pdoc) => batch.delete(pdoc.ref));
00213: 
00214:         batch.delete(ref);
00215:       }
00216: 
00217:       await batch.commit();
00218:       deleted += chunk.length;
00219:       console.log(`Removidos: ${deleted}/${remove.length}`);
00220:     }
00221: 
00222:     console.log("");
00223:     console.log(`CONCLUÍDO. Duplicados removidos: ${deleted}`);
00224:   }
00225: 
00226:   console.log("");
00227:   console.log("Primeiras duplicidades:");
00228:   report.duplicated.slice(0, 20).forEach((g) => {
00229:     console.log(
00230:       `dup | ${g.key} | count=${g.count} | keep=${g.keep.id} ${g.keep.date} ${g.keep.hour} | remove=${g.remove.map((x) => x.id).join(",")}`
00231:     );
00232:   });
00233: }
00234: 
00235: main().catch((e) => {
00236:   console.error("ERRO:", e);
00237:   process.exit(1);
00238: });

00001: "use strict";

00009:   if (i >= 0 && process.argv[i + 1]) return process.argv[i + 1];
00010:   return def;

00013: function safeFilePart(s) {
00014:   return String(s ?? "").replace(/[^\w.\-]+/g, "_");
00015: }
00016: 
00017: function pad2(n) { return String(n).padStart(2, "0"); }
00018: 
00019: // extrai de d_PT_RIO_2026-01-31_2100  => { ymd:"2026-01-31", hh:"21", mm:"00" }
00020: function parseLegacyId(id) {
00021:   const s = String(id || "");
00022:   const m = s.match(/^d_([A-Z_]+)_(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})_(\d{2})(\d{2})$/);
00023:   if (!m) return null;
00024:   return { lottery: m[1], ymd: m[2], hh: m[3], mm: m[4] };
00025: }
00026: 
00027: (async () => {
00028:   try {
00029:     const lottery = String(arg("lottery", "")).trim().toUpperCase();
00030:     const from = arg("from");
00031:     const to = arg("to");
00032:     const apply = arg("apply") === "1";
00033:     const dryRun = !apply;
00034: 
00035:     if (!lottery) throw new Error("Informe --lottery (ex: PT_RIO)");
00036:     if (!from || !to) throw new Error("Informe --from e --to (YYYY-MM-DD)");
00037: 
00038:     const db = getDb();
00039:     const col = db.collection("draws");
00040: 
00041:     console.log("Mode:", dryRun ? "DRY-RUN (não apaga)" : "APPLY (apagando)");
00042:     console.log("Filter:", { lottery, from, to });
00043: 
00044:     // pega docs do range (pela ymd preenchida); os legacy podem ter ymd null,
00045:     // então vamos varrer por duplicatas específicas com query direta no dia suspeito.
00046:     // Como você já tem o DUP apontando o ID, vamos trabalhar por "id startsWith d_".
00047:     // Para não fazer scan global, vamos buscar por lottery_key + range e coletar ids.
00048:     const snap = await col
00049:       .where("lottery_key", "==", lottery)
00050:       .where("ymd", ">=", from)
00051:       .where("ymd", "<=", to)
00052:       .get();
00053: 
00054:     const idSet = new Set(snap.docs.map(d => d.id));
00055: 
00056:     // inclui também docs legacy que possam estar no range mas com ymd faltando:
00057:     // vamos usar o arquivo dups gerado pra achar, mas aqui faremos uma busca simples por ID conhecido:
00058:     // (fallback: se não achar, você me passa a lista e eu faço um scanner por id prefix)
00059:     const toDelete = [];
00060:     const reasons = [];
00061: 
00062:     for (const id of idSet) {
00063:       if (!id.startsWith("d_")) continue;
00064: 
00065:       const p = parseLegacyId(id);
00066:       if (!p) continue;
00067:       if (p.lottery !== lottery) continue;
00068:       if (p.ymd < from || p.ymd > to) continue;
00069: 
00070:       // irmão canônico
00071:       const suffix = `LT_${lottery}_${p.hh}HS`;
00072:       const sibling = `${lottery}__${p.ymd}__${p.hh}-00__${suffix}`;
00073: 
00074:       if (idSet.has(sibling)) {
00075:         toDelete.push(id);
00076:         reasons.push({ id, rule: "legacy d_* duplicate", keep: sibling });
00077:       }
00078:     }
00079: 
00080:     console.log("Candidates to delete:", toDelete.length);
00081: 
00082:     const logDir = path.join(process.cwd(), "logs");
00083:     if (!fs.existsSync(logDir)) fs.mkdirSync(logDir, { recursive: true });
00084: 
00085:     const logFile = path.join(
00086:       logDir,
00087:       `removed-legacy-ids-${safeFilePart(lottery)}-${safeFilePart(from)}_to_${safeFilePart(to)}-${Date.now()}.json`
00088:     );
00089: 
00090:     if (!dryRun && toDelete.length) {
00091:       let idx = 0;
00092:       while (idx < toDelete.length) {
00093:         const chunk = toDelete.slice(idx, idx + 450);
00094:         const batch = db.batch();
00095:         chunk.forEach(x => batch.delete(col.doc(x)));
00096:         await batch.commit();
00097:         idx += chunk.length;
00098:       }
00099:       console.log("Deleted:", toDelete.length);
00100:     }
00101: 
00102:     fs.writeFileSync(logFile, JSON.stringify({ lottery, from, to, apply, count: toDelete.length, toDelete, reasons }, null, 2), "utf8");
00103:     console.log("Log saved:", logFile);
00104: 
00105:     if (dryRun && toDelete.length) {
00106:       console.log("\nSample (up to 10):");
00107:       toDelete.slice(0, 10).forEach(x => console.log(" -", x));
00108:     }
00109: 
00110:   } catch (e) {
00111:     console.error("ERRO:", e?.stack || e?.message || e);
00112:     process.exit(1);
00113:   }

01217:       proof.targetSavedCount = 0;

01219:       proof.targetSavedCount = proof.targetWriteCount;

01227:       ` | skip_vazio=${skippedEmpty} | skip_invalido=${skippedInvalid} | skip_close=${skippedCloseHour}` +

01256: /**
01257:  * ✅ Cleanup automático: remove docs legados (lottery_id null) quando existe doc UUID no mesmo date+slot.
01258:  * - Usa query mínima por date (sem índice composto)

01261: async function cleanupLegacyDrawsForDate({ date, lotteryKey }) {
01262:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
01263:   if (!date || !isISODate(date) || !lk)
01264:     return { ok: false, deleted: 0, slots: 0, reason: "bad_args" };
01265: 
01266:   const snap = await db.collection("draws").where("date", "==", date).get();
01267:   const rows = snap.docs.map((doc) => ({ ref: doc.ref, id: doc.id, ...doc.data() }));
01268: 
01269:   const mine = rows.filter(
01270:     (r) => String(r.lottery_key || "").trim().toUpperCase() === lk
01271:   );
01272: 
01273:   const bySlot = new Map();
01274:   for (const r of mine) {
01275:     const slot = String(r.close_hour || "").trim();
01276:     if (!slot) continue;
01277:     if (!bySlot.has(slot)) bySlot.set(slot, []);
01278:     bySlot.get(slot).push(r);
01279:   }
01280: 
01281:   let deleted = 0;
01282:   let slots = 0;
01283:   let batch = db.batch();
01284:   let ops = 0;
01285: 
01286:   for (const [slot, arr] of bySlot.entries()) {
01287:     const hasUuid = arr.some((x) => x.lottery_id && String(x.lottery_id).includes("-"));
01288:     if (!hasUuid) continue;
01289: 
01290:     const leg = arr.filter((x) => !x.lottery_id);
01291:     if (!leg.length) continue;
01292: 
01293:     for (const d of leg) {
01294:       batch.delete(d.ref);
01295:       deleted += 1;
01296:       ops += 1;
01297:       if (ops >= 450) {
01298:         await batch.commit();
01299:         batch = db.batch();
01300:         ops = 0;
01301:       }
01302:     }
01303:     slots += 1;
01304:   }
01305: 
01306:   if (ops > 0) await batch.commit();
01307: 
01308:   return { ok: true, deleted, slots };
01309: }

00168: async function getFetch() {
00169:   if (typeof fetch === "function") return fetch;
00170: 
00171:   try {
00172:     // eslint-disable-next-line global-require
00173:     const mod = require("node-fetch");
00174:     return mod.default || mod;
00175:   } catch (e) {
00176:     throw new Error(
00177:       "Este Node não possui fetch global e o pacote 'node-fetch' não está instalado. " +
00178:         "Use Node 18+ ou instale: npm i node-fetch"
00179:     );
00180:   }
00181: }
00182: 
00183: /**
00184:  * fetch JSON com timeout + retry
00185:  * ENV:
00186:  * - FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS (default 15000)
00187:  * - FETCH_RETRIES (default 2)
00188:  * - FETCH_RETRY_DELAY_MS (default 600)
00189:  */
00190: async function fetchJson(url) {
00191:   const _fetch = await getFetch();
00192: 
00193:   const TIMEOUT_MS = envInt("FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS", 15000);
00194:   const RETRIES = envInt("FETCH_RETRIES", 2);
00195:   const DELAY_MS = envInt("FETCH_RETRY_DELAY_MS", 600);
00196: 
00197:   const maxAttempts = 1 + Math.max(0, RETRIES);
00198: 
00199:   for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= maxAttempts; attempt++) {
00200:     const ctrl =
00201:       typeof AbortController !== "undefined" ? new AbortController() : null;
00202:     const t = ctrl ? setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), TIMEOUT_MS) : null;
00203: 
00204:     try {
00205:       const r = await _fetch(url, {
00206:         headers: { accept: "application/json" },
00207:         signal: ctrl ? ctrl.signal : undefined,
00208:       });
00209: 
00210:       const txt = await r.text();
00211:       let j = null;
00212:       try {
00213:         j = JSON.parse(txt);
00214:       } catch {}
00215: 
00216:       if (!r.ok) {
00217:         const msg = j && j.error ? j.error : txt || `HTTP ${r.status}`;
00218:         throw new Error(msg);
00219:       }
00220: 
00221:       // ✅ IMPORTANT: API pode responder 200 com ok:false
00222:       if (j && j.ok === false) {
00223:         const msg = j.error || j.message || "Resposta ok:false";
00224:         throw new Error(msg);
00225:       }
00226: 
00227:       return j;
00228:     } catch (e) {
00229:       const isLast = attempt === maxAttempts;
00230:       const msg =
00231:         e?.name === "AbortError"
00232:           ? `timeout após ${TIMEOUT_MS}ms`
00233:           : e?.message || String(e);
00234: 
00235:       if (isLast) throw new Error(`${msg} (url=${url})`);
00236: 
00237:       // retry
00238:       // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
00239:       console.warn(
00240:         `[WARN] fetch falhou (${attempt}/${maxAttempts}) url=${url} => ${msg}`
00241:       );
00242:       if (DELAY_MS > 0) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, DELAY_MS));
00243:     } finally {
00244:       if (t) clearTimeout(t);
00245:     }
00246:   }
00247: 
00248:   throw new Error("fetchJson: falha inesperada");
00249: }
00250: 
00251: function isDayNoDraw({ blocked, dayStatus, blockedReason }) {
00252:   const ds = String(dayStatus || "").trim();
00253:   const br = String(blockedReason || "").trim();
00254: 
00255:   if (ds === "holiday_no_draw") return true;
00256:   if (blocked === true && ds === "holiday_no_draw") return true;
00257:   if (blocked === true && (br === "holiday_no_draw" || br === "day_no_draw"))
00258:     return true;
00259: 
00260:   return false;
00261: }

00421:         dayStatus: r.dayStatus,
00422:         presentHours: r.presentHours,
00423:       })),
00424:   };
00425: 
00426:   const logsDir = path.join(__dirname, "..", "logs");
00427:   const out = path.join(
00428:     logsDir,
00429:     `backfillPlan-${lottery}-${String(audit?.startYmd || "start")}_to_${String(
00430:       audit?.endYmd || "end"
00431:     )}.json`
00432:   );
00433: 
00434:   fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(out), { recursive: true });
00435:   fs.writeFileSync(out, JSON.stringify(plan, null, 2));
00436: 
00437:   console.log("==================================");
00438:   console.log("[PLAN] Backfill gerado:");
00439:   console.log(out);
00440:   console.log("[TOTALS]", plan.totals);
00441:   console.log("==================================");
00442: }
00443: 
00444: main().catch((e) => {
00445:   console.error("ERRO:", e?.stack || e?.message || e);
00446:   process.exit(1);
00447: });

00005: function isISODate(s) {
00006:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
00007:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(str)) return false;
00008:   const [y, m, d] = str.split("-").map(Number);
00009:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
00010:   return dt.getUTCFullYear() === y && dt.getUTCMonth() === (m - 1) && dt.getUTCDate() === d;
00011: }

00308: function normalizeLotteryCodeAny(d) {

00311:     d?.lotteryCode ??

00321: function sortDrawsLocal(draws) {
00322:   const toNum = (h) => {
00323:     const s = normalizeHourLike(h);
00324:     const m = s.match(/^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
00325:     if (!m) return 0;
00326:     return Number(m[1]) * 100 + Number(m[2]);
00327:   };
00328: 
00329:   return [...(draws || [])].sort((a, b) => {
00330:     const ya = String(a?.ymd || "");
00331:     const yb = String(b?.ymd || "");
00332:     if (ya !== yb) return ya.localeCompare(yb);
00333: 
00334:     const ha = toNum(a?.close_hour);
00335:     const hb = toNum(b?.close_hour);
00336:     if (ha !== hb) return ha - hb;
00337: 
00338:     const la = normalizeLotteryCodeAny(a);
00339:     const lb = normalizeLotteryCodeAny(b);
00340:     if (la !== lb) return la.localeCompare(lb);
00341: 
00342:     const ia = String(a?.drawId || a?.id || "");
00343:     const ib = String(b?.drawId || b?.id || "");
00344:     return ia.localeCompare(ib);
00345:   });
00346: }

00350:     const s = normalizeHourLike(h);

00352:     if (!m) return 0;
00353:     return Number(m[1]) * 100 + Number(m[2]);

00358:     const yb = String(b?.ymd || "");
00359:     if (ya !== yb) return yb.localeCompare(ya);
00360:

00374: 
00375: /**
00376:  * ✅ DEDUPE
00377:  * - chave lógica: ymd + hour (+ lottery_code quando existir)
00378:  * - se não houver ymd/hour, cai no id/idx
00379:  *
00380:  * ✅ FIX CRÍTICO:
00381:  * Não colapsar dois draws distintos do mesmo dia/horário com lottery_code diferente.
00382:  */
00383: function dedupeDrawsLocal(draws) {
00384:   const arr = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
00385: 
00386:   const byKey = new Map();
00387:   const order = [];
00388: 
00389:   function score(d) {
00390:     const prizesLen = Array.isArray(d?.prizes) ? d.prizes.length : 0;
00391:     const pc = Number.isFinite(Number(d?.prizesCount))
00392:       ? Number(d.prizesCount)
00393:       : 0;
00394:     const hasLogical = !!(d?.ymd && d?.close_hour);
00395:     return prizesLen * 1_000_000 + pc * 1_000 + (hasLogical ? 10 : 0);
00396:   }
00397: 
00398:   for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i += 1) {
00399:     const raw = arr[i] || {};
00400: 
00401:     const ymd = raw.ymd || normalizeToYMD(raw.date) || "";
00402:     const hour = normalizeHourLike(raw.close_hour || raw.closeHour || "");
00403: 
00404:     const lotCode = normalizeLotteryCodeAny(raw);
00405: 
00406:     const drawId = raw.drawId ?? raw.id ?? raw.__name__ ?? null;
00407:     const idStr = drawId != null ? String(drawId) : "";
00408: 
00409:     const hasLogical = !!(ymd && hour);
00410: 
00411:     const key = hasLogical
00412:       ? lotCode
00413:         ? `${ymd}__${hour}__${lotCode}`
00414:         : `${ymd}__${hour}`
00415:       : `id__${idStr || `idx_${i}`}`;
00416: 
00417:     const normalized = {
00418:       ...raw,
00419:       ymd: ymd || raw.ymd || null,
00420:       close_hour: hour || raw.close_hour || raw.closeHour || "",
00421:       closeHour: hour || raw.closeHour || raw.close_hour || "",
00422:       lottery_code: lotCode || raw.lottery_code || raw.lotteryCode || null,
00423:       drawId: raw.drawId || raw.id || raw.__name__ || null,
00424:       id: raw.id || raw.drawId || raw.__name__ || null,
00425:     };
00426: 
00427:     if (!byKey.has(key)) {
00428:       byKey.set(key, normalized);
00429:       order.push(key);
00430:       continue;
00431:     }
00432: 
00433:     const prev = byKey.get(key);
00434:     if (score(normalized) > score(prev)) {
00435:       byKey.set(key, normalized);
00436:     }
00437:   }
00438: 
00439:   return order.map((k) => byKey.get(k)).filter(Boolean);
00440: }
00441: 
00442: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limitN, mapper) {
00443:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
00444:   const concurrency = Math.max(1, Number(limitN) || 6);
00445:   const results = new Array(arr.length);
00446:   let idx = 0;
00447: 
00448:   async function worker() {
00449:     while (true) {
00450:       const current = idx;
00451:       idx += 1;
00452:       if (current >= arr.length) break;
00453:       results[current] = await mapper(arr[current], current);
00454:     }
00455:   }
00456: 
00457:   await Promise.all(
00458:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, () => worker())
00459:   );
00460:   return results;
00461: }

00709: /* =========================
00710:    Bounds (min/max reais)
00711: ========================= */
00712: 
00713: function pickMinMaxFromMapped(mapped) {
00714:   let minYmd = null;
00715:   let maxYmd = null;
00716: 
00717:   for (const d of mapped) {
00718:     const y = d?.ymd || normalizeToYMD(d?.date);
00719:     if (!y || !isYMD(y)) continue;
00720:     if (!minYmd || y < minYmd) minYmd = y;
00721:     if (!maxYmd || y > maxYmd) maxYmd = y;
00722:   }
00723: 
00724:   return { minYmd, maxYmd };
00725: }
00726: 
00727: /**
00728:  * ✅ “Prova de vida” do maxYmd sem depender de índice composto nem de docId:
00729:  * tenta dias recentes (==) desc até achar pelo menos 1 draw.
00730:  */
00731: async function probeRecentMaxYmd(uf, lookbackDays = 45) {
00732:   const base = utcTodayYmd();
00733:   const n = Math.max(3, Math.min(120, Number(lookbackDays) || 45));
00734: 
00735:   for (let i = 0; i <= n; i += 1) {
00736:     const day = addDaysUTCLocal(base, -i);
00737:     const { docs, error } = await fetchDrawDocsPreferUf({
00738:       uf,
00739:       extraWheres: [where("ymd", "==", day)],
00740:       policy: "server",
00741:       extraLimit: limit(5),
00742:     });
00743:     if (error) return { ok: false, maxYmd: null, error };
00744:     if (docs && docs.length) return { ok: true, maxYmd: day, error: null };
00745:   }
00746: 
00747:   return { ok: false, maxYmd: null, error: null };
00748: }

00750: /**
00751:  * ✅ API bounds (backend) — fonte única preferencial
00752:  */
00753: async function fetchBoundsFromApi(ufOrObj) {
00754:   const uf = String(extractUfParam(ufOrObj) || "").trim();
00755:   if (!uf) return { ok: false, error: new Error("uf vazio") };
00756: 
00757:   const lot = canonicalScopeKey(uf) || String(uf).trim().toUpperCase();
00758:   const url = apiUrl(`/api/bounds?lottery=${encodeURIComponent(lot)}`);
00759:   try {
00760:     const r = await fetch(url, { method: "GET" });
00761:     const j = await r.json().catch(() => null);
00762: 
00763:     if (!r.ok || !j || !j.ok) {
00764:       return {
00765:         ok: false,
00766:         error: new Error(`bounds api not ok: ${j?.message || r.status}`),
00767:         payload: j || null,
00768:       };
00769:     }
00770: 
00771:     const minYmd = String(j.minYmd || "").trim();
00772:     const maxYmd = String(j.maxYmd || "").trim();
00773:     if (!isYMD(minYmd) || !isYMD(maxYmd)) {
00774:       return {
00775:         ok: false,
00776:         error: new Error("bounds api retornou min/max inválidos"),
00777:         payload: j,
00778:       };
00779:     }
00780: 
00781:     return {
00782:       ok: true,

01070: function collapseFederalDailyCloneDraws(draws) {
01071:   const arr = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
01072:   const byKey = new Map();
01073: 
01074:   for (const d of arr) {
01075:     const ymd = String(d?.ymd || normalizeToYMD(d?.date) || "").trim();
01076:     const sig = buildPrizeSignature(d);
01077: 
01078:     if (!ymd || !sig) {
01079:       const fallbackKey = `id::${String(
01080:         d?.drawId || d?.id || Math.random()
01081:       )}`;
01082:       if (!byKey.has(fallbackKey)) byKey.set(fallbackKey, d);
01083:       continue;
01084:     }
01085: 
01086:     const key = `${ymd}__${sig}`;
01087:     const prev = byKey.get(key);
01088: 
01089:     if (!prev) byKey.set(key, d);
01090:     else byKey.set(key, pickBetterFederalClone(prev, d));
01091:   }
01092: 
01093:   return sortDrawsLocal(Array.from(byKey.values()));
01094: }

01096: function drawsCacheKeyDay({ scopeKey, ymd, positionsArr, hourFilter }) {
01097:   const p = positionsArr && positionsArr.length ? positionsArr.join(",") : "all";
01098:   const h =
01099:     hourFilter?.kind === "bucket"
01100:       ? `bucket:${hourFilter.bucket}`
01101:       : hourFilter?.kind === "exact"
01102:       ? `exact:${hourFilter.hhmm}`
01103:       : "all";
01104:   return `day::${scopeKey}::${ymd}::pos=${p}::h=${h}`;
01105: }

01107: export async function getKingResultsByDate({
01108:   uf,
01109:   date,
01110:   closeHour = null,
01111:   closeHourBucket = null,
01112:   positions = null,
01113: }) {
01114:   if (!uf || !date) throw new Error("Parâmetros obrigatórios: uf e date");
01115: 
01116:   const scopeKey = canonicalScopeKey(uf);
01117: 
01118:   const positionsArr = normalizePositions(positions);
01119:   const ymdDate = normalizeToYMD(date);
01120:   if (!ymdDate) return [];
01121: 
01122:   const hourFilter = resolveHourFilter({ closeHour, closeHourBucket });
01123: 
01124:   const dayKey = drawsCacheKeyDay({
01125:     scopeKey,
01126:     ymd: ymdDate,
01127:     positionsArr,
01128:     hourFilter,
01129:   });
01130: 
01131:   const forceFreshServer = shouldForceFreshServerRead(ymdDate);
01132:   const allowMemoryCache = !forceFreshServer;
01133: 
01134:   if (allowMemoryCache) {
01135:     const cached = cacheGet(DRAWS_CACHE, dayKey);
01136:     if (cached) return cached;
01137:   }
01138: 
01139:   const readPolicy = forceFreshServer ? "server" : DEFAULT_READ_POLICY;
01140: 
01141:   const docCandidates = [];
01142: 
01143:   {
01144:     const { docs: found, error } = await fetchDrawDocsPreferUf({
01145:       uf,
01146:       extraWheres: [where("ymd", "==", ymdDate)],
01147:       policy: readPolicy,
01148:     });
01149:     if (error) throw error;
01150:     if (found.length) docCandidates.push(...found);
01151:   }
01152: 
01153:   if (!docCandidates.length) {
01154:     const rawDate = String(date || "").trim();
01155:     if (rawDate) {
01156:       const { docs: found, error } = await fetchDrawDocsPreferUf({
01157:         uf,
01158:         extraWheres: [where("date", "==", rawDate)],
01159:         policy: readPolicy,
01160:       });
01161:       if (error) throw error;
01162:       if (found.length) docCandidates.push(...found);
01163:     }
01164:   }
01165: 
01166:   if (!docCandidates.length) {
01167:     const { docs: found, error } = await fetchDrawDocsPreferUf({
01168:       uf,
01169:       extraWheres: [where("date", "==", ymdDate)],
01170:       policy: readPolicy,
01171:     });
01172:     if (error) throw error;
01173:     if (found.length) docCandidates.push(...found);
01174:   }
01175: 
01176:   if (!docCandidates.length) {
01177:     const brDate = ymdToBR(ymdDate);
01178:     if (brDate) {
01179:       const { docs: found, error } = await fetchDrawDocsPreferUf({
01180:         uf,
01181:         extraWheres: [where("date", "==", brDate)],
01182:         policy: readPolicy,
01183:       });
01184:       if (error) throw error;
01185:       if (found.length) docCandidates.push(...found);
01186:     }
01187:   }
01188: 
01189:   if (!docCandidates.length) return [];
01190: 
01191:   let baseAll = dedupeDrawsLocal(docCandidates.map(mapDrawDoc));
01192:   baseAll = baseAll.filter((x) => (x.ymd || normalizeToYMD(x.date)) === ymdDate);
01193: 
01194:   const orderedAll = sortDrawsLocal(baseAll);
01195: 
01196:   const hydratedAll = await mapWithConcurrency(orderedAll, 6, async (item) => {
01197:     const prizes = await fetchPrizesForDraw(item.drawId, positionsArr, item.prizes);
01198:     const pc = Array.isArray(prizes) ? prizes.length : 0;
01199:     return { ...item, prizes, prizesCount: pc, __mode: "detailed" };
01200:   });
01201: 
01202:   let dayAll = dedupeDrawsLocal(hydratedAll);
01203: 
01204: if (scopeKey === FEDERAL_SCOPE_CODE) {
01205:   dayAll = collapseFederalDailyCloneDraws(dayAll);
01206: }
01207: 
01208:   const base = hourFilter?.kind
01209:     ? dayAll.filter((d) => drawPassesHourFilter(d, hourFilter))
01210:     : dayAll;
01211: 
01212:   let out = dedupeDrawsLocal(sortDrawsLocal(base));
01213: 
01214: if (scopeKey === FEDERAL_SCOPE_CODE) {
01215:   out = collapseFederalDailyCloneDraws(out);
01216: }
01217: 
01218:   if (allowMemoryCache) {
01219:     cacheSet(DRAWS_CACHE, dayKey, out);
01220:   }
01221: 
01222:   return out;
01223: }

01229: function drawsCacheKeyRange({
01230:   scopeKey,
01231:   from,
01232:   to,
01233:   positionsArr,
01234:   hourFilter,
01235:   mode,
01236: }) {
01237:   const p = positionsArr && positionsArr.length ? positionsArr.join(",") : "all";
01238:   const h =
01239:     hourFilter?.kind === "bucket"
01240:       ? `bucket:${hourFilter.bucket}`
01241:       : hourFilter?.kind === "exact"
01242:       ? `exact:${hourFilter.hhmm}`
01243:       : "all";
01244:   const m = mode ? String(mode) : "detailed";
01245:   return `range::${scopeKey}::${from}..${to}::pos=${p}::h=${h}::mode=${m}`;
01246: }
01247: 
01248: function chooseRangeConcurrency(drawCount) {
01249:   if (!Number.isFinite(drawCount)) return 6;
01250:   if (drawCount <= 50) return 4;
01251:   if (drawCount <= 300) return 6;
01252:   if (drawCount <= 1200) return 8;
01253:   return 10;
01254: }
01255: 
01256: export function decideKingRangeMode({ dateFrom, dateTo, mode = "detailed" }) {
01257:   const ymdFrom = normalizeToYMD(dateFrom);
01258:   const ymdTo = normalizeToYMD(dateTo);
01259:   if (!ymdFrom || !ymdTo) return "detailed";
01260: 
01261:   if (mode === "detailed" || mode === "aggregated") return mode;
01262:   if (mode !== "auto") return "detailed";
01263: 
01264:   const days = daysBetweenInclusiveUTC(ymdFrom, ymdTo);
01265:   if (!Number.isFinite(days) || days <= 0) return "detailed";
01266:   return "detailed";
01267: }

01292:       ...d,
01293:       prizes: embedded,

01295:       __mode: "aggregated",
01296:     };
01297:   });

01300: export async function getKingResultsByRange({
01301:   uf,
01302:   dateFrom,
01303:   dateTo,
01304:   closeHour = null,
01305:   closeHourBucket = null,
01306:   positions = null,
01307:   mode = "detailed",
01308:   readPolicy = DEFAULT_READ_POLICY,
01309:   bypassCache = false,
01310: }) {
01311:   if (!uf || !dateFrom || !dateTo) {
01312:     throw new Error("Parâmetros obrigatórios: uf, dateFrom, dateTo");
01313:   }
01314: 
01315:   const scopeKey = canonicalScopeKey(uf);
01316: 
01317:   const positionsArr = normalizePositions(positions);
01318: 
01319:   const ymdFrom = normalizeToYMD(dateFrom);
01320:   const ymdTo = normalizeToYMD(dateTo);
01321:   if (!ymdFrom || !ymdTo) return [];

01323:   const hourFilter = resolveHourFilter({ closeHour, closeHourBucket });
01324: 
01325:   const effectiveMode = decideKingRangeMode({
01326:     dateFrom: ymdFrom,
01327:     dateTo: ymdTo,
01328:     mode,
01329:   });
01330: 
01331:   const rangeKey = drawsCacheKeyRange({
01332:     scopeKey,
01333:     from: ymdFrom,
01334:     to: ymdTo,
01335:     positionsArr,
01336:     hourFilter,
01337:     mode: effectiveMode,
01338:   });
01339:   if (!bypassCache) {
01340:     const cached = cacheGet(DRAWS_CACHE, rangeKey);
01341:     if (cached) return cached;
01342:   }
01343: 
01344:   const DOC_ID = documentId();
01345:   const rangeOrder = [orderBy("ymd", "asc"), orderBy(DOC_ID)];
01346: 
01347:   const { docs, error, usedField } = await fetchDrawDocsPreferUf({
01348:     uf,
01349:     extraWheres: [where("ymd", ">=", ymdFrom), where("ymd", "<=", ymdTo)],
01350:     extraOrderBy: rangeOrder,
01351:     policy: readPolicy,
01352:   });
01353: 
01354:   if (error) {
01355:     if (isIndexError(error)) {
01356:       const days = daysBetweenInclusiveUTC(ymdFrom, ymdTo);
01357:       const MAX_FALLBACK_DAYS = 31;
01358: 
01359:       if (Number.isFinite(days) && days > 0 && days <= MAX_FALLBACK_DAYS) {
01360:         if (effectiveMode === "aggregated") {
01361:           const all = [];
01362:           for (let i = 0; i < days; i += 1) {
01363:             const day = addDaysUTC(ymdFrom, i);
01364:             const dayDraws = await fetchDrawsDayNoPrizes({ uf, dayYmd: day });
01365:             if (dayDraws.length) all.push(...dayDraws);
01366:           }
01367: 
01368:           const baseAll = dedupeDrawsLocal(all);
01369:           const base = hourFilter?.kind
01370:             ? baseAll.filter((d) => drawPassesHourFilter(d, hourFilter))
01371:             : baseAll;
01372: 
01373:           let out = dedupeDrawsLocal(sortDrawsLocal(base));
01374: 
01375:           if (!bypassCache) {
01376:       cacheSet(DRAWS_CACHE, rangeKey, out);
01377:     }
01378:           return out;
01379:         }
01380: 
01381:         const all = [];
01382:         for (let i = 0; i < days; i += 1) {
01383:           const day = addDaysUTC(ymdFrom, i);
01384:           const dayDraws = await getKingResultsByDate({
01385:             uf,
01386:             date: day,
01387:             closeHour,
01388:             closeHourBucket,
01389:             positions: positionsArr,
01390:           });
01391:           if (Array.isArray(dayDraws) && dayDraws.length) all.push(...dayDraws);
01392:         }
01393: 
01394:         const baseAll = dedupeDrawsLocal(all);
01395:         const base = hourFilter?.kind
01396:           ? baseAll.filter((d) => drawPassesHourFilter(d, hourFilter))
01397:           : baseAll;
01398: 
01399:         let out = dedupeDrawsLocal(sortDrawsLocal(base));
01400: 
01401:         if (!bypassCache) {
01402:       cacheSet(DRAWS_CACHE, rangeKey, out);
01403:     }
01404:         return out;
01405:       }
01406: 
01407:       const rawCode = String(error?.code || "");
01408:       const rawMsg = String(error?.message || "");
01409: 
01410:       throw new Error(
01411:         `Firestore: falta índice composto para RANGE (campo ${usedField} + ymd).\n` +
01412:           `Crie/valide o índice no console (Collection: draws | Fields: ${usedField} ASC, ymd ASC, __name__ (documentId) ASC).\n` +
01413:           `Obs: fallback automático só é aplicado para ranges até ${MAX_FALLBACK_DAYS} dias.\n` +

01415:       );
01416:     }
01417: 
01418:     throw error;
01419:   }
01420: 
01421:   if (!docs || !docs.length) {
01422:     const days = daysBetweenInclusiveUTC(ymdFrom, ymdTo);
01423:     const MAX_EMPTY_RANGE_FALLBACK_DAYS = 120;
01424: 
01425:     if (
01426:       Number.isFinite(days) &&
01427:       days > 0 &&
01428:       days <= MAX_EMPTY_RANGE_FALLBACK_DAYS
01429:     ) {
01430:       if (effectiveMode === "aggregated") {
01431:         const all = [];
01432:         for (let i = 0; i < days; i += 1) {
01433:           const day = addDaysUTC(ymdFrom, i);
01434:           const dayDraws = await fetchDrawsDayNoPrizes({ uf, dayYmd: day });
01435:           if (dayDraws.length) all.push(...dayDraws);
01436:         }

01438:         const baseAll = dedupeDrawsLocal(all);
01439:         const base = hourFilter?.kind
01440:           ? baseAll.filter((d) => drawPassesHourFilter(d, hourFilter))
01441:           : baseAll;
01442: 
01443:         let out = dedupeDrawsLocal(sortDrawsLocal(base));
01444: 
01445:         if (!bypassCache) {
01446:       cacheSet(DRAWS_CACHE, rangeKey, out);
01447:     }
01448:         return out;
01449:       }
01450:

01452:       for (let i = 0; i < days; i += 1) {

01454:         const dayDraws = await getKingResultsByDate({

01468:

01389:             positions: positionsArr,
01390:           });
01391:           if (Array.isArray(dayDraws) && dayDraws.length) all.push(...dayDraws);
01392:         }
01393: 
01394:         const baseAll = dedupeDrawsLocal(all);
01395:         const base = hourFilter?.kind
01396:           ? baseAll.filter((d) => drawPassesHourFilter(d, hourFilter))
01397:           : baseAll;
01398: 
01399:         let out = dedupeDrawsLocal(sortDrawsLocal(base));
01400: 
01401:         if (!bypassCache) {
01402:       cacheSet(DRAWS_CACHE, rangeKey, out);
01403:     }
01404:         return out;
01405:       }
01406: 
01407:       const rawCode = String(error?.code || "");
01408:       const rawMsg = String(error?.message || "");
01409: 
01410:       throw new Error(
01411:         `Firestore: falta índice composto para RANGE (campo ${usedField} + ymd).\n` +
01412:           `Crie/valide o índice no console (Collection: draws | Fields: ${usedField} ASC, ymd ASC, __name__ (documentId) ASC).\n` +
01413:           `Obs: fallback automático só é aplicado para ranges até ${MAX_FALLBACK_DAYS} dias.\n` +
01414:           (rawCode || rawMsg ? `Detalhe: ${rawCode} ${rawMsg}` : "")
01415:       );
01416:     }
01417: 
01418:     throw error;
01419:   }
01420: 
01421:   if (!docs || !docs.length) {
01422:     const days = daysBetweenInclusiveUTC(ymdFrom, ymdTo);
01423:     const MAX_EMPTY_RANGE_FALLBACK_DAYS = 120;
01424: 
01425:     if (
01426:       Number.isFinite(days) &&
01427:       days > 0 &&
01428:       days <= MAX_EMPTY_RANGE_FALLBACK_DAYS
01429:     ) {
01430:       if (effectiveMode === "aggregated") {
01431:         const all = [];
01432:         for (let i = 0; i < days; i += 1) {
01433:           const day = addDaysUTC(ymdFrom, i);
01434:           const dayDraws = await fetchDrawsDayNoPrizes({ uf, dayYmd: day });
01435:           if (dayDraws.length) all.push(...dayDraws);
01436:         }
01437: 
01438:         const baseAll = dedupeDrawsLocal(all);
01439:         const base = hourFilter?.kind
01440:           ? baseAll.filter((d) => drawPassesHourFilter(d, hourFilter))
01441:           : baseAll;
01442: 
01443:         let out = dedupeDrawsLocal(sortDrawsLocal(base));
01444: 
01445:         if (!bypassCache) {
01446:       cacheSet(DRAWS_CACHE, rangeKey, out);
01447:     }
01448:         return out;
01449:       }
01450: 
01451:       const all = [];
01452:       for (let i = 0; i < days; i += 1) {
01453:         const day = addDaysUTC(ymdFrom, i);
01454:         const dayDraws = await getKingResultsByDate({
01455:           uf,
01456:           date: day,
01457:           closeHour,
01458:           closeHourBucket,
01459:           positions: positionsArr,
01460:         });
01461:         if (Array.isArray(dayDraws) && dayDraws.length) all.push(...dayDraws);
01462:       }
01463: 
01464:       const baseAll = dedupeDrawsLocal(all);
01465:       const base = hourFilter?.kind
01466:         ? baseAll.filter((d) => drawPassesHourFilter(d, hourFilter))
01467:         : baseAll;
01468: 
01469:       const ordered = sortDrawsLocal(base);
01470: 
01471:       const conc = chooseRangeConcurrency(ordered.length);
01472:       const results = await mapWithConcurrency(ordered, conc, async (item) => {
01473:         const prizes = await fetchPrizesForDraw(item.drawId, positionsArr, item.prizes);
01474:         const pc = Array.isArray(prizes) ? prizes.length : 0;
01475:         return { ...item, prizes, prizesCount: pc, __mode: "detailed" };
01476:       });
01477: 
01478:       const out = dedupeDrawsLocal(results);
01479:       if (!bypassCache) {
01480:       cacheSet(DRAWS_CACHE, rangeKey, out);
01481:     }
01482:       return out;
01483:     }
01484: 
01485:     return [];
01486:   }
01487: 
01488:   const baseAll = dedupeDrawsLocal(docs.map(mapDrawDoc));
01489:   const base = hourFilter?.kind
01490:     ? baseAll.filter((d) => drawPassesHourFilter(d, hourFilter))
01491:     : baseAll;
01492: 
01493:   const ordered = sortDrawsLocal(base);
01494: 
01495:   if (effectiveMode === "aggregated") {
01496:     const out = dedupeDrawsLocal(
01497:       ordered.map((d) => {
01498:         const embedded = normalizeEmbeddedPrizesForAggregated(d?.prizes);
01499:         return {
01500:           ...d,
01501:           prizes: embedded,
01502:           prizesCount: Number.isFinite(Number(d?.prizesCount))
01503:             ? Number(d.prizesCount)
01504:             : embedded.length || 0,
01505:           __mode: "aggregated",
01506:         };
01507:       })
01508:     );
01509:     if (!bypassCache) {
01510:       cacheSet(DRAWS_CACHE, rangeKey, out);
01511:     }
01512:     return out;
01513:   }
01514: 
01515:   const conc = chooseRangeConcurrency(ordered.length);
01516: 
01517:   const results = await mapWithConcurrency(ordered, conc, async (item) => {
01518:     const prizes = await fetchPrizesForDraw(item.drawId, positionsArr, item.prizes);
01519:     const pc = Array.isArray(prizes) ? prizes.length : 0;
01520:     return { ...item, prizes, prizesCount: pc, __mode: "detailed" };
01521:   });
01522: 
01523:   const out = dedupeDrawsLocal(results);
01524:   if (!bypassCache) {
01525:       cacheSet(DRAWS_CACHE, rangeKey, out);
01526:     }
01527:   return out;
01528: }
01529: 
01530: /* =========================
01531:    ✅ Hidratação de prizes (para UX no "Todos")
01532: ========================= */
01533: 
01534: export async function hydrateKingDrawsWithPrizes({
01535:   draws,
01536:   positions = null,
01537:   concurrency = null,
01538: }) {
01539:   const arr = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
01540:   if (!arr.length) return [];
01541: 
01542:   const positionsArr = normalizePositions(positions);
01543: 
01544:   const conc =
01545:     Number.isFinite(Number(concurrency)) && Number(concurrency) > 0
01546:       ? Number(concurrency)
01547:       : chooseRangeConcurrency(arr.length);
01548: 
01549:   const results = await mapWithConcurrency(arr, conc, async (item) => {
01550:     const prizes = await fetchPrizesForDraw(item.drawId, positionsArr, item.prizes);
01551:     const pc = Array.isArray(prizes) ? prizes.length : 0;
01552:     return { ...item, prizes, prizesCount: pc, __mode: "detailed" };
01553:   });
01554: 
01555:   return dedupeDrawsLocal(results);
01556: }
01557: 
01558: /* =========================
01559:    ✅ ATRASADOS (Late) — 100% baseado na SUA base
01560: ========================= */
01561: 
01562: function normalizeLotteriesList(lotteries) {
01563:   const arr = Array.isArray(lotteries) ? lotteries : [];
01564:   const cleaned = arr
01565:     .map((x) => String(x ?? "").trim().toUpperCase())
01566:     .filter(Boolean);
01567:   return cleaned.length ? Array.from(new Set(cleaned)) : null;
01568: }
01569: 
01570: function drawPassesLotteriesFilter(draw, lotteriesArr) {
01571:   if (!lotteriesArr || !lotteriesArr.length) return true;
01572:   const code = String(draw?.lottery_code ?? "").trim().toUpperCase();
01573:   if (!code) return false;
01574:   return lotteriesArr.includes(code);
01575: }
01576: 
01577: function lateCacheKey({
01578:   scopeKey,
01579:   fromYmd,
01580:   toYmd,
01581:   baseYmd,
01582:   prizePosition,
01583:   hourFilter,
01584:   lotteriesArr,
01585:   chunkDays,
01586: }) {
01587:   const h =
01588:     hourFilter?.kind === "bucket"
01589:       ? `bucket:${hourFilter.bucket}`
01590:       : hourFilter?.kind === "exact"
01591:       ? `exact:${hourFilter.hhmm}`
01592:       : "all";
01593:   const lots = lotteriesArr && lotteriesArr.length ? lotteriesArr.join(",") : "all";
01594:   const cd = Number(chunkDays) || 15;
01595:   const pos = Number(prizePosition) || 1;
01596:   return `late::${scopeKey}::${fromYmd}..${toYmd}::base=${baseYmd}::pos=${pos}::h=${h}::lots=${lots}::chunk=${cd}`;
01597: }
01598: 
01599: function hourToNumSafe(hhmm) {
01600:   const s = normalizeHourLike(hhmm);
01601:   const m = s.match(/^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
01602:   if (!m) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
01603:   return Number(m[1]) * 100 + Number(m[2]);
01604: }
01605: 
01606: export async function getKingLateByRange({
01607:   uf,
01608:   dateFrom,
01609:   dateTo,
01610:   baseDate = null,
01611:   lotteries = null,
01612:   prizePosition = 1,
01613:   closeHour = null,
01614:   closeHourBucket = null,
01615:   chunkDays = 15,
01616: }) {
01617:   if (!uf || !dateFrom || !dateTo) {
01618:     throw new Error("Parâmetros obrigatórios: uf, dateFrom, dateTo");
01619:   }
01620: 
01621:   const scopeKey = canonicalScopeKey(uf);
01622: 
01623:   const fromYmd = normalizeToYMD(dateFrom);
01624:   const toYmd = normalizeToYMD(dateTo);
01625:   if (!fromYmd || !toYmd) return [];
01626: 
01627:   const baseYmd = normalizeToYMD(baseDate) || toYmd;
01628: 
01629:   const hourFilter = resolveHourFilter({ closeHour, closeHourBucket });
01630:   const lotteriesArr = normalizeLotteriesList(lotteries);
01631: 
01632:   const cacheKey = lateCacheKey({
01633:     scopeKey,
01634:     fromYmd,
01635:     toYmd,
01636:     baseYmd,
01637:     prizePosition,
01638:     hourFilter,
01639:     lotteriesArr,
01640:     chunkDays,
01641:   });
01642: 
01643:   const cached = cacheGet(LATE_CACHE, cacheKey);
01644:   if (cached) return cached;
01645: 
01646:   const posNum = Number(prizePosition) || 1;
01647: 
01648:   const lastSeen = new Map();
01649: 
01650:   let cursorTo = toYmd;
01651:   const chunk = Math.max(3, Math.min(60, Number(chunkDays) || 15));
01652: 
01653:   if (cursorTo < fromYmd) {
01654:     const outEmpty = Array.from({ length: 25 }, (_, i) => ({
01655:       pos: i + 1,
01656:       grupo: i + 1,
01657:       lastYmd: null,
01658:       lastHour: null,
01659:       lastDrawId: null,
01660:       lastLottery: null,
01661:       atrasoDias: null,
01662:     }));
01663:     cacheSet(LATE_CACHE, cacheKey, outEmpty);
01664:     return outEmpty;
01665:   }
01666: 
01667:   while (cursorTo >= fromYmd && lastSeen.size < 25) {
01668:     let chunkFrom = addDaysUTC(cursorTo, -(chunk - 1));
01669:     if (chunkFrom < fromYmd) chunkFrom = fromYmd;
01670: 
01671:     const draws = await getKingResultsByRange({
01672:       uf,
01673:       dateFrom: chunkFrom,
01674:       dateTo: cursorTo,
01675:       closeHour,
01676:       closeHourBucket,
01677:       positions: [posNum],
01678:       mode: "detailed",
01679:     });
01680: 
01681:     const desc = sortDrawsLocalDesc(dedupeDrawsLocal(draws));
01682: 
01683:     for (const d of desc) {
01684:       if (!d) continue;
01685: 
01686:       if (hourFilter?.kind && !drawPassesHourFilter(d, hourFilter)) continue;
01687:       if (lotteriesArr && !drawPassesLotteriesFilter(d, lotteriesArr)) continue;
01688: 
01689:       const ymd = d.ymd || normalizeToYMD(d.date);
01690:       if (!ymd || !isYMD(ymd)) continue;
01691: 
01692:       const p = Array.isArray(d.prizes)
01693:         ? d.prizes.find(
01694:             (x) => Number(x?.position) === posNum && isValidGrupo(x?.grupo)
01695:           )
01696:         : null;
01697: 
01698:       const g = p?.grupo;
01699:       if (!isValidGrupo(g)) continue;
01700: 
01701:       if (!lastSeen.has(g)) {
01702:         lastSeen.set(g, {
01703:           ymd,
01704:           hour: d.close_hour || d.closeHour || "",
01705:           drawId: d.drawId || d.id || null,
01706:           lottery_code: d.lottery_code || null,
01707:         });
01708: 
01709:         if (lastSeen.size >= 25) break;
01710:       }
01711:     }
01712: 
01713:     cursorTo = addDaysUTC(chunkFrom, -1);
01714:   }
01715: 
01716:   const rows = [];
01717:   for (let g = 1; g <= 25; g += 1) {
01718:     const seen = lastSeen.get(g) || null;
01719:     const lastYmd = seen?.ymd || null;
01720:     const diff = lastYmd ? daysDiffUTC(lastYmd, baseYmd) : NaN;
01721:     const atrasoDias =
01722:       lastYmd && Number.isFinite(diff) ? Math.max(0, diff) : null;
01723: 
01724:     rows.push({
01725:       pos: 0,
01726:       grupo: g,
01727:       lastYmd,
01728:       lastHour: seen?.hour ? normalizeHourLike(seen.hour) : null,
01729:       lastDrawId: seen?.drawId || null,
01730:       lastLottery: seen?.lottery_code || null,
01731:       atrasoDias,
01732:     });
01733:   }
01734: 
01735:   const sorted = [...rows].sort((a, b) => {
01736:     const aa = Number.isFinite(Number(a?.atrasoDias)) ? Number(a.atrasoDias) : -1;
01737:     const bb = Number.isFinite(Number(b?.atrasoDias)) ? Number(b.atrasoDias) : -1;
01738:     if (bb !== aa) return bb - aa;
01739: 
01740:     const ha = hourToNumSafe(a?.lastHour);
01741:     const hb = hourToNumSafe(b?.lastHour);
01742:     if (ha !== hb) return ha - hb;
01743: 
01744:     return Number(a?.grupo || 0) - Number(b?.grupo || 0);
01745:   });
01746: 
01747:   const out = sorted.map((r, idx) => ({ ...r, pos: idx + 1 }));
01748:
